Change Log
Date          A*, M, D   In charge         Change Description

17/10/2026    A          Ducnv             Use case specifications for FE-01 Authentication and
                                           Account Access, FE-02 Farm Workspace and FE-03
                                           Membership added.

21/10/2026    M          G35 Team          FE-03 reduced to 9 use cases: Manager permissions are
                                           fixed by role (UC "Configure Manager Permissions"
                                           removed). FE-09 restructured into schedule maintenance,
                                           recording, deferral, history and correction use cases.
                                           UC-FE02-03 restricted to the Farm Owner.

21/10/2026    M          G35 Team          Consolidated revision of the whole SRS against the Report
                                           3 template: Trigger and End condition for all business
                                           flows; feeding steps removed from BF-02, BF-04 and BF-06
                                           (FE-23 is out of scope); Farm Technician made read-only in
                                           all farm operations; care and reproduction events
                                           recorded by the Farm Manager; sign-in with Google or
                                           verified email and password everywhere; subscription
                                           terms 1/2/3 years.

21/10/2026    A          G35 Team          Business ERD added as subject areas and relationship table
                                           (I.3.1); Data Business Rules given an Enforced On column
                                           (I.3.3); Permission Matrix rewritten as Entity × Action with
                                           Full / Restricted / No (I.4.4); Screen List, External API
                                           Inventory and Background Job Inventory completed (I.5).

21/10/2026    M          G35 Team          Use case specifications limited to main-flow and complex
                                           use cases in one table format (II); Functional Requirements
                                           rewritten per screen with field descriptions (III); business
                                           rules unified as GBR-01 to GBR-89 and messages as
                                           MSG-COM / MSG-FExx (V). FE-09 use cases renumbered
                                           UC-FE09-01 to UC-FE09-08; reproduction correction use
                                           cases taken into scope.




                                                                                                      8
21/10/2026   M   G35 Team   Cattle status model refined: life stage made physiological
                            (Calf with dam, Weaned calf, Young stock, Adult) and
                            separated from production purpose; production purposes
                            given a fixed purpose type (Breeding, Fattening, Sire,
                            Undecided) and derived herd groups added; health state
                            set to Healthy / Monitoring / Under treatment with a
                            CHRONIC case status and a meat-withdrawal flag that also
                            covers vaccinations; reproductive status limited to
                            applicable females (NOT_ELIGIBLE removed) with a
                            separate breeding-eligibility indicator; REM-08 extended
                            to life-stage suggestions.

22/10/2026   M   G35 Team   Herd entry and herd exit changed to vouchers of one or
                            more cattle (herd entry voucher PN, herd exit voucher PX)
                            with line values, payments to sellers and collections from
                            buyers: FE-06 now UC-FE06-01 to UC-FE06-05, FE-13 now
                            UC-FE13-01 to UC-FE13-04; screens SCR-33 to SCR-37 and
                            SCR-69 to SCR-72 redefined and SCR-105 to SCR-110
                            added.

22/10/2026   A   G35 Team   FE-23 Feed Management taken into scope: feed stock, feed
                            receipts with supplier payments, stock adjustment,
                            versioned rations, feeding log with automatic stock
                            deduction, feed catalogue and suppliers, consumption and
                            cost report (UC-FE23-01 to UC-FE23-11, SCR-111 to
                            SCR-122, BF-09, III.23). New entities (Trading Partner,
                            vouchers, Voucher Payment, subject area L Feed
                            Management), relationships R90 to R114, BR-D34 to
                            BR-D39, GBR-90 to GBR-105, reminders REM-10 and
                            REM-11. Background jobs and external APIs renumbered
                            III.24 and III.25.

22/10/2026   M   G35 Team   Data model reworked to conceptual level (I.3): the ERD
                            shows 42 business entities in nine subject areas by name
                            only, with 70 relationships (R01–R70) in Crow's Foot
                            notation; line items, histories and versions are described
                            as "Detail" items of their owning entity, and technical or
                            derived records (audit log, record revisions, notifications,
                            stock movements, receipts, report snapshots, public
                            content) moved to 3.2 J Supporting Records. Overview and
                            per-area diagrams added.




                                                                                         9

                            instead of one generic User Account – Farm Owner, Farm

                            Manager and System Admin – with the role-specific
                            relationships (owns, works in, sends/accepts invitation,

Definition and Acronyms
Acronym / Term         Definition

BeefCare (BCMS)        On-farm Beef Cattle Care Management System, the product specified in this
                       document (project SEP490-G35).

SRS                    Software Requirement Specification.

BF                     Business Flow: an end-to-end business process (BF-01 to BF-09).

FE                     Feature: a group of related system capabilities (FE-01 to FE-23).

UC                     Use Case: a goal an actor achieves through BeefCare (UC-FExx-nn).

SCR                    Screen identifier used in the Screen List (I.5.1) and Functional Requirements (III).

GBR / BR-D             General Business Rule (V.1) / Data Business Rule (I.3.3).

MSG                    System message code (V.2): MSG-COM-nn for common messages, MSG-FExx-nn for
                       feature messages.

REM                    Reminder rule REM-01 to REM-13 (III.14).

JOB / API              Background job (III.24) / External API (III.25).

NFR / OR               Non-Functional Requirement (IV) / Other Requirement (V.3).

Tenant                 An isolated farm workspace. One tenant is one farm.

Farm Owner, Farm       The three farm roles. Owner: owns the farm, manages members, subscription and
Manager, Farm          herd exits. Manager: records and corrects care and reproduction events and
Technician             maintains farm data. Technician: read-only field user.

CS, PM, SA             Customer Support, Platform Manager and System Admin: platform staff roles
                       independent of any farm.

Membership             The link between an account and one farm, carrying the role and status in that
                       farm.

Invitation             An Owner's invitation for an email address to join one farm with a role; valid for 3,
                       7 or 14 days (default 7).

Farm service status    PENDING_PAYMENT, ACTIVE, EXPIRED or SUSPENDED (GBR-13).



                                                                                                              11
Internal Cattle ID         The permanent, never-reused system identifier of a cattle.

Display code               The human-readable cattle code (BC-nnnnnn) printed on the ear tag above the QR.

QR tag                     The printed cattle ear tag whose QR code encodes the BeefCare address of the tag.
                           It opens the cattle profile only for a signed-in member of the farm (GBR-28);
                           BeefCare has no public cattle page. Different from a payment VietQR.

Herd status                ACTIVE (Đang nuôi), EXITED (Đã xuất, with exit type and date) or VOIDED (Nhập
                           nhầm – đã hủy: herd entry voided as a data-entry mistake); separate from
                           production purpose, life stage, health state and reproductive status (GBR-24, 3.4).

Exit pending               A derived indicator of an ACTIVE cattle on a DRAFT or PENDING herd exit voucher
                           line: Chờ xuất, or Chờ xác nhận chết / mất for a PENDING death voucher, which
                           also blocks new events (GBR-26, GBR-95).

Production purpose         Why the farm raises the cattle, chosen from the farm's purpose catalogue (for
                           example "Bò sinh sản", "Vỗ béo xuất chuồng"); changed by the Owner only.

Purpose type               The fixed business meaning of a purpose catalogue item: BREEDING, FATTENING,
                           SIRE or UNDECIDED. Rules and herd groups use the type, never the free-text
                           purpose name (GBR-25).

Life stage                 The cattle's physiological stage, from a fixed list: UNWEANED_CALF (Bê chưa cai
                           sữa), WEANED_CALF (Bê cai sữa), YOUNG_STOCK (Bò tơ / hậu bị), ADULT (Trưởng
                           thành). Use (breeding, fattening) is the purpose type, and pregnancy is a
                           reproductive status, not a life stage.

Herd group                 A derived grouping used in herd lists and reports: Calves (Bê), Fattening (Vỗ béo),
                           Breeding bulls (Đực giống), Breeding females (Bò cái sinh sản), Young stock (Bò
                           tơ), Other (Khác). Each ACTIVE cattle is in exactly one group (GBR-24).

Health state               Derived from health cases for an ACTIVE cattle: HEALTHY (Khỏe mạnh),
                           MONITORING (Theo dõi) or UNDER_TREATMENT (Đang điều trị) (GBR-45).

Reproductive status        Derived from reproduction events for an applicable female: AWAITING_BREEDING
                           (Chờ phối), IN_ESTRUS (Động dục), BRED (Đã phối – chờ khám thai), PREGNANT
                           (Mang thai; sub-label Sắp đẻ near the expected date) or POST_CALVING (Sau sinh);
                           other cattle show "Not applicable" (GBR-54, 3.4.3).

Record status              Status of a recorded event (weight, vaccination, estrus, breeding, check, calving,
                           feeding): VALID or VOIDED; a corrected record stays VALID with a "Corrected"
                           marker and a Record Revision; a weight may also be EXCLUDED as an outlier
                           (BR-D15).

Breeding eligibility       An advisory indicator, Met or Not met, from the farm's breeding conditions
                           (minimum age, minimum weight, no OPEN health case); it never blocks a recorded
                           event (GBR-55).

Pen / Reception criteria   A pen or care area with a capacity / advisory purposes, stages, sex and minimum
                           age or weight that the pen accepts.

Herd entry / Herd exit     The record of one cattle entering the herd (EXTERNAL or FARM_BORN) / leaving it
                           (SALE, CULL, DEATH with loss kind Died or Missing, OTHER); each is one line of a
                           voucher.




                                                                                                                12
Herd entry voucher       One receipt of one or more cattle into the farm, by purchase or as farm-born
(phiếu nhập)             calves (code PN-YYYY-nnnn), with one line per cattle, the total value and the
                         payments to the seller. DRAFT → POSTED → VOIDED; posting creates the cattle
                         (GBR-90).

Herd exit voucher        One departure of one or more cattle (sale, cull, death or missing, other; code
(phiếu xuất)             PX-YYYY-nnnn), with one line per cattle, the total receivable and the collections
                         from the buyer. DRAFT or PENDING → CONFIRMED → VOIDED; confirmed by the
                         Owner only (GBR-94).

Trading partner          A seller, buyer or feed supplier in the farm's contact list. Partners have no BeefCare
                         account (GBR-97).

Voucher payment          One payment to a seller or supplier, or one collection from a buyer, recorded
                         against a voucher. Farm bookkeeping only; not an invoice, tax or accounting
                         document (GBR-91).

Pricing basis            Per kg (unit price × weight) or per head (unit price per cattle) for the lines of a herd
                         entry or exit voucher (GBR-92).

UT                       "Ước tính": marks an estimated weight (basis ESTIMATED) in vouchers and lists.

Draft calf               A live calf saved at calving with status PENDING_CONFIRMATION, waiting for
                         registration by the Owner or a Manager.

BCS                      Body Condition Score; default farm scale 1.0–5.0.

Feed item                A feed or supplement in the farm's feed catalogue (code TA-nn), stocked in kg
                         (GBR-98).

Feed receipt / Stock     A delivery of feed from a supplier that adds stock (PNK-YYYY-nnnn) / a stock count
adjustment               that records the difference between counted and system stock (ĐCK-YYYY-nnnn).

Ration (khẩu phần)       The planned kg of each feed per head and session for the pens it applies to;
                         changed only by saving a new version with an effective date (GBR-101).

Feeding session (cữ cho One feeding of one pen on one date (Morning, Afternoon or Evening). Saving it
ăn)                     deducts the actual feed from stock (GBR-102).

Weighted average cost    Stock value ÷ stock quantity of a feed item, recalculated at each posted receipt;
                         used to value feeding, adjustments and rations (GBR-98, GBR-105).

ADG                      Average Daily Gain in kg/day between two valid weights (GBR-39).

MEASURED /               Basis of a weight: scale measurement / tape or visual estimate.
ESTIMATED

Withdrawal period        Days after a treatment or an on-farm vaccination during which meat must not be
                         sold; the profile shows "Meat withdrawal until dd/mm/yyyy" while one is active
                         (GBR-46).

Estrus / Breeding / AI   Heat observed in a female / natural service or insemination / artificial insemination
                         (Vietnamese: TTNT).

Pregnancy episode        One confirmed pregnancy from the confirming check until calving, loss or exit.




                                                                                                              13
 Expected calving date    TENTATIVE (from breeding) or CONFIRMED (after a PREGNANT check) date =
                          breeding time + gestation length.

 LMLM / FMD               Lở mồm long móng / Foot-and-Mouth Disease.

 Plan price version       An immutable published price list for the 1-, 2- and 3-year terms.

 Payment request          An immutable 24-hour transfer instruction for one farm and one term, with a
 (quote)                  unique payment reference and VietQR.

 Purchased period         The service period granted by one accepted payment.

 Policy A                 The subscription confirmation and period rules (GBR-69 to GBR-75).

 Policy B                 The support ticket closure rule requiring explicit Owner acknowledgment
                          (GBR-80).

 VietQR                   A Vietnamese bank-transfer QR code containing receiving account, amount and
                          transfer content.

 SePay                    External service that reports bank transactions to BeefCare through webhooks.

 Webhook                  An HTTP call from SePay to the BeefCare backend when a transaction occurs.

 HMAC-SHA256              Keyed signature used to authenticate SePay webhook calls.

 Bank transfer evidence   An authenticated bank transaction kept by BeefCare whether or not it matches a
                          payment request.

 Internal request         A named request from the assigned CS to a PM or SA about a ticket.

 Resolution               The Owner's explicit confirmation that a resolution version solved the issue.
 acknowledgment

 Subscription receipt     A read-only view of one accepted subscription payment.

 Manual finance record    Platform income or expense recorded by the PM (DRAFT, POSTED, VOID).

 Net cash flow            Receipts + posted other income − posted expenses for a period; not statutory
                          profit.

 Snapshot                 An immutable result of one report run, used for display and export.

 Request key              A client-generated key that makes a command safe to repeat (GBR-88).

 Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh         The default time zone (UTC+7) for farm dates and platform finance.

 OAuth 2.0 / OIDC         Open standards used for Google sign-in.




I. Overall Requirements

1. Context Diagram
BeefCare is a responsive web SaaS application that helps small and medium beef cattle farms (about
150–300 head, mainly Zebu đỏ and Lai Sind) keep one digital record for every animal, from herd
entry to herd exit. Field staff identify an animal by scanning its QR ear tag with a phone browser; the


                                                                                                           14
Farm Manager records weights, vaccinations, health cases and reproduction events against that
animal and the daily feeding of each pen; the Farm Owner follows the herd through the dashboard,
reminders and reports and makes ownership decisions (members, subscription, herd exits).
Each farm is an independent tenant. One Owner may own several farms; each farm has its own
members, data, subscription and service status. A farm is registered in status PENDING_PAYMENT
and becomes ACTIVE when its first subscription payment (1, 2 or 3 years, paid by bank transfer
through VietQR and confirmed by SePay) is accepted. Platform staff operate the service: Customer
Support handles Owner tickets, the Platform Manager manages farm service, subscriptions, payment
reconciliation, plan prices and basic platform cash records, and the System Admin manages accounts,
platform staff access and the audit history. Platform staff never access cattle or care records.
  [Figure to be inserted] Context diagram – BeefCare in the centre; human actors Guest, Farm Owner, Farm
  Manager, Farm Technician, Customer Support, Platform Manager, System Admin; external systems Google
                   Identity Provider, SePay, Email Delivery Service; label flows as in Table 1-1




Table 1-1 External entities and information exchanged

External entity       Direction      Main information exchanged

Guest                 In / Out       Views the public introduction, features, 1/2/3-year prices, FAQ and
                                     contact; submits registration, sign-in and password-reset requests.

Farm Owner            In / Out       Owner profile, farm registration and information, invitations and
                                     member changes, herd entry vouchers and payments, pen and profile
                                     changes, herd exit vouchers and collections, feed stock and rations,
                                     catalogues and parameters, announcements, subscription payment
                                     requests, support tickets; receives dashboard, reports, reminders,
                                     notifications, payment instructions and ticket replies.

Farm Manager          In / Out       Pens, herd entry vouchers, cattle profiles and QR tags, feed receipts,
                                     stock counts, rations and feeding sessions, growth, vaccination, health
                                     and reproduction records and corrections, catalogues and parameters,
                                     announcements to Technicians; receives reminders, dashboard and
                                     reports.

Farm Technician       In / Out       QR scans and searches; receives read-only cattle, care, reproduction
                                     and today's ration information, reminders and announcements.

Customer Support      In / Out       Ticket assignment, replies, internal notes, resolutions, closure, internal
                                     requests; receives the ticket queue and support notifications.

Platform Manager      In / Out       Farm suspension/restoration, payment reconciliation decisions, plan
                                     price versions, manual finance records, internal request results;
                                     receives farm service overview, subscriptions, bank evidence, receipts
                                     and cash reports.

System Admin          In / Out       Account suspension/restoration, platform staff access, internal
                                     request results; receives account lists and the audit history.




                                                                                                            15
 Google Identity         In / Out        BeefCare sends OAuth 2.0 / OpenID Connect authorization requests;
 Provider                                Google returns the authenticated identity (subject, verified email,
                                         name).

 SePay                   In / Out        SePay sends authenticated bank-transaction webhooks; BeefCare
                                         looks up transactions for reconciliation and renders VietQR images for
                                         payment requests.

 Email Delivery          Out             BeefCare sends invitation, email-verification, password-reset,
 Service                                 password-changed and notification emails.




2. Main Business Flow
The nine business flows below cover the main processes of BeefCare. Each flow lists its trigger, end
condition and the ordered steps that the swimlane diagram shows (lanes = Role column). Decision
steps state their branches in the Note column.

2.1 Farm Onboarding, Subscription and Membership (BF-01)
Trigger: A Guest registers as a Farm Owner, or an existing Owner registers an additional farm or
renews the subscription of an owned farm.
End condition: The farm is ACTIVE with an accepted purchased period, and each invited staff
member has an ACTIVE membership (or the invitation is cancelled or expired).

2.3 Cattle Lifecycle: Herd Entry, Placement and Herd Exit (BF-03)


                                                                                                        18
Trigger: Cattle are bought or brought into the farm, live calves are waiting for registration (REM-09),
or the Owner sells, culls or loses animals.
End condition: Each received animal has one permanent identity, profile, pen placement and history
from a posted herd entry voucher; each departed animal is EXITED with a read-only record on a
confirmed herd exit voucher; the amounts paid to sellers and collected from buyers are recorded (or
the voucher is voided).

abnormal sign is observed in the pen.
End condition: The care performed is recorded against each animal, reminders are updated, and any
death or loss is pending the Owner's herd-exit decision.

2.9 Feed Supply and Daily Feeding (BF-09)
Trigger: Feed is delivered by a supplier, a feeding session is due (REM-11), a ration has to change, the
stock of a feed reaches its minimum level (REM-10) or a stock count is planned.

feeding sessions are recorded against its effective ration; consumption and cost are available for the
period.

3. Conceptual Data Model

3.1 Entity Relationship Diagram



                                                                                                     30
The conceptual model shows the business entities of BeefCare and the relationships between them.
It is drawn at conceptual level: each entity is shown by its name only, in Crow's Foot notation, with a
business verb read from Entity A to Entity B and the minimum and maximum cardinality at each end.
Attributes, identifiers, foreign keys, line items, histories and versions are not drawn; they are
described with their owning entity in 3.2 ("Detail" items), and data integrity rules are listed in 3.3.
Every farm-owned entity belongs to exactly one Farm (tenant); only the main ownership
relationships of the Farm are drawn. Users are shown by role rather than as one generic user. A
person has one User Account; Farm Owner, Farm Manager and Farm Technician (the roles a person
holds in farms) and Customer Support, Platform Manager and System Admin (platform staff roles)
are specialisations of it, each drawn as an "is a" relationship to User Account with cardinality 0..1 : 1
(R01): a role belongs to exactly one account and an account holds each role at most once. The roles
overlap – one account may be the Owner of one farm and a Manager or Technician of another (one
role in each farm), and holds at most one platform staff role – and a new account may hold no role
yet. An invitation is accepted by the User Account of the invited person, because the Manager or
Technician role exists only after acceptance (R07). Relationships that can be derived from other
relationships are not drawn (for example, the Owner who sends an invitation is the Owner of the
farm that issues it); who may perform each action is given in the Permission Matrix (4.4). Platform
entities (accounts, plan prices, payments, tickets and finance records) belong to the platform.
Technical and derived records (reminders, audit log, record revisions, notifications, stock movements,
receipts and report snapshots) are not part of the conceptual diagram and are described in 3.2 J.
     [Figure to be inserted] Conceptual ERD – overview of all business entities and relationships (Crow's Foot,
                                               entity names only)
