                                                                                a named PM or SA.

 R81    Platform Manager     handles          Internal Request    0..1 : 0..*   Named PM recipient.

 R82    System Admin         handles          Internal Request    0..1 : 0..*   Named SA recipient; each
                                                                                request has exactly one
                                                                                recipient.



3.2 Entity Descriptions
Each conceptual entity of 3.1 is described by its purpose, key business attributes, business identity
and lifecycle. "Detail" items are parts, line items, histories or versions that belong to the entity; they
are not separate entities in the conceptual diagram.



                                                                                                           38
A. Users and Farm Membership
The six role entities below are overlapping specialisations of the User Account ("is a", 0..1 : 1, R01):
the farm roles come from the Farm Membership of the person in each farm (Detail of Farm) and the
staff roles from the platform role of the account. A specialisation exists while the account holds the
role; a change between Manager and Technician in a farm is a change of the membership role.
Entity: User Account
 Field                 Value

 Purpose               The one global account of a person, reused for every farm the person owns or joins
                       and for any platform staff role. The six role entities of this area are overlapping
                       specialisations of it ("is a"); the account accepts farm invitations, and the System
                       Admin suspends or restores accounts.

 Key business          Full name, verified email, optional contact phone, account status, Owner registration
 attributes            completed (yes/no, time), last sign-in time, created time.

 Business identity     Verified email is unique across all accounts (case-insensitive). The contact phone is
                       never an identifier.

 Status / lifecycle    ACTIVE ⇄ SUSPENDED (System Admin, with reason). Created ACTIVE at the first
                       Google sign-in or when an email/password registration is verified. Never deleted
                       while it has memberships, farms or history.

 Detail – Sign-in      A way the account signs in: a Google identity or a BeefCare password. Attributes:
 Method                Account, method type (GOOGLE / PASSWORD), Google subject (GOOGLE), password
                       hash, failed-attempt count and locked-until time (PASSWORD), linked time, last used
                       time. Identity: Google subject is unique and belongs to exactly one account; an
                       account has at most one method of each type. Lifecycle: GOOGLE is linked at the first
                       Google sign-in and never re-linked. PASSWORD is PENDING_VERIFICATION after email
                       registration, ACTIVE after the email is verified, and is replaced on change or reset.
                       Locked for 15 minutes after 5 failures (GBR-02).



Entity: Farm Owner
 Field                 Value

 Purpose               The person who registers, owns and pays for a farm. Only the Owner manages
                       members and roles, farm information, the subscription and payments, herd exits and
                       production-purpose changes, and only the Owner submits support tickets and
                       confirms their resolution.

 Key business          User account (full name, verified email, contact phone), owned farms, Owner
 attributes            registration completed (yes/no, time).

 Business identity     The account that holds the OWNER membership of a farm. One person may own
                       several farms and may also be a Manager or Technician of other farms.

 Status / lifecycle    Follows the user account (ACTIVE ⇄ SUSPENDED by the System Admin). The OWNER
                       membership is created with the farm and is never transferred.



Entity: Farm Manager


                                                                                                               39
Field                 Value

Purpose               A farm member who runs daily operations: records and corrects care, health,
                      reproduction and feeding events, manages pens, catalogues, parameters, herd entry
                      vouchers, cattle profiles and QR tags, and writes announcements to Farm
                      Technicians.

Key business          User account, farm, MANAGER membership (status, source invitation, joined time,
attributes            last change with effective date and reason).

Business identity     Account + farm; a person has exactly one role in each farm.

Status / lifecycle    Membership ACTIVE ⇄ INACTIVE and MANAGER ⇄ TECHNICIAN, changed only by the
                      Owner with an effective date and reason. Permissions are fixed by role.



Entity: Farm Technician
Field                 Value

Purpose               A farm member with read-only access to farm records. A Technician may be named
                      as observer or weigher in records entered by a Manager and receives
                      announcements from the Owner and Managers.

Key business          User account, farm, TECHNICIAN membership (status, source invitation, joined time,
attributes            last change with effective date and reason).

Business identity     Account + farm; a person has exactly one role in each farm.

Status / lifecycle    Membership ACTIVE ⇄ INACTIVE and TECHNICIAN ⇄ MANAGER, changed only by the
                      Owner with an effective date and reason.



Entity: Customer Support
Field                 Value

Purpose               Platform staff who handles the support tickets of Farm Owners: public replies,
                      CS-only notes, resolutions and internal requests to a named Platform Manager or
                      System Admin. Customer Support never accesses cattle or care records.

Key business          User account, platform role CS, access status, reason, changed by, changed time,
attributes            assigned tickets.

Business identity     An account with an ACTIVE CS platform role; at most one ACTIVE platform role per
                      account, independent of any farm.

Status / lifecycle    Access ACTIVE ⇄ REVOKED, changed only by a System Admin with a reason; role
                      changes are kept as history. Tickets of a suspended CS return to the unassigned
                      queue.



Entity: Platform Manager
Field                 Value




                                                                                                         40
Purpose                Platform staff who manages farm service (suspension and restoration), subscriptions,
                       plan price versions, bank-transfer reconciliation and basic platform cash records, and
                       handles internal requests addressed to them.

Key business           User account, platform role PM, access status, reason, changed by, changed time.
attributes

Business identity      An account with an ACTIVE PM platform role; at most one ACTIVE platform role per
                       account, independent of any farm.

Status / lifecycle     Access ACTIVE ⇄ REVOKED, changed only by a System Admin with a reason; role
                       changes are kept as history.



Entity: System Admin
Field                  Value

Purpose                Platform staff who suspends or restores user accounts, grants, changes, revokes or
                       restores CS, PM and SA access, reviews the platform audit history and handles
                       internal requests addressed to them.

Key business           User account, platform role SA, access status, reason, changed by, changed time.
attributes

Business identity      An account with an ACTIVE SA platform role; at most one ACTIVE platform role per
                       account.

Status / lifecycle     Access ACTIVE ⇄ REVOKED by another System Admin with a reason. At least one
                       ACTIVE System Admin must always remain, and an SA cannot suspend their own
                       account.



Entity: Farm
Field                  Value

Purpose                An independent farm workspace (tenant). All cattle, pens, catalogues, members and
                       the subscription of the farm belong to it.

Key business           Farm name, province, commune, detailed address, contact person and phone,
attributes             registration certificate information, expected herd size, main breeds, time zone,
                       operating notes, service status (derived), suspension flag, created time.

Business identity      System-generated Farm ID. Farm names need not be unique. One registration
                       request creates at most one farm.

Status / lifecycle     Service status PENDING_PAYMENT → ACTIVE ⇄ EXPIRED, and SUSPENDED whenever
                       suspended by a PM (GBR-13). Never deleted once it has history.

Detail – Farm          The link between an account and one farm, carrying the person's role in that farm.
Membership             Attributes: Account, farm, role (OWNER / MANAGER / TECHNICIAN), status, source
                       invitation, joined time, last change (effective date, reason). Identity: Account + farm
                       is unique. Exactly one OWNER membership per farm. Lifecycle: ACTIVE ⇄ INACTIVE
                       (Owner only). OWNER membership is created with the farm and never changes.
                       MANAGER ⇄ TECHNICIAN by the Owner. Never deleted.



                                                                                                             41
 Detail – Farm Service    One suspension or restoration of a farm's service by a Platform Manager. Attributes:
 Change                   Farm, action (SUSPEND / RESTORE), status before, status after, reason, PM, time.
                          Identity: Ordered by time within a farm. Lifecycle: Immutable once saved; the Owner
                          is notified after each change.

 Detail – Farm            One version of the farm's state and reminder parameters used by suggestions and
 Parameter Version        reminders. Attributes: Farm, version number, life-stage ages (weaning age,
                          young-stock age, adult age, in months), breeding conditions (minimum age,
                          minimum weight), gestation length, pregnancy-check interval, re-check interval,
                          calving preparation lead time, breeding window (start and end hours after estrus),
                          estrus cycle length, postpartum interval, BCS scale, weighing interval, vaccination
                          reminder lead time, target finishing weight (optional), source note, effective from,
                          changed by. Identity: Version number is unique within the farm; exactly one version
                          is effective at a time. Lifecycle: A new version applies to evaluations after its effective
                          time; old versions are kept.



Entity: Farm Invitation
 Field                   Value

 Purpose                 An Owner's invitation for an email address to join a farm as Farm Manager or Farm
                         Technician; it is accepted by the User Account whose verified email equals the invited
                         email.

 Key business            Farm, recipient email, role, invited by, sent time, last sent time, validity (3, 7 or 14
 attributes              days), expiry time, status, accepted account and membership.

 Business identity       At most one PENDING invitation per farm + email (case-insensitive). The link token is
                         single-use and stored only as a hash.

 Status / lifecycle      PENDING → ACCEPTED, CANCELLED or EXPIRED (all final).



B. Farm Reference Data
Entity: Breed
 Field                   Value

 Purpose                 A cattle breed used by the farm (for example Zebu đỏ, Lai Sind, Brahman đỏ).

 Key business            Farm, code, name, group, description, status, number of cattle referencing it.
 attributes

 Business identity       Code is unique within the farm.

 Status / lifecycle      ACTIVE ⇄ INACTIVE. A referenced breed is never deleted.



Entity: Production Purpose
 Field                   Value




                                                                                                                    42
 Purpose              The purpose for which the farm raises a cattle (for example "Bò sinh sản", "Vỗ béo
                      xuất chuồng", "Bò đực giống", "Chưa xác định").

 Key business         Farm, code, name, purpose type (BREEDING / FATTENING / SIRE / UNDECIDED),
 attributes           description, status.

 Business identity    Code is unique within the farm. Several purposes may share one purpose type.

 Status / lifecycle   ACTIVE ⇄ INACTIVE. Deactivation only blocks new selection; existing cattle keep their
                      purpose. The purpose type is fixed once the purpose is referenced (BR-D32).



Entity: Vaccine
 Field                Value

 Purpose              A vaccine reference used in schedules and vaccination records.

 Key business         Farm, code, name, disease prevented, manufacturer, reference dose and route,
 attributes           booster interval (days), meat withdrawal period (days), status.

 Business identity    Code is unique within the farm.

 Status / lifecycle   ACTIVE ⇄ INACTIVE. A vaccine never referenced may be deleted.



Entity: Medicine
 Field                Value

 Purpose              A reference medicine used when recording treatments. Reference only: no stock,
                      purchasing or prescription.

 Key business         Farm, code, name, reference dose, route, meat withdrawal period (days), notes,
 attributes           status.

 Business identity    Code is unique within the farm.

 Status / lifecycle   ACTIVE ⇄ INACTIVE. A referenced medicine is never deleted.



Entity: Pen
 Field                Value

 Purpose              A pen or care area of the farm in which cattle are placed.

 Key business         Farm, pen code, pen name, capacity, description, availability status, current
 attributes           headcount (derived), current criteria version.

 Business identity    Pen code is unique within the farm.

 Status / lifecycle   ACTIVE ⇄ INACTIVE (with effective date and reason). A pen with placement history is
                      never deleted.




                                                                                                           43
 Detail – Pen              One version of the advisory criteria a pen uses to check placements and to suggest
 Reception Criteria        pen moves. Attributes: Pen, version number, accepted purpose types, accepted life
                           stages, accepted sex, minimum age (months), minimum weight (kg), effective from,
                           changed by. Identity: Version number is unique within the pen. Lifecycle: A new
                           version applies to future placements and suggestions only.



C. Cattle and Herd Movement
Entity: Cattle
 Field                     Value

 Purpose                   One animal of the farm, followed from herd entry to herd exit. Its activity timeline is
                           a derived view of its source records (GBR-37).

 Key business              Internal Cattle ID, display code, secondary ear tag, name, sex, breed, birth date or
 attributes                estimated birth month with accuracy (EXACT / ESTIMATED / UNKNOWN), colour and
                           markings, production purpose, life stage (UNWEANED_CALF / WEANED_CALF /
                           YOUNG_STOCK / ADULT), herd status (ACTIVE / EXITED / VOIDED), exit type and date
                           or void reason, dam, sire (internal cattle or external sire/semen code). Derived: exit
                           pending, current pen, herd group, health state (HEALTHY / MONITORING /
                           UNDER_TREATMENT), meat withdrawal end date, reproductive status
                           (AWAITING_BREEDING / IN_ESTRUS / BRED / PREGNANT / POST_CALVING, or not
                           applicable), breeding eligibility, latest weight, ADG and projected finishing date
                           (FATTENING), reproduction indicators (females). The status dimensions are
                           summarised in 3.4.1.

 Business identity         Internal Cattle ID is system-generated, immutable and never reused. Display code is
                           unique within the farm.

 Status / lifecycle        Herd status ACTIVE → EXITED (confirmed exit) → ACTIVE (voided exit); ACTIVE →
                           VOIDED (herd entry voided; final). An EXITED or VOIDED cattle is read-only, and a
                           cattle on a PENDING death voucher accepts no new event (GBR-26, 3.4.2).

 Detail – Cattle Profile   The history of one change of production purpose, life stage, secondary ear tag or
 Change                    descriptive data. Attributes: Cattle, field group, old value, new value, effective date,
                           reason, changed by, changed time. Identity: Ordered by effective date within a cattle
                           and field. Lifecycle: Immutable once saved.

 Detail – Pen              One period during which a cattle is placed in a pen. Attributes: Cattle, pen, from date,
 Assignment                to date, source (HERD_ENTRY / MOVE / CORRECTION), reason, criteria version
                           checked, reception-check result and warning reason, moved by, recorded time.
                           Identity: At most one open assignment per cattle; periods never overlap. Lifecycle:
                           OPEN → CLOSED (move or exit). A closed assignment is never edited.



Entity: Cattle QR Tag
 Field                     Value

 Purpose                   A printed QR tag that identifies a cattle in the field.




                                                                                                                 44
Key business          Cattle, tag code, status, issued by, issued time, replacement reason (LOST /
attributes            DAMAGED / OTHER), note, replaced-by tag.

Business identity     Tag code is unique; at most one ACTIVE tag per cattle.

Status / lifecycle    ACTIVE → REPLACED (new tag issued) or RETIRED (herd entry voided); never
                      reactivated. The tag of an EXITED cattle stays ACTIVE so that a scan by a farm
                      member opens its read-only profile.



Entity: Herd Entry Voucher
Field                 Value

Purpose               One receipt of cattle into the farm (phiếu nhập), by purchase or as farm-born calves,
                      grouping one or more herd entries with their value and the payments to the seller.

Key business          Voucher code, entry type (PURCHASE / FARM_BORN), entry date, seller (trading
attributes            partner), quarantine certificate number (PURCHASE), vehicle plate, note, pricing
                      basis (PER_KG / PER_HEAD), cattle amount, transport fee, total payable, amount
                      paid, outstanding amount, payment due date, attachments, created by, posted by
                      and time, void reason, voided by and time. Derived: payment status (UNPAID /
                      PARTLY_PAID / PAID), number of cattle, total weight.

Business identity     Code PN-YYYY-nnnn, unique within the farm and never reused (BR-D34).

Status / lifecycle    DRAFT → POSTED → VOIDED; a DRAFT can be discarded. Only a DRAFT is edited;
                      posting creates the cattle of all lines in one transaction (GBR-90). A FARM_BORN
                      voucher has no amounts (GBR-93).

Detail – Herd Entry   How and when one cattle entered the herd; one line of a herd entry voucher.
                      Attributes: Herd entry voucher, line number, cattle, source (EXTERNAL /
                      FARM_BORN), entry date (from the voucher; the birth date for a farm-born calf),
                      entry weight with basis, unit price, line amount, calf outcome (FARM_BORN), initial
                      pen, status. Identity: Exactly one VALID herd entry per cattle; a calf outcome is
                      registered at most once; line number unique within the voucher. Lifecycle: Created
                      VALID when its voucher is posted. VALID → VOID for one line (UC-FE06-03) or for all
                      lines with the voucher (UC-FE06-05), only when the cattle has no other event
                      (GBR-33). Corrections create Record Revisions.

Detail – Pre-entry    Declared history of a bought-in cattle before entry, per category. Attributes: Herd
History Declaration   entry, category (VACCINATION / DISEASE / REPRODUCTION), status (KNOWN / NONE
                      / UNKNOWN), details (vaccine, date, disease, description), information source.
                      Identity: One declaration per herd entry and category, with item lines for KNOWN.
                      Lifecycle: Corrected only through UC-FE06-03.



Entity: Herd Exit Voucher
Field                 Value

Purpose               One departure of cattle from the farm (phiếu xuất) – sale, cull, death or missing, or
                      other – grouping herd exits with their value and the collections from the buyer;
                      confirmed by the Owner.



                                                                                                              45
 Key business         Voucher code, exit type (SALE / CULL / DEATH / OTHER), exit date, buyer (trading
 attributes           partner) or destination, transport quarantine certificate, vehicle plate, reason/notes,
                      loss kind (DIED / MISSING), cause and carcass disposal (DEATH), source health case,
                      pricing basis, cattle amount, deductions, total receivable, amount collected,
                      outstanding amount, collection due date, attachments, reported by, confirmed by
                      and time, dismiss or void reason, actor and time.

 Business identity    Code PX-YYYY-nnnn, unique within the farm and never reused (BR-D34).

 Status / lifecycle   DRAFT → CONFIRMED → VOIDED (Owner); PENDING (created from a health case
                      closed as DIED or MISSING) → CONFIRMED or DISMISSED; a DRAFT can be discarded
                      (GBR-94 to GBR-96). A DEATH voucher has no amounts.

 Detail – Herd Exit   The end of one cattle's stay in the herd; one line of a herd exit voucher. Attributes:
                      Herd exit voucher, line number, cattle, exit weight with basis (default the latest
                      weight), unit price, line amount, pre-confirmation check results (withdrawal, open
                      cases, future doses and reminders, pregnancy, draft calves), last pen, status. Identity:
                      A cattle is on at most one DRAFT, PENDING or CONFIRMED exit line at a time
                      (BR-D11, BR-D35). Lifecycle: Follows its voucher: DRAFT or PENDING → CONFIRMED
                      (cattle EXITED) or removed / DISMISSED; CONFIRMED → VOIDED with the voucher
                      (GBR-95).



Entity: Trading Partner
 Field                Value

 Purpose              A seller, buyer, market, slaughterhouse or feed supplier the farm deals with. Partners
                      have no BeefCare account.

 Key business         Name, partner roles (SELLER / BUYER / SUPPLIER), contact phone, area or address,
 attributes           note, status.

 Business identity    Name is unique within the farm (case-insensitive).

 Status / lifecycle   ACTIVE ⇄ INACTIVE; never deleted once referenced by a voucher (GBR-97).



Entity: Voucher Payment
 Field                Value

 Purpose              One payment made to a seller or feed supplier, or one collection received from a
                      buyer, recorded against a voucher.

 Key business         Voucher (herd entry voucher, herd exit voucher or feed receipt), direction (PAY /
 attributes           COLLECT), payment date, amount, method (CASH / BANK_TRANSFER), reference or
                      transfer content, attachment, note, recorded by and time, void reason, voided by and
                      time.

 Business identity    Ordered by payment date within the voucher; the sum of RECORDED payments never
                      exceeds the voucher total (BR-D39).




                                                                                                            46
Status / lifecycle    RECORDED → VOIDED (with reason; a wrong payment is voided and recorded again).
                      Farm bookkeeping only: not an invoice or tax document and not part of platform
                      finance (GBR-91).



D. Care and Health
Entity: Growth Assessment
Field                 Value

Purpose               One measurement of weight and/or body condition score of a cattle.

Key business          Cattle, measured time, weight (kg), method (ELECTRONIC_SCALE / TAPE /
attributes            OTHER_ESTIMATE), basis (MEASURED / ESTIMATED), BCS and scale version,
                      measured by, note, entry time, status.

Business identity     No natural key; at least one of weight or BCS is present.

Status / lifecycle    VALID → EXCLUDED (outlier) or VOIDED, with reason; a correction keeps the record
                      VALID with a "Corrected" marker and a Record Revision (GBR-40).



Entity: Vaccination Schedule
Field                 Value

Purpose               A plan to vaccinate a defined set of cattle with one vaccine.

Key business          Farm, name, vaccine, target selection (pens, herd groups, life stages, individual
attributes            cattle), start date, number of doses, days between doses, booster interval (months),
                      status, created by.

Business identity     System-generated ID; name is unique among ACTIVE schedules of the farm.

Status / lifecycle    ACTIVE → STOPPED. Changes affect only doses not yet given.

Detail – Scheduled    One planned dose of one cattle in a schedule. Attributes: Schedule, cattle, dose
Dose                  number (or booster), due date, status (PLANNED / DONE / DEFERRED / CANCELLED),
                      deferral reason, new expected date, fulfilling vaccination record. Identity: Schedule +
                      cattle + dose number is unique. Lifecycle: PLANNED → DONE, DEFERRED (→
                      PLANNED again with new date, or DONE) or CANCELLED.



Entity: Vaccination Record
Field                 Value

Purpose               One vaccination actually given to a cattle, or declared from its history.

Key business          Cattle, vaccine (and name snapshot), administered time, batch number, dose, route,
attributes            withdrawal days and withdrawal end date (derived, ON_FARM only), vaccinator,
                      source (ON_FARM / HISTORICAL), information source, scheduled dose, entry time,
                      status.

Business identity     No natural key; one record per cattle per administration.




                                                                                                           47
 Status / lifecycle    VALID → VOIDED, with reason; a correction keeps the record VALID with a
                       "Corrected" marker and a Record Revision (GBR-44).



Entity: Health Case
 Field                 Value

 Purpose               One health problem of a cattle, grouping its observations and treatments.

 Key business          Case code, cattle, opened time, main signs, severity (MILD / MODERATE / SEVERE),
 attributes            status (OPEN / CHRONIC / CLOSED), chronic since, outcome (RECOVERED / DIED /
                       MISSING / CLOSED_BY_EXIT), closed time, closed by, linked vaccination (reaction).

 Business identity     Case code is unique within the farm.

 Status / lifecycle    OPEN ⇄ CHRONIC (long-term monitoring; a new treatment returns the case to OPEN)
                       → CLOSED with an outcome (GBR-45). DIED or MISSING creates a PENDING herd
                       exit.

 Detail – Health       The opening observation or a follow-up observation of a health case. Attributes:
 Observation           Health case, observed time, body temperature, signs or progress description,
                       progress (IMPROVED / UNCHANGED / WORSENED, for follow-ups), observer,
                       recorded by, entry time. Identity: No natural key. Lifecycle: Saved once; an
                       observation is not a diagnosis.



Entity: Treatment
 Field                 Value

 Purpose               One treatment actually given within a health case.

 Key business          Health case, medicine (and name snapshot), dose, route, administered time,
 attributes            withdrawal days, withdrawal end date (derived), performer, veterinary advice,
                       recorded by, entry time.

 Business identity     No natural key; each administration is a separate record.

 Status / lifecycle    Saved once; a planned course is recorded as separate administrations when given.



E. Reproduction
Entity: Estrus Event
 Field                 Value

 Purpose               An observed estrus of a female cattle.

 Key business          Female, observed time, observer, description, recorded by, entry time, status.
 attributes

 Business identity     No natural key; a possible duplicate (same female within 12 hours) is flagged.

 Status / lifecycle    VALID → VOIDED (Manager, with reason); a correction keeps the record VALID with a
                       "Corrected" marker.



                                                                                                           48
Entity: Breeding Event
 Field                   Value

 Purpose                 An actual breeding (natural service or artificial insemination) of a female.

 Key business            Female, bred time, data source, method (NATURAL / AI), sire source (INTERNAL /
 attributes              EXTERNAL), internal sire, sire breed, semen straw/batch code, semen supplier,
                         performer, linked estrus, tentative expected calving date, note, recorded by, status.

 Business identity       No natural key. An internal sire is a different male cattle of the farm.

 Status / lifecycle      VALID → VOIDED; a correction keeps the record VALID with a "Corrected" marker.
                         Sets the female to BRED.



Entity: Pregnancy Check
 Field                   Value

 Purpose                 An actual pregnancy examination result.

 Key business            Female, checked time, data source, result (PREGNANT / NOT_PREGNANT /
 attributes              INCONCLUSIVE), method (ULTRASOUND / RECTAL / OTHER), examiner, linked
                         breeding, note, recorded by, status.

 Business identity       No natural key.

 Status / lifecycle      VALID → VOIDED; a correction keeps the record VALID with a "Corrected" marker. A
                         valid PREGNANT result opens or confirms an episode.



Entity: Pregnancy Episode
 Field                   Value

 Purpose                 One confirmed pregnancy of a female, from the confirming check to calving or loss.

 Key business            Female, pregnancy number (parity sequence), confirming check, linked breeding,
 attributes              expected calving date and basis, status, closed time, closing reason.

 Business identity       At most one OPEN episode per female.

 Status / lifecycle      OPEN → CALVED, LOST or DAM_EXITED; VOIDED when its confirming check is voided
                         without dependencies (3.4.5).

 Detail – Pregnancy      An observation, complication or loss recorded during a pregnancy. Attributes:
 Update                  Episode, occurred time, data source, update type (OBSERVATION / COMPLICATION /
                         LOSS), details, linked health case, recorded by, status. Identity: No natural key.
                         Lifecycle: VALID → VOIDED; a correction keeps it VALID with a "Corrected" marker. A
                         LOSS closes the episode as LOST.



Entity: Calving Event
 Field                   Value



                                                                                                                 49
Purpose                An actual birth of a dam, saved with all its calf outcomes.

Key business           Dam, linked episode (optional), birth time, data source, birth attendant, calving
attributes             course (NORMAL / ASSISTED / CAESAREAN), dam condition (GOOD /
                       NEEDS_MONITORING / CRITICAL), number of calves, recorded by, status.

Business identity      One calving per dam per birth; a second calving within 30 days is flagged.

Status / lifecycle     VALID → VOIDED; a correction keeps the record VALID with a "Corrected" marker.
                       Saving closes the episode as CALVED.



Entity: Calf Outcome
Field                  Value

Purpose                The result for one calf of a calving, including the draft data of a live calf.

Key business           Calving, calf number, outcome (LIVE / STILLBORN), sex (MALE / FEMALE /
attributes             UNKNOWN), birth weight, unit and basis, ear-tag/QR code, proposed pen, breed,
                       registration choice, registration state, registered cattle.

Business identity      Calf number is unique within the calving; ear-tag/QR code is unique within the farm.

Status / lifecycle     LIVE: PENDING_CONFIRMATION → REGISTERED, or DIED_BEFORE_REGISTRATION;
                       Register now goes directly to REGISTERED. STILLBORN is never registered.



F. Feed Management
Entity: Feed Item
Field                  Value

Purpose                A feed or supplement the farm stocks and feeds.

Key business           Code, name, feed group (GREEN_ROUGHAGE / DRY_ROUGHAGE / CONCENTRATE /
attributes             TMR / MINERAL_ADDITIVE / CALF_FEED), stock unit (kg), purchase unit and kg per
                       purchase unit, minimum stock level, nutrition note, status. Derived: current stock,
                       weighted average cost, stock value, average daily use (7 days), days of cover.

Business identity      Code TA-nn, unique within the farm.

Status / lifecycle     ACTIVE ⇄ INACTIVE; code and stock unit cannot change after the first stock
                       movement; a feed with movements is never deleted (GBR-98).



Entity: Feed Receipt
Field                  Value

Purpose                One delivery of feed from a supplier (phiếu nhập kho) that adds stock.

Key business           Receipt code, receipt date, supplier (trading partner), invoice or delivery-note
attributes             number, receiver, storage location, note, goods amount, transport fee, total payable,
                       amount paid, outstanding amount, payment due date, attachments, posted by and
                       time, void reason, voided by and time.



                                                                                                             50
 Business identity       Code PNK-YYYY-nnnn, unique within the farm (BR-D34).

 Status / lifecycle      DRAFT → POSTED → VOIDED; a DRAFT changes no stock; a POSTED receipt is voided
                         only while the stock still covers every line (GBR-99).

 Detail – Feed Receipt   One feed received on a feed receipt. Attributes: Feed receipt, line number, feed item,
 Line                    lot number, expiry date, quantity in the purchase unit, kg per purchase unit, quantity
                         in kg, unit price, line amount. Identity: Line number unique within the receipt.
                         Lifecycle: Follows its receipt; produces one RECEIPT stock movement when posted.



Entity: Stock Adjustment
 Field                   Value

 Purpose                 A stock count or loss record (phiếu điều chỉnh kho) that aligns system stock with
                         counted stock.

 Key business            Adjustment code, count date, adjustment type (PERIODIC_COUNT / LOSS / SPOILAGE
 attributes              / OTHER), counted by, attachments, total difference in kg and value, posted by and
                         time.

 Business identity       Code ĐCK-YYYY-nnnn, unique within the farm (BR-D34).

 Status / lifecycle      DRAFT → POSTED. A posted adjustment is immutable; a mistake is reversed by a new
                         adjustment (GBR-100).

 Detail – Stock          The count result of one feed item on a stock adjustment. Attributes: Stock
 Adjustment Line         adjustment, feed item, system stock, counted stock, difference, reason, value at
                         weighted average cost. Identity: One line per feed item per adjustment; a reason is
                         required when the difference is not zero. Lifecycle: Follows its adjustment; a
                         non-zero line produces one ADJUSTMENT stock movement.



Entity: Ration
 Field                   Value

 Purpose                 A planned feeding (khẩu phần) for one or more pens, kept as versions.

 Key business            Ration code, name, version number, herd group, pens, sessions per day and times,
 attributes              effective from, effective to, basis or source, created by and time. Derived: kg and
                         estimated cost per head per day, planned daily quantity and cost.

 Business identity       Code + version unique within the farm; at most one version is effective per pen and
                         date (BR-D38).

 Status / lifecycle      SCHEDULED → EFFECTIVE → ENDED. Saving a change creates a new version that
                         ends the previous one the day before its effective date; a SCHEDULED version can be
                         cancelled (GBR-101).

 Detail – Ration         The quantity of one feed in one ration version. Attributes: Ration version, feed item,
 Component               kg per head for each session, kg per head per day. Identity: One component per feed
                         item per ration version. Lifecycle: Immutable after the version takes effect.




                                                                                                               51
Entity: Feeding Record
 Field                 Value

 Purpose               One feeding session of one pen on one date.

 Key business          Pen, feeding date, session (MORNING / AFTERNOON / EVENING), feeding time, ration
 attributes            version, headcount at feeding, planned total kg, actual total kg, leftover kg, fed by
                       (member), recorded by and time, note, status.

 Business identity     Pen + date + session unique among VALID records (BR-D37).

 Status / lifecycle    VALID → VOIDED (with reason). A correction keeps the record VALID with a
                       "Corrected" marker and saves a Record Revision; stock is adjusted by the difference
                       (GBR-102).

 Detail – Feeding      The planned and actual quantity of one feed in one feeding record. Attributes:
 Record Line           Feeding record, feed item, planned kg, actual kg, in-ration flag, unit cost and cost at
                       weighted average cost. Identity: One line per feed item per feeding record. Lifecycle:
                       Follows its record; produces one FEEDING stock movement (and a correction
                       movement when changed).



G. Farm Announcements
Entity: Farm Announcement
 Field                   Value

 Purpose                 An administrative message from the Owner or a Manager to members of the farm.

 Key business            Farm, author membership, intended audience, state, current published version,
 attributes              created time.

 Business identity       No natural key; one farm and one author.

 Status / lifecycle      DRAFT → PUBLISHED. Published content is corrected only by new versions.

 Detail –                One immutable published content version. Attributes: Announcement, version
 Announcement            number, title, body, correction reason, published by, published time. Identity:
 Version                 Version number is unique within the announcement. Lifecycle: Immutable once
                         published.

 Detail –                The delivery of one version to one recipient, fixed at publication. Attributes: Version,
 Announcement            recipient membership, delivered time, read time. Identity: Version + recipient is
 Delivery                unique. Lifecycle: DELIVERED → READ.



H. Subscription and Platform Finance
Entity: Plan Price Version
 Field                 Value

 Purpose               An immutable published price list of the single BeefCare plan.




                                                                                                               52
 Key business          Version number, price for 1, 2 and 3 years (VND), supported terms, effective from,
 attributes            published by, published time.

 Business identity     Version number is unique; exactly one version is effective at any instant.

 Status / lifecycle    SCHEDULED → EFFECTIVE → SUPERSEDED, or SCHEDULED → WITHDRAWN before it
                       takes effect; never edited after publication.



Entity: Subscription
 Field                 Value

 Purpose               The billing relationship of one farm with BeefCare and its current coverage.

 Key business          Farm, current period (derived), future periods (derived), latest confirmed end,
 attributes            renewal opening date (derived), anniversary anchor.

 Business identity     At most one subscription per farm.

 Status / lifecycle    Created with the first payment request; coverage is derived from purchased periods.



Entity: Payment Request
 Field                 Value

 Purpose               One Owner-initiated quote for a 1-, 2- or 3-year purchase with immutable transfer
                       instructions.

 Key business          Payment reference (e.g. BCF7K2Q9), farm, subscription, requested by, price version,
 attributes            term (years), amount (VND), receiving bank, account number and account name,
                       created time, expiry time (created + 24 h), status, bank-paid time, received time,
                       confirmed time, confirmation source (SEPAY / PM).

 Business identity     Payment reference is unique; at most one unexpired PENDING request per farm.

 Status / lifecycle    PENDING → PAID or EXPIRED; EXPIRED → PAID only for evidence paid within the
                       original window.

 Detail – Purchased    The service period granted by one accepted payment. Attributes: Payment request,
 Period                subscription, start, end, term. Identity: One period per PAID payment request.
                       Lifecycle: Immutable once created.



Entity: Bank Transfer Evidence
 Field                 Value

 Purpose               An authenticated bank transaction received from SePay (or looked up by the PM),
                       kept whether or not it matches a payment.

 Key business          Provider, environment, account namespace, provider transaction ID, bank, receiving
 attributes            account, direction, amount, transfer content, extracted payment code, bank
                       reference, bank-paid time, first received time, delivery attempts, match state, review
                       state, allocated payment, handling lock.




                                                                                                            53
 Business identity       Provider + environment + namespace + provider transaction ID is unique.

 Status / lifecycle      RECEIVED → MATCHED (allocated, terminal) or UNMATCHED / NEEDS_REVIEW →
                         ACCEPTED, HELD, REJECTED or LOCKED_FOR_RETURN.

 Detail – Evidence       A PM decision or handling event on bank evidence. Attributes: Evidence, related
 Review                  payment request, decision (ACCEPT / HOLD / REJECT / LOCK / UNLOCK /
                         RETURN_VERIFIED), reason, evidence references, PM, time. Identity: One entry per
                         committed decision. Lifecycle: Immutable history; the latest entry gives the current
                         review state.



Entity: Manual Finance Record
 Field                   Value

 Purpose                 Platform income or expense other than subscription receipts, recorded by the PM.

 Key business            Record code (FR-nnnn), type (INCOME / EXPENSE), category, amount (VND),
 attributes              occurrence date, scope (GENERAL / FARM) and farm, document number, description,
                         source (MANUAL / BANK_TRANSACTION) and bank transaction reference, status,
                         created/posted/voided by and time, void reason, replacement record.

 Business identity       Record code is unique; a bank transaction is recognized at most once (GBR-84).

 Status / lifecycle      DRAFT → POSTED → VOID; a DRAFT may be discarded.



I. Customer Support
Entity: Support Ticket
 Field                   Value

 Purpose                 An Owner's support case about their account or one owned farm.

 Key business            Ticket code (TK-nnnn), requester, related farm (optional), category, priority, subject,
 attributes              description, status, assigned CS, latest resolution version, current acknowledgment,
                         created and updated time.

 Business identity       Ticket code is unique; the requester never changes.

 Status / lifecycle      OPEN → IN_PROGRESS ⇄ WAITING_CUSTOMER → RESOLVED → CLOSED; RESOLVED
                         → OPEN on reopen (GBR-78).

 Detail – Ticket History The immutable chronology of assignment and status changes of a ticket and its
 Event                   internal requests. Attributes: Ticket, internal request (optional), event type,
                         before/after status or assignee, actor and role, reason, time. Identity: One event per
                         committed change. Lifecycle: Append-only.



Entity: Ticket Message
 Field                   Value

 Purpose                 A PUBLIC reply or a CS-only internal note on a ticket.




                                                                                                                54
 Key business          Ticket, author and role, audience (PUBLIC / CS_ONLY), content, created time.
 attributes

 Business identity     No natural key; ordered by time.

 Status / lifecycle    Immutable; no edit or delete.



Entity: Ticket Resolution
 Field                 Value

 Purpose               One immutable PUBLIC resolution version recorded by the assigned CS.

 Key business          Ticket, version number, resolution text, resolved by, resolved time.
 attributes

 Business identity     Version number is unique within the ticket.

 Status / lifecycle    Immutable; a later resolution supersedes it for closure.

 Detail – Resolution   The Owner's explicit confirmation that a resolution version solved the issue.
 Acknowledgment        Attributes: Ticket, resolution version, requester, confirmed time, optional comment,
                       reviewed by CS and time. Identity: One effective acknowledgment per resolution
                       version. Lifecycle: EFFECTIVE → SUPERSEDED (reopen or new resolution).



Entity: Internal Request
 Field                 Value

 Purpose               A named request from the assigned CS to a Platform Manager or System Admin.

 Key business          Request code (IR-nnn), ticket, target role (PM / SA), recipient, created by CS,
 attributes            question, permitted context references, status, outcome (PERFORMED / UNABLE /
                       OUTSIDE_DOMAIN), sanitized result, domain reference, completed time,
                       reassignment/cancellation reason.

 Business identity     At most one OPEN request per ticket and target role.

 Status / lifecycle    OPEN → DONE or CANCELLED.



J. Supporting Records (not drawn in the conceptual diagram)
These records are created by the system from the business entities above for reminders, traceability,
notification or reporting. They are not managed as business entities by users and are not drawn in
the conceptual diagram.
Entity: Reminder
 Field                 Value

 Purpose               A system-generated reminder or suggestion derived from a source record and a
                       parameter (rules REM-01 to REM-13).




                                                                                                          55
 Key business          Farm, cattle, rule, source record, parameter version, due time or window, basis label
 attributes            (TENTATIVE / CONFIRMED where relevant), audience, lifecycle.

 Business identity     Rule + source record + occurrence key is unique.

 Status / lifecycle    ACTIVE → RESOLVED, CANCELLED or SUPERSEDED.



Entity: Audit Log Entry
 Field                 Value

 Purpose               An append-only record of one important committed change on the platform,
                       reviewed centrally by the System Admin.

 Key business          Time, actor account and role (empty for system actions), farm (when applicable),
 attributes            action code, target type and ID, allowlisted before/after values, correlation ID.

 Business identity     One entry per committed change; system-generated ID.

 Status / lifecycle    No status. Written in the same transaction as the change; never edited or deleted.



Entity: Record Revision
 Field                 Value

 Purpose               The immutable before/after history of a correction or void of a correctable record
                       (herd entry, growth assessment, vaccination record, reproductive records).

 Key business          Record type and ID, action (CORRECT / EXCLUDE / VOID), reason, before values, after
 attributes            values, changed by, changed time.

 Business identity     One revision per accepted correction command.

 Status / lifecycle    Immutable.



Entity: Notification
 Field                 Value

 Purpose               An in-app notice to one recipient about one business target.

 Key business          Recipient account, category (subscription, support, reconciliation, announcement),
 attributes            target type and ID, title, summary, created time, read time.

 Business identity     Recipient + source event is unique.

 Status / lifecycle    UNREAD → READ.



Entity: Stock Movement
 Field                 Value

 Purpose               The append-only ledger of every stock change of a feed item.




                                                                                                            56
 Key business         Feed item, movement time, type (RECEIPT / RECEIPT_VOID / ADJUSTMENT / FEEDING
 attributes           / FEEDING_CORRECTION / FEEDING_VOID), quantity in kg (+/−), unit cost, value,
                      source line, balance after.

 Business identity    Ordered by movement time within a feed item.

 Status / lifecycle   Immutable. Current stock = sum of movements and is never negative (BR-D36).



Entity: Subscription Receipt
 Field                Value

 Purpose              A derived, read-only receipt of one accepted PAID payment (not stored as an editable
                      record).

 Key business         Receipt code (RC-yy-nnnn), payment reference, farm, term, price version, amount,
 attributes           bank-paid time, confirmed time, purchased period, evidence reference.

 Business identity    One receipt per PAID payment request.

 Status / lifecycle   No lifecycle; exists while its payment is PAID.



Entity: Cash Report Snapshot
 Field                Value

 Purpose              One immutable result of a platform cash report run, used for display and export.

 Key business         Snapshot code, requested by, generated time, period, farm filter, source filter, rows
 attributes           with source references and versions, totals (receipts, other income, expense, net) per
                      scope.

 Business identity    Snapshot code is unique.

 Status / lifecycle   AVAILABLE → EXPIRED (after the retention time); a refresh creates a new snapshot.



Entity: Public Landing Content
 Field                Value

 Purpose              The published static content of the public landing page (introduction, features, how it
                      works, FAQ, contact).

 Key business         Content release reference, sections, FAQ items, contact email and phone; prices are
 attributes           read from the effective Plan Price Version.

 Business identity    One published release at a time.

 Status / lifecycle   Changed by deployment; no end-user content management.

3.4 State Models
This section brings together the statuses of a cattle and of the records that change them. A stored
status changes only through the use cases named below; a derived status is recalculated after every
saved source change and is never edited directly (GBR-24). The state diagrams use UML state
machine notation: each transition is labelled with its triggering event and, where needed, a guard in
brackets.

3.4.1 Cattle Status Dimensions
A cattle has several independent status dimensions. Table 3.4-1 lists them with their values and the
Vietnamese interface labels.

On lists and in the profile header the badges appear in this order: herd status (or Exit pending),
health state, meat withdrawal, reproductive status. An EXITED or VOIDED cattle shows only its herd
status with the exit type and date or the void reason; its derived states are not shown and its full
history stays visible (GBR-26).

3.4.3 Reproductive Status




                                                                                                              63
                    [Figure to be inserted] State diagram – Reproductive status of a female




Reproductive status applies to an ACTIVE female at life stage YOUNG_STOCK or ADULT whose
purpose type is BREEDING or UNDECIDED, and to any female with an OPEN pregnancy episode
(GBR-54). It is recalculated from her latest VALID events; a voided event is ignored.
Table 3.4-3 Reproductive status transitions

From                     Event                           To                       Rule or guard

Not applicable           The female becomes              AWAITING_BREEDING, GBR-54
                         applicable (stage or purpose    or the status given by

3.4.4 Health Case and Health State
                           [Figure to be inserted] State diagram – Health case




Table 3.4-4 Health case transitions

 From              Event (use case)                      To                  Rule or guard

 —                 Abnormal observation opens a case     OPEN                An observation may instead
                   (UC-FE10-02)                                              be added to an OPEN or
                                                                             CHRONIC case of the same
                                                                             cattle (GBR-45).

 OPEN              Manager moves the case to             CHRONIC             —
                   long-term monitoring (UC-FE10-03)

 CHRONIC           Treatment recorded (UC-FE10-04)       OPEN                GBR-45
                   or Manager reopens the case

 OPEN, CHRONIC     Closed with outcome RECOVERED         CLOSED              —
                   (UC-FE10-03)

 OPEN, CHRONIC     Closed with outcome DIED or           CLOSED              Creates a PENDING death
                   MISSING                                                   voucher and REM-12
                                                                             (GBR-95).

 OPEN, CHRONIC     Herd exit confirmed                   CLOSED              Outcome CLOSED_BY_EXIT
                                                                             (GBR-35).


The health state of an ACTIVE cattle is UNDER_TREATMENT while an OPEN case has at least one
treatment, otherwise MONITORING while any case is OPEN or CHRONIC, otherwise HEALTHY. It
returns to MONITORING when the Manager moves the treated case to CHRONIC, and to HEALTHY
when all cases are closed (GBR-45).

4. User Requirements

4.1 Actors
#      Actor                   Description

1      Guest                   Unauthenticated visitor. Views the public landing page (introduction,
                               features, 1/2/3-year prices, FAQ, contact), registers as a Farm Owner, signs
                               in and resets a forgotten password. Sees no other farm, ticket or financial
                               data.

2      Farm Owner              Account holder who completed Owner registration and owns one or more
                               farms. In each owned farm: views all farm data; manages farm information,
                               members and roles; manages the subscription and payments; changes
                               production purposes; records herd entry vouchers and their payments
                               (including farm-born calves); records, confirms, dismisses and voids herd
                               exit vouchers and records collections from buyers; maintains pens, cattle
                               profiles and QR tags; maintains feed stock, feed receipts, rations and the
                               feed catalogue and views the feeding log; writes announcements to
                               Managers and Technicians; uses the dashboard, reports and support. Does
                               not record care or reproduction events or feeding sessions.

3      Farm Manager            Farm member with the Manager role in one farm. Records and corrects
                               growth, vaccination, health and reproduction events; records daily feeding
                               sessions; maintains pens, pen moves, herd entry vouchers and their
                               payments (including farm-born calves), cattle profiles (except production
                               purpose), QR tags, catalogues and parameters, feed stock, feed receipts,
                               rations and the feed catalogue; writes announcements to Technicians; uses
                               the dashboard and reports. Cannot manage members, the subscription or
                               herd exit vouchers.

4      Farm Technician         Farm member with the Technician role in one farm. Field user with
                               read-only access: scans QR tags, views cattle, pens, care and reproduction
                               information, today's rations, the feeding log, reminders and received
                               announcements. May be named as observer or performer in records
                               entered by a Manager, or as the person who fed in a feeding record.

5      Customer Support (CS)   Platform staff. Reads the support queue; the assigned CS claims, processes,
                               replies, writes internal notes, resolves and closes tickets and sends internal
                               requests to a named PM or SA. Has no farm operational access.

6      Platform Manager        Platform staff. Views farm service status and suspends or restores farm
       (PM)                    service; views subscriptions, payments and bank evidence and reconciles
                               payments; publishes plan prices; manages manual finance records and the
                               platform cash report; handles internal requests assigned to them. Has no
                               access to cattle or care data.




                                                                                                            67
 7     System Admin (SA)         Platform staff. Views and suspends or restores user accounts, grants and
                                 revokes CS, PM and SA access, reviews the platform audit history and
                                 handles internal requests assigned to them. Keeps at least one ACTIVE
                                 System Admin.

 8     Google Identity           External system that authenticates a person through OAuth 2.0 / OpenID
       Provider                  Connect and returns the verified identity.

 9     SePay                     External system that reports bank transactions on the BeefCare receiving
                                 account through authenticated webhooks, provides transaction lookup and
                                 renders VietQR images.

 10    Email Delivery Service    External system that delivers transactional emails (invitations, verification
                                 and reset links, notices).

 11    System Timer              Internal scheduler that runs the background jobs in III.24 (reminder
                                 evaluation, expiries, notices).



4.2 Use Cases (UC)
The use cases are grouped by feature (FE-01 to FE-23); Table 4.2-1 maps the features to the business
flows. Use case names follow the verb + object form; actor conditions are detailed in the Permission
Matrix (4.4) and the specifications (II).

4.2.1 FE-01 Authentication and Account Access
 ID            Use Case                     Feature              Use Case Description

               Account                                           with an email and password verified through
                                                                 an emailed link, and completes the Owner
                                                                 profile so the account can register farms.

                                                                 verified email and password and reaches only
                                                                 the farms and platform areas currently
                                                                 authorized.

 UC-FE01-03    Sign Out                     FE-01                A signed-in user ends the BeefCare session
                                                                 and clears the selected farm on the device.

 UC-FE01-04    View and Update Own          FE-01                A signed-in user views own profile, sign-in
               Profile                                           methods, roles and permissions in the
                                                                 current farm, and updates full name and
                                                                 contact phone.

 UC-FE01-05    Reset Forgotten              FE-01                A Guest receives a single-use reset link by
               Password                                          email and sets a new password.

                                                                 adds a first password to an account that uses
                                                                 only Google.

5. System Functionalities
BeefCare is one responsive web application used in desktop, tablet and phone browsers; no native
mobile application is required. Farm users work inside the selected farm (left sidebar on desktop,
bottom navigation on phone); platform staff use a separate platform console. Scanning a QR ear tag
uses the phone camera through the browser. External integrations are Google sign-in, SePay (bank
transaction webhooks, transaction lookup and VietQR) and an email delivery service; background
jobs evaluate reminders, expiries and notices.

from the cattle profile tab of the selected animal (opened by QR scan or from a list), except batch
vaccination recording, which opens from the vaccination schedule.

5.2 External API Inventory
#         API Name                    Calling Path            Description

API-01    VietQR image                BeefCare → SePay QR     Renders the VietQR image of a payment
                                      service                 request from the receiving bank, account
                                                              number, amount and payment reference.

API-02    SePay transaction           SePay → BeefCare        Delivers each bank transaction on the
          webhook                                             BeefCare receiving account; BeefCare
                                                              authenticates it, stores the evidence and runs
                                                              payment confirmation.

API-03    SePay transaction lookup BeefCare → SePay           Lets the PM reconciliation workbench retrieve
                                                              transactions of the receiving account for a
                                                              period or transaction ID.

API-04    Transactional email         BeefCare → Email        Sends invitation, email-verification,
                                      Delivery Service        password-reset, password-changed,
                                                              subscription and support notification emails.

API-05    Google sign-in (OAuth       BeefCare ↔ Google       Redirects the user to Google and validates the
          2.0 / OpenID Connect)       Identity Provider       returned identity (subject, verified email,
                                                              name) for registration, sign-in and invitation
                                                              acceptance.



5.3 Background Job Inventory
#         Job Name                           Group              Description

JOB-01    Evaluate farm reminders            Scheduled +        Every 15 minutes and after each saved
                                             event              source change: creates, updates, resolves or
                                                                cancels reminders REM-01 to REM-13.

JOB-02    Expire farm invitations            Scheduled          Every 15 minutes: marks PENDING
                                                                invitations past their expiry time as
                                                                EXPIRED.




                                                                                                          97
 JOB-03    Expire unpaid payment requests     Scheduled          Every 5 minutes: marks PENDING payment
                                                                 requests past their 24-hour expiry as
                                                                 EXPIRED.

 JOB-04    Evaluate subscription coverage     Scheduled          Every 15 minutes: recalculates farm service
                                                                 status when a purchased period starts or
                                                                 ends.

 JOB-05    Generate subscription notices      Scheduled          Daily at 07:00 (Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh): creates
                                                                 renewal-opening, expiry-approaching and
                                                                 expired notices for Owners.

 JOB-06    Process received bank evidence     Queue              Continuously: processes stored SePay
                                                                 evidence through payment confirmation;
                                                                 retries unfinished work.

 JOB-07    Dispatch notifications and         Queue              Every minute: delivers committed in-app
           emails                                                notifications and emails with retry and
                                                                 deduplication.

 JOB-08    Purge expired tokens and           Scheduled          Daily at 02:00: removes expired verification,
           snapshots                                             reset and invitation link tokens and expired
                                                                 report snapshots.




II. Use Case Specifications
This part specifies the use cases that belong to the main business flows or contain complex rules.
Viewing, search and simple maintenance (CRUD) use cases are described screen by screen in Part III.
Every specification uses the same identifiers as I.4.2; messages (MSG-…) are listed in V.2 and rules
(GBR-…, BR-D…) in V.1 and I.3.3. Common behaviour that applies to all specifications: the system
rechecks permission and farm service on every request (GBR-09), repeated submissions return the
first result (GBR-88), and an update based on an outdated version is rejected with MSG-COM-05
(GBR-89).
Table II-1 Use cases specified in this part

 Feature                  Specified use cases (other use cases of the feature are described in Part III)

 FE-01                    UC-FE01-01, UC-FE01-02, UC-FE01-05, UC-FE01-06

 FE-02                    UC-FE02-01, UC-FE02-02, UC-FE02-05

 FE-03                    UC-FE03-01, UC-FE03-03, UC-FE03-04, UC-FE03-06, UC-FE03-08, UC-FE03-09

 FE-04                    UC-FE04-05

 FE-05                    UC-FE05-03, UC-FE05-04, UC-FE05-05

 FE-06                    UC-FE06-01, UC-FE06-03, UC-FE06-05

 FE-07                    UC-FE07-02, UC-FE07-03

 FE-08                    UC-FE08-01, UC-FE08-03



                                                                                                            98
FE-09                      UC-FE09-02, UC-FE09-05, UC-FE09-06, UC-FE09-08

FE-10                      UC-FE10-02, UC-FE10-03, UC-FE10-04

FE-13                      UC-FE13-01, UC-FE13-03
