1. FE-01 Authentication and Account Access

1.1 Register Farm Owner Account
UC ID and Name         UC-FE01-01 — Register Farm Owner Account

Created By             Ducnv                               Date Created        17/10/2026

Primary Actor          Guest                               Secondary Actors    Google Identity Provider;
                                                                               Email Delivery Service

Trigger                The Guest selects "Register as Farm Owner" (Đăng ký chủ trại) on the landing page or
                       the sign-in page.

Description            As a farm owner, I want to create a BeefCare account with my Google account or with
                       my email and a password, so that I can register and manage my farms.

Preconditions          PRE-1: The user is not signed in.
                       PRE-2: The registration page is available.

Postconditions         POST-1: One ACTIVE account with a verified email exists (created or reused) and the
                       user is signed in.
                       POST-2: Owner registration is completed and the user is on Register Farm (UC-FE02-01).
                       POST-3: No farm, membership, platform role or subscription has been created.




                                                                                                             99
Normal Flow            1. Guest opens Register Farm Owner (SCR-03) and selects Continue with Google.
                       2. System redirects the Guest to Google (API-05).
                       3. Guest authenticates and gives consent at Google.
                       4. Google returns the identity (subject, verified email, name) to the System.
                       5. System validates the identity and finds the account by Google subject or verified
                           email; when none exists it creates an ACTIVE account with that email and links
                           the Google identity (GBR-01).
                       6. System signs the user in and shows Complete Owner Profile (SCR-07): verified
                           email read-only, full name proposed from Google.
                       7. Guest confirms the full name, enters an optional contact phone, accepts the
                           Terms of Use and Data Policy and selects "Complete and register farm".
                       8. System validates the fields, marks Owner registration complete and opens
                           Register Farm (SCR-11).

Alternative Flows   AF1 – Register with email and password (step 1)
                       1. Guest enters full name, email, password and confirmation, accepts the terms and
                           selects Create account.
                       2. System validates the fields (GBR-02) and checks that no account uses the email.
                       3. System creates the account with a password method pending verification, sends
                           a verification link valid for 24 hours (API-04, GBR-03) and shows Verify Email
                           (SCR-04) with MSG-FE01-04.
                       4. Guest opens the link from the email.
                       5. System verifies the link, activates the account and its password, signs the user in
                           and continues at step 6 with the name already entered.
                    AF2 – Account already exists (step 5): the existing account is reused. If Owner
                    registration is already complete, the System opens Select Farm (SCR-10); otherwise it
                    continues at step 6.
                    AF3 – Guest leaves before step 7: the account remains; the next sign-in resumes
                    Complete Owner Profile.
                    AF4 – Resend verification email (AF1 step 3): the System invalidates the previous link
                    and sends a new one; at most 5 emails per hour per address.

Exceptions          E1: Google sign-in cancelled or not completed: MSG-FE01-12; no account is created.
                    E2: AF1 with an email that already has an account: MSG-FE01-10 with links to Sign In
                    and Forgot Password; no second account.
                    E3: Invalid fields: MSG-COM-06, MSG-COM-10, MSG-FE01-08, MSG-FE01-09 or
                    MSG-FE01-14 under the field.
                    E4: Verification link expired or already used: MSG-FE01-06 with Resend.
                    E5: Account SUSPENDED: the Account Suspended screen (MSG-FE01-03) is shown; no
                    session.
                    E6: Email delivery or save failure: MSG-COM-02; nothing is partially saved.

Priority            High

Frequency of Use    Once per Farm Owner.

Business Rules      GBR-01, GBR-02, GBR-03, GBR-05; BR-D02, BR-D03. Owner registration grants no farm,
                    role or service. Phone is contact information only.




                                                                                                          100
Other Information   Screens SCR-01, SCR-03, SCR-04, SCR-07. API-04, API-05. Related: UC-FE01-02,
                    UC-FE02-01.

Assumptions         Google manages its own authentication, security and recovery; BeefCare never receives
                    a Google password.



1.2 Sign In
UC ID and Name      UC-FE01-02 — Sign In

Created By          Ducnv                               Date Created           17/10/2026

Primary Actor       Guest                               Secondary Actors       Google Identity Provider

Trigger             The Guest selects "Sign in", or opens a protected page, a notification link or an
                    invitation link while signed out.

Description         As a registered user, I want to sign in with Google or with my email and password, so
                    that I can work in the farms and platform areas I am authorized for.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The user has a BeefCare account.
                    PRE-2: The user is not signed in.

Postconditions      POST-1: A session exists and the user sees only the farms and platform areas currently
                    authorized.
                    POST-2: Signing in never accepts an invitation, restores revoked access or changes a
                    subscription.

Normal Flow            1. Guest opens Sign In (SCR-02), enters email and password, optionally ticks
                           "Remember me" and selects Sign in.
                       2. System checks that the account exists, its password is active (email verified),
                           password sign-in is not locked and the password matches the stored hash
                           (GBR-02).
                       3. System checks that the account is ACTIVE (GBR-04) and resets the failed-attempt
                           counter.
                       4. System creates the session: valid until 8 hours of inactivity, or 30 days with
                           "Remember me".
                       5. System resolves the user's current platform role and ACTIVE farm memberships
                           (GBR-09).
                       6. System opens the destination: the requested page when it is still authorized;
                           otherwise the role home screen of a platform staff member (CS: SCR-96, PM:
                           SCR-13, SA: SCR-20), the farm home screen when the user has exactly one
                           available farm (Owner or Manager: SCR-77, Technician: SCR-104), or Select Farm
                           (SCR-10) when there are several.




                                                                                                            101
Alternative Flows   AF1 – Continue with Google (step 1): System redirects to Google (API-05), validates the
                    returned identity and finds the account by Google subject or verified email. When no
                    account exists, the System creates an ACTIVE account without Owner registration and
                    continues at step 4.
                    AF2 – Started from an invitation link (step 6): System continues with UC-FE03-04.
                    AF3 – No farm and no platform role (step 6): System shows MSG-FE01-15 with
                    "Register as Farm Owner" (continues at UC-FE01-01 step 6) and guidance to open the
                    invitation email.
                    AF4 – Owner registration started but not completed (step 6): System opens Complete
                    Owner Profile (SCR-07).
                    AF5 – Forgot password (step 1): UC-FE01-05.
                    AF6 – Email not verified (step 2): MSG-FE01-11 with "Resend verification email".

Exceptions          E1: Wrong email or password: MSG-FE01-01; the failed attempt is counted. The
                    message does not reveal which value is wrong.
                    E2: 5 consecutive failures: password sign-in is locked for 15 minutes (MSG-FE01-02);
                    Google sign-in remains available.
                    E3: Account SUSPENDED: Account Suspended screen (MSG-FE01-03) with the official
                    contact (GBR-87); no session.
                    E4: Google sign-in cancelled or failed: MSG-FE01-12.
                    E5: The requested farm is no longer authorized: MSG-COM-01 and the remaining
                    authorized farms are shown.
                    E6: Service unavailable: MSG-COM-03; no session.

Priority            High

Frequency of Use    Several times a day per user.

Business Rules      GBR-01, GBR-02, GBR-04, GBR-09, GBR-14.

Other Information   Screens SCR-02, SCR-10. API-05. Related: UC-FE01-01, UC-FE01-05, UC-FE02-02,
                    UC-FE03-04.

Assumptions         Session duration values are configuration defaults.



1.3 Reset Forgotten Password
UC ID and Name      UC-FE01-05 — Reset Forgotten Password

Created By          Ducnv                               Date Created         17/10/2026

Primary Actor       Guest                               Secondary Actors     Email Delivery Service

Trigger             The Guest selects "Forgot password?" (Quên mật khẩu?) on Sign In.

Description         As a user who forgot my password, I want to receive a single-use link by email and set a
                    new password, so that I can sign in again.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The user is not signed in.

Postconditions      POST-1: The new password hash is saved, all sessions of the account are ended and a
                    password-changed email is sent.
                    POST-2: Roles, memberships and the Google sign-in method are unchanged.



                                                                                                           102
Normal Flow            1. Guest enters the email on Forgot Password (SCR-05) and selects "Send reset link".
                       2. System always shows the same confirmation (MSG-FE01-05). When an ACTIVE
                           password method exists for the email, it creates a reset link valid for 30 minutes
                           and invalidates any earlier link (GBR-03).
                       3. Email Delivery Service sends the link (API-04).
                       4. Guest opens the link.
                       5. System checks that the link is unused and unexpired and shows Set New
                           Password (SCR-06) with the email read-only.
                       6. Guest enters and confirms the new password and selects "Save new password".
                       7. System validates the password (GBR-02), saves only its hash, marks the link used,
                           ends all sessions, clears any lockout and sends a password-changed email.
                       8. System shows Sign In with MSG-FE01-07.

Alternative Flows   AF1 – Account uses only Google (step 2): the email advises signing in with Google; no
                    password is created.
                    AF2 – Resend (step 2): Guest selects Resend; a new link replaces the previous one (max
                    5 per hour).
                    AF3 – Guest remembers the password: Guest returns to Sign In; nothing changes.

Exceptions          E1: Link expired or already used (step 5): screen "Link no longer valid" (MSG-FE01-06)
                    with "Send new link".
                    E2: Password rules not met or confirmation different: MSG-FE01-08 / MSG-FE01-09; the
                    old password stays.
                    E3: Account SUSPENDED: the password can be reset but sign-in stays blocked (GBR-04).
                    E4: Email delivery or save failure: MSG-COM-02.

Priority            Medium

Frequency of Use    Rare.

Business Rules      GBR-02, GBR-03, GBR-04. The response never reveals whether an email is registered.

Other Information   Screens SCR-02, SCR-05, SCR-06. API-04.

Assumptions         The user has access to the mailbox of the account email.



1.4 Change Password
UC ID and Name      UC-FE01-06 — Change Password

Created By          Ducnv                           Date Created               17/10/2026

Primary Actor       Farm Owner, Farm Manager,       Secondary Actors           Google Identity Provider
                    Farm Technician, Customer                                  (re-authentication); Email
                    Support, Platform Manager,                                 Delivery Service
                    System Admin

Trigger             The user selects "Change password" (Đổi mật khẩu) or "Add password" on My Profile
                    (SCR-08).

Description         As a signed-in user, I want to change my password, or add a first password to my
                    Google-only account, so that I can also sign in with email and password.



                                                                                                            103
Preconditions       PRE-1: The user is signed in and the account is ACTIVE.

Postconditions      POST-1: The new password hash is saved, other sessions are ended and a
                    password-changed email is sent.
                    POST-2: Roles, memberships and the Google sign-in method are unchanged.

Normal Flow             1. User selects Change password; System shows the Change Password dialog
                            (SCR-09).
                        2. User enters the current password, the new password and the confirmation and
                            selects Save.
                        3. System checks the current password (a wrong value counts toward lockout,
                            GBR-02) and validates the new password.
                        4. System saves the new hash, keeps the current session, ends the other sessions
                            and sends a password-changed email.
                        5. System closes the dialog and shows MSG-FE01-07.

Alternative Flows   AF1 – Add a first password (step 1): for an account without a password, System asks
                    the user to re-authenticate with Google (sign-in within the last 5 minutes); the dialog
                    then asks only for the new password and confirmation. The password becomes active
                    immediately because the email is already verified.
                    AF2 – Forgot the current password (step 2): user re-authenticates with Google instead,
                    or signs out and uses UC-FE01-05.
                    AF3 – Cancel: nothing changes.

Exceptions          E1: Current password wrong: MSG-FE01-16; after 5 failures MSG-FE01-02.
                    E2: New password invalid or confirmation different: MSG-FE01-08 / MSG-FE01-09.
                    E3: Session expired: the user must sign in again; nothing is saved.
                    E4: Save failure: MSG-COM-02; the previous password stays.

Priority            Medium

Frequency of Use    Rare.

Business Rules      GBR-02. Passwords are never displayed, logged or returned.

Other Information   Screens SCR-08, SCR-09. API-04, API-05.

Assumptions         —




2. FE-02 Farm Workspace and Service Management

2.1 Register Farm Workspace
UC ID and Name      UC-FE02-01 — Register Farm Workspace

Created By          Ducnv                            Date Created             17/10/2026

Primary Actor       Farm Owner                       Secondary Actors         None

Trigger             The Owner completes the Owner profile (UC-FE01-01) or selects "Register new farm"
                    (Đăng ký trại mới) on Select Farm.




                                                                                                         104
Description         As a farm owner, I want to register a farm, so that I can subscribe it and manage its herd
                    separately from my other farms.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The Owner is signed in, the account is ACTIVE and Owner registration is
                    complete.

Postconditions      POST-1: One new farm exists in PENDING_PAYMENT with exactly one OWNER
                    membership for this account.
                    POST-2: The Owner is on plan selection for the new farm (SCR-87).
                    POST-3: No subscription, payment request or service period is created; other owned
                    farms are unchanged.

Normal Flow            1. System shows Register Farm (SCR-11) as step 2 of onboarding.
                       2. Owner enters farm name, province, commune and optional detailed address,
                           contact person, contact phone, registration certificate information, expected
                           herd size and main breeds, and selects "Create farm and continue".
                       3. System validates the fields and the request key (GBR-88).
                       4. System creates, in one transaction, the farm in PENDING_PAYMENT with default
                           time zone Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh, its OWNER membership, the initial farm
                           parameter version (default values, UC-FE18-17) and the audit entry.
                       5. System shows MSG-FE02-01 and opens plan selection for the farm (UC-FE19-02,
                           SCR-87).

Alternative Flows   AF1 – "Later" on plan selection (step 5): the farm stays PENDING_PAYMENT on Select
                    Farm with "Pay to activate".
                    AF2 – Repeated submission or page reload (step 3): the farm already created is
                    returned; no second farm or membership.
                    AF3 – Back / cancel before step 2 ends: no farm is created.

Exceptions          E1: Owner registration not complete: MSG-FE02-02 and Complete Owner Profile opens;
                    no farm.
                    E2: Missing or invalid fields: MSG-COM-06 / MSG-COM-10; nothing is saved.
                    E3: Save failure: MSG-COM-02; no partial farm.

Priority            High

Frequency of Use    Once per farm.

Business Rules      GBR-05, GBR-12, GBR-13; BR-D04. One registration request creates at most one farm.

Other Information   Screens SCR-07, SCR-10, SCR-11, SCR-87. Related: UC-FE19-02.

Assumptions         Farm names need not be unique.



2.2 View and Select Authorized Farm Workspace
UC ID and Name      UC-FE02-02 — View and Select Authorized Farm Workspace

Created By          Ducnv                            Date Created             17/10/2026

Primary Actor       Farm Owner, Farm Manager,        Secondary Actors         None
                    Farm Technician




                                                                                                           105
Trigger             The user signs in with more than one farm, or opens the farm switcher.

Description         As a farm member, I want to see my farms with my role and their service status and
                    choose one, so that I work only with that farm's data.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The user is signed in and the account is ACTIVE.

Postconditions      POST-1: The selected farm is the current context with the user's role in that farm.
                    POST-2: No farm, membership or subscription is changed.

Normal Flow             1. User opens Select Farm (SCR-10).
                        2. System lists every farm where the user has an ACTIVE membership as a card:
                            farm name, location, service status, user's role, number of active cattle and
                            service end date. For an Owner it also lists owned farms in PENDING_PAYMENT,
                            EXPIRED or SUSPENDED.
                        3. User selects "Enter farm" on an ACTIVE farm.
                        4. System rechecks membership, role and service status (GBR-09, GBR-14).
                        5. System clears the data and filters of the previous farm, sets the new current farm
                            and opens its home (Farm Dashboard for Owner and Manager, Technician Home
                            on phone).

Alternative Flows   AF1 – Owner selects a PENDING_PAYMENT or EXPIRED farm: the card shows "Pay to
                    activate" / "Renew"; the System opens Farm Subscription (SCR-86) with MSG-FE02-03;
                    only farm information, subscription and support are available.
                    AF2 – Owner selects a SUSPENDED farm: the card shows "Send support ticket";
                    MSG-FE02-03.
                    AF3 – Manager or Technician card of a farm that is not ACTIVE: the card is disabled
                    with MSG-FE02-04.
                    AF4 – Only one available farm: it is opened directly.
                    AF5 – No farm: MSG-FE01-15; an Owner sees "Register new farm".

Exceptions          E1: Membership deactivated or role changed after the list was shown: MSG-COM-01
                    and the list is refreshed.
                    E2: Loading failure: MSG-COM-03.

Priority            High

Frequency of Use    At every sign-in; several times a day for multi-farm users.

Business Rules      GBR-09, GBR-12, GBR-13, GBR-14.

Other Information   Screens SCR-10, SCR-12, SCR-86.

Assumptions         —



2.3 Suspend or Restore Farm Service
UC ID and Name      UC-FE02-05 — Suspend or Restore Farm Service

Created By          Ducnv                             Date Created                17/10/2026

Primary Actor       Platform Manager                  Secondary Actors            None




                                                                                                          106
Trigger             The PM selects "Suspend" or "Restore" (Tạm ngưng / Khôi phục) on a farm in Farm
                    Service Overview.

Description         As a Platform Manager, I want to suspend a farm's service with a reason, or restore it,
                    so that the platform can stop misuse without changing payments.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The PM is signed in with ACTIVE PM access.
                    PRE-2: Suspend: the farm is not SUSPENDED. Restore: the farm is SUSPENDED.

Postconditions      POST-1: Suspend: the farm is SUSPENDED; members lose farm operations at their next
                    request; the Owner keeps farm information, subscription and support.
                    POST-2: Restore: the suspension is removed and the status is derived from the
                    subscription (ACTIVE, EXPIRED or PENDING_PAYMENT).
                    POST-3: A Farm Service Change and an audit entry are saved and the Owner is notified;
                    no payment, period or farm data changes.

Normal Flow             1. PM selects Suspend or Restore on the farm.
                        2. System shows Suspend / Restore Farm Service (SCR-14) with farm, Owner, current
                            status, purchased period and, for restore, the status after restore and the
                            previous suspension reason.
                        3. PM enters the reason and confirms (MSG-FE02-06 / MSG-FE02-07).
                        4. System rechecks PM access, farm status and version.
                        5. System saves the suspension flag change, the Farm Service Change and the audit
                            entry in one transaction and derives the new status (GBR-13, GBR-15).
                        6. System notifies the Owner (JOB-07) and shows MSG-FE02-08.

Alternative Flows   AF1 – Cancel: nothing changes.
                    AF2 – Restore without a current period: the status becomes EXPIRED or
                    PENDING_PAYMENT, not ACTIVE.

Exceptions          E1: No ACTIVE PM access: MSG-COM-01.
                    E2: Empty reason: MSG-COM-06.
                    E3: The farm is already in the requested state: MSG-FE02-09; no new record.
                    E4: Changed in another session: MSG-COM-05.
                    E5: Save failure: MSG-COM-02; status unchanged.
                    E6: Notification failure: the change is kept; the notice is retried without duplicates
                    (GBR-64).

Priority            High

Frequency of Use    Rare.

Business Rules      GBR-11, GBR-13, GBR-15, GBR-86; BR-D29.

Other Information   Screens SCR-13, SCR-14. Related: UC-FE20-13 (when requested by CS).

Assumptions         —




3. FE-03 User Role and Permission Management

3.1 Send Farm Member Invitation


                                                                                                             107
UC ID and Name      UC-FE03-01 — Send Farm Member Invitation

Created By          Ducnv                            Date Created              17/10/2026

Primary Actor       Farm Owner                       Secondary Actors          Email Delivery Service

Trigger             The Owner selects "Invite member" (Mời thành viên) on Members and Roles or Farm
                    Invitations.

Description         As a farm owner, I want to invite a person by email as Farm Manager or Farm
                    Technician, so that they can join my farm with the permissions of that role.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The Owner is signed in and owns the current farm.
                    PRE-2: The farm service is ACTIVE.

Postconditions      POST-1: One PENDING invitation bound to the farm, the email and the role exists with
                    its expiry time.
                    POST-2: The invitation email with a single-use link has been sent.
                    POST-3: No membership is created before acceptance.

Normal Flow            1. System shows Invite Member (SCR-16) with the farm name.
                       2. Owner enters the recipient email, selects the role Farm Manager or Farm
                           Technician and the validity (3, 7 or 14 days; default 7). The System shows the
                           fixed permissions of the selected role (GBR-06).
                       3. Owner selects "Send invitation".
                       4. System rechecks ownership and farm service, validates the email and checks that
                           the email is not an ACTIVE member and has no PENDING invitation for this farm.
                       5. System creates the PENDING invitation with expiry time and a link token stored as
                           a hash, and the audit entry.
                       6. System sends the invitation email (API-04) and shows MSG-FE03-01.

Alternative Flows   AF1 – A PENDING invitation for the email exists (step 4): MSG-FE03-03 offers Resend
                    (UC-FE03-03); no second invitation.
                    AF2 – The email belongs to an INACTIVE member (step 4): the System proposes
                    Reactivate in UC-FE03-06 instead.
                    AF3 – Cancel: nothing is created.
                    AF4 – Email delivery fails (step 6): the invitation stays PENDING; delivery is retried and
                    the Owner may resend.

Exceptions          E1: Not the Owner of the farm: MSG-COM-01.
                    E2: Farm not ACTIVE: MSG-FE03-04.
                    E3: Email empty or invalid: MSG-COM-06 / MSG-COM-10.
                    E4: Email is already an ACTIVE member: MSG-FE03-02.
                    E5: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.

Priority            High

Frequency of Use    Occasionally, when the farm adds staff.

Business Rules      GBR-06, GBR-14, GBR-16; BR-D05.

Other Information   Screens SCR-15, SCR-16, SCR-17. API-04.

Assumptions         Phone numbers are not used for invitations.




                                                                                                            108
3.2 Manage Pending Farm Invitation
UC ID and Name      UC-FE03-03 — Manage Pending Farm Invitation

Created By          Ducnv                            Date Created              17/10/2026

Primary Actor       Farm Owner                       Secondary Actors          Email Delivery Service

Trigger             The Owner selects "Resend" or "Cancel" (Gửi lại / Hủy) on a PENDING invitation in
                    Farm Invitations.

Description         As a farm owner, I want to resend or cancel a pending invitation, so that the right
                    person can join or a wrong invitation stops working.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The Owner is signed in and owns the farm.
                    PRE-2: The invitation is PENDING.
                    PRE-3: Resend only: the farm service is ACTIVE. Cancel is allowed in any service status.

Postconditions      POST-1: Resend: the same invitation is sent again and its validity restarts; email and role
                    are unchanged.
                    POST-2: Cancel: the invitation is CANCELLED and its link grants nothing.
                    POST-3: No membership is created.

Normal Flow             1. Owner selects Resend or Cancel.
                        2. For Cancel, the System asks for confirmation (MSG-FE03-06).
                        3. Owner confirms.
                        4. System rechecks ownership, invitation status and version (and farm service for
                            Resend).
                        5. Resend: System updates the last-sent and expiry times and sends the email again.
                            Cancel: System sets CANCELLED and saves the audit entry.
                        6. System shows MSG-FE03-05 or MSG-FE03-07 and refreshes the list.

Alternative Flows   AF1 – Owner closes the confirmation: nothing changes.
                    AF2 – Email delivery fails on Resend: the invitation remains PENDING with the new
                    validity; delivery is retried.

Exceptions          E1: Not the Owner: MSG-COM-01.
                    E2: Invitation already ACCEPTED, CANCELLED or EXPIRED: MSG-FE03-08 with the
                    current status.
                    E3: Resend while the farm is not ACTIVE: MSG-FE03-04.
                    E4: Changed in another session: MSG-COM-05.
                    E5: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.

Priority            Medium

Frequency of Use    Occasionally.

Business Rules      GBR-14, GBR-16.

Other Information   Screen SCR-17. API-04. JOB-02 expires invitations.

Assumptions         —




                                                                                                           109
3.3 Accept Farm Invitation
UC ID and Name      UC-FE03-04 — Accept Farm Invitation

Created By          Ducnv                            Date Created              17/10/2026

Primary Actor       Invited person (future Farm      Secondary Actors          Google Identity Provider
                    Manager or Farm Technician)                                (sign-in)

Trigger             The invited person opens the invitation link from the email.

Description         As an invited person, I want to review the farm and role and accept the invitation with
                    my invited email, so that I can start working in the farm.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The invitation is PENDING and unexpired.
                    PRE-2: The invited person can sign in (UC-FE01-02) or register an account with the
                    invited email.

Postconditions      POST-1: Exactly one ACTIVE membership exists for this account and farm with the
                    invited role.
                    POST-2: The invitation is ACCEPTED, the Owner is notified and the audit entry is saved.
                    POST-3: No Owner or platform authority is granted.

Normal Flow            1. Invited person opens the link.
                       2. System asks the person to sign in when no session exists (UC-FE01-02; a first
                           Google sign-in creates the account).
                       3. System checks the link, invitation status and expiry, farm service and that the
                           signed-in verified email equals the invited email.
                       4. System shows Accept Invitation (SCR-19): farm, role, invited by, sent and expiry
                           time, invited and signed-in email, and the fixed permissions of the role.
                       5. Invited person selects "Accept and enter farm".
                       6. System rechecks all conditions and membership uniqueness (GBR-17).
                       7. System creates the membership, sets the invitation ACCEPTED and saves the
                           audit entry in one transaction.
                       8. System notifies the Owner, shows MSG-FE03-10 and opens the farm.

Alternative Flows   AF1 – "Decide later": the invitation stays PENDING until it expires.
                    AF2 – Link opened again after acceptance: the existing membership is shown; no
                    second membership.
                    AF3 – No account yet and the person prefers email and password: UC-FE01-01 AF1 is
                    used with the invited email; the link is resumed after verification.

Exceptions          E1: Invitation EXPIRED, CANCELLED or already used, or link invalid: screen "Invitation no
                    longer valid" (MSG-FE03-08) with "Back to home" and the official contact.
                    E2: Signed-in email differs from the invited email: MSG-FE03-09; the farm details are
                    not shown.
                    E3: Farm not ACTIVE: MSG-FE03-04; the invitation stays PENDING.
                    E4: Account SUSPENDED: sign-in is refused (UC-FE01-02 E3).
                    E5: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.

Priority            High

Frequency of Use    Once per invited member.




                                                                                                            110
Business Rules      GBR-14, GBR-16, GBR-17; BR-D04.

Other Information   Screen SCR-19. API-05.

Assumptions         —

3.5 Suspend or Restore Platform User Account
UC ID and Name      UC-FE03-08 — Suspend or Restore Platform User Account

Created By          Ducnv                            Date Created             17/10/2026

Primary Actor       System Admin                     Secondary Actors         None

Trigger             The SA selects "Suspend account" or "Restore account" on an account in User Accounts.


Description         As a System Admin, I want to suspend or restore a user account with a reason, so that a
                    compromised or misused account cannot sign in.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The SA is signed in with ACTIVE SA access.
                    PRE-2: Suspend: the account is ACTIVE and is not the SA's own account. Restore: the
                    account is SUSPENDED.

Postconditions      POST-1: Suspend: the account is SUSPENDED; every sign-in method is blocked and
                    existing sessions are rejected at the next request.
                    POST-2: Restore: the account is ACTIVE; revoked roles or memberships are not restored.
                    POST-3: History and audit are saved; at least one ACTIVE SA remains.

Normal Flow             1. SA opens the account detail on User Accounts (SCR-20) and selects Suspend or
                            Restore.
                        2. System asks for the reason and confirmation (MSG-FE03-14 / MSG-FE03-15).
                        3. SA enters the reason and confirms.
                        4. System rechecks SA access, target status, version and that at least one ACTIVE SA
                            remains (GBR-10).
                        5. System updates the account status and saves history and audit in one transaction.
                        6. System shows MSG-FE03-16. When a suspended account is the assigned CS of
                            tickets, those tickets return to the unassigned queue (GBR-77); OPEN internal
                            requests assigned to it are flagged for reassignment.

Alternative Flows   AF1 – Cancel: nothing changes.




                                                                                                          112
Exceptions          E1: No ACTIVE SA access: MSG-COM-01.
                    E2: Empty reason: MSG-COM-06.
                    E3: Suspending the last ACTIVE SA or own account: MSG-FE03-17.
                    E4: Already in the requested status: MSG-FE03-20.
                    E5: Changed in another session: MSG-COM-05.
                    E6: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.

Priority            Medium

Frequency of Use    Rare.

Business Rules      GBR-04, GBR-10, GBR-77, GBR-86; BR-D06.

Other Information   Screen SCR-20. Related: UC-FE20-13 (when requested by CS).

                    and audit.

                            role and status and the role history.

                            one-ACTIVE-role rule and the last-SA rule.

                        6. System shows MSG-FE03-19. Tickets assigned to a CS who loses CS access return

                    E6: Changed in another session: MSG-COM-05.

4. FE-04 Pen Management

4.1 Reassign Cattle to Another Pen
UC ID and Name      UC-FE04-05 — Reassign Cattle to Another Pen

Created By          G35 Team                         Date Created             21/10/2026

Primary Actor       Farm Owner, Farm Manager         Secondary Actors         None

Trigger             The user selects "Move pen" (Chuyển chuồng) on a cattle profile (Pen History tab), on
                    cattle selected in a pen's detail, or from a pen-move suggestion (REM-08).

Description         As a farm manager, I want to move one or several cattle to another pen with a date and
                    reason, so that pen occupancy and each animal's pen history stay correct.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The user is the Owner or a Manager of an ACTIVE farm.
                    PRE-2: The selected cattle are ACTIVE (not EXITED, not draft calves).

Postconditions      POST-1: For each moved cattle the current assignment is closed at the move date and a
                    new assignment is open in the destination pen.
                    POST-2: Occupancy of both pens is updated; warning reasons are stored with the
                    assignment.
                    POST-3: A related REM-08 suggestion is resolved.




                                                                                                       114
Normal Flow             1. System shows Move Pen (SCR-25) with the selected cattle (code, breed, sex, age,
                            latest weight) and the current pen with occupancy.
                        2. User selects the destination pen (list shows only ACTIVE pens with
                            headcount/capacity), the move date (default today) and the reason; the
                            performer defaults to the user.
                        3. System runs the reception check for each cattle and shows the result table: pen
                            ACTIVE and has enough free places; purpose type, life stage, sex and minimum
                            age/weight against the destination criteria (GBR-21).
                        4. User selects "Confirm move".
                        5. System rechecks pen status, free places, each cattle's state and versions.
                        6. System closes the current assignments and opens the new ones in one
                            transaction (GBR-22), updates occupancy and pen history and resolves REM-08.
                        7. System shows MSG-FE04-08.

Alternative Flows   AF1 – Criteria not met (step 3): the result shows "Not met" for the failing criteria and
                    MSG-FE04-06; the user must enter a warning reason to continue; the reason is saved
                    with each affected assignment.
                    AF2 – Correction of a mistaken move: user selects the previous pen with reason
                    "Correction"; the system records a reverse move.
                    AF3 – Cancel: nothing changes.

Exceptions          E1: Destination INACTIVE or not enough free places: MSG-FE04-05; the move is
                    blocked.
                    E2: Move date before the start of the current assignment or in the future:
                    MSG-FE04-09.
                    E3: Cattle already in the destination pen: MSG-FE04-07; that cattle is skipped.
                    E4: Cattle EXITED or changed meanwhile: MSG-COM-13 / MSG-COM-05.
                    E5: Save failure: MSG-COM-02; no assignment changes.

Priority            High

Frequency of Use    Several times a week.

Business Rules      GBR-20, GBR-21, GBR-22, GBR-26; BR-D09.

Other Information   Screens SCR-22, SCR-25, SCR-28. Related: UC-FE04-01, UC-FE14-01.

Assumptions         —




5. FE-05 Cattle Profile, Identification and State

5.1 Update Cattle Profile
UC ID and Name      UC-FE05-03 — Update Cattle Profile

Created By          G35 Team                         Date Created              21/10/2026

Primary Actor       Farm Owner, Farm Manager         Secondary Actors          None

Trigger             The user selects "Update profile" (Cập nhật hồ sơ) on a cattle profile, or opens a
                    life-stage suggestion (REM-08) from Farm Reminders or the profile.



                                                                                                           115
Description         As a farm owner or manager, I want to change a cattle's life stage, production purpose,
                    ear tag or descriptive data with an effective date and reason, so that the profile is
                    correct and its history explains every change.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The user is the Owner or a Manager of an ACTIVE farm.
                    PRE-2: The cattle is ACTIVE.

Postconditions      POST-1: The changed values are current and a Cattle Profile Change with old/new value,
                    effective date, reason and actor is saved for each changed group.
                    POST-2: The herd group and the applicability of reproductive status are re-derived from
                    the new stage or purpose type (GBR-24, GBR-54); pen, health state and reproduction
                    events are unchanged.
                    POST-3: A related REM-08 life-stage suggestion is resolved.

Normal Flow            1. System shows Update Cattle Profile (SCR-30) with tabs Identification &
                           description, Production purpose, Life stage.
                       2. User selects a tab and enters the new values: life stage (new stage from
                           UNWEANED_CALF, WEANED_CALF, YOUNG_STOCK, ADULT, effective date,
                           reason); or secondary ear tag, name, colour/markings and notes; or production
                           purpose (new purpose with its purpose type shown, effective date, reason –
                           Owner only).
                       3. User selects Save.
                       4. System validates the values, uniqueness of the ear tag among ACTIVE cattle,
                           catalogue availability, the effective date (not in the future and not before herd
                           entry), the user's right to change the group (GBR-25) and the version.
                       5. System saves the new values and the change history in one transaction.
                       6. System re-derives the herd group and reproduction applicability, re-evaluates
                           reminders that depend on purpose or stage (REM-08) and shows MSG-FE05-01.

Alternative Flows   AF1 – Manager opens the profile: the Production purpose tab is read-only with
                    MSG-FE05-03.
                    AF2 – From a life-stage suggestion: the Life stage tab opens with the suggested stage,
                    the effective date (the date the threshold was reached, or the calving date) and the
                    reason proposed; the user confirms or edits them.
                    AF3 – Stage change that ends reproduction follow-up: when the new stage or purpose
                    type makes reproductive status "Not applicable" while the female has no OPEN
                    pregnancy, MSG-FE05-11 is shown before saving; reproduction history is kept.
                    AF4 – Cancel: nothing changes.

Exceptions          E1: Ear tag used by another ACTIVE cattle: MSG-FE05-02.
                    E2: Inactive catalogue value: MSG-FE18-08.
                    E3: Missing reason or effective date, or invalid date: MSG-COM-06 / MSG-COM-07 /
                    MSG-COM-15.
                    E4: Cattle EXITED: MSG-COM-13.
                    E5: Changed in another session: MSG-COM-05.
                    E6: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.

Priority            Medium

Frequency of Use    Weekly.




                                                                                                           116
Business Rules      GBR-23, GBR-24, GBR-25, GBR-26, GBR-54; BR-D08.

Other Information   Screens SCR-27, SCR-30, SCR-73.

5.2 Issue or Replace Cattle QR Tag
UC ID and Name      UC-FE05-04 — Issue or Replace Cattle QR Tag

Created By          G35 Team                          Date Created             21/10/2026

Primary Actor       Farm Owner, Farm Manager          Secondary Actors         None

Trigger             The user selects "Reissue QR" (Cấp lại QR) on a cattle profile, or chooses "Issue later" at
                    herd entry and issues the tag afterwards.

Description         As a farm owner or manager, I want to issue or replace an animal's QR tag and print it,
                    so that the animal can always be identified in the field without changing its identity.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The user is the Owner or a Manager of an ACTIVE farm.
                    PRE-2: The cattle is ACTIVE.

Postconditions      POST-1: The cattle has exactly one ACTIVE tag; a replaced tag is REPLACED with reason.
                    POST-2: Internal Cattle ID, display code and history are unchanged.

Normal Flow             1. System shows Issue / Replace QR Tag (SCR-31) with the current tag (code, status,
                            issue date) and a newly generated tag code.
                        2. User selects the replacement reason (LOST, DAMAGED, OTHER) and an optional
                            note.
                        3. User selects "Print test tag" to check the printout (optional) and then "Confirm
                            new tag".
                        4. System checks the cattle state and that the new code is unique.
                        5. System marks the old tag REPLACED, activates the new tag and saves the tag
                            history in one transaction (GBR-27).
                        6. System shows MSG-FE05-04 and offers printing (display code above the QR,
                            OR-02).

Alternative Flows   AF1 – First issue (no ACTIVE tag): no replacement reason is asked.
                    AF2 – Cancel: nothing changes.

Exceptions          E1: Cattle EXITED: MSG-COM-13.
                    E2: Printing fails after saving: MSG-FE05-08; the tag remains ACTIVE and can be printed
                    again from the QR Tag tab.
                    E3: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.

Priority            Medium

Frequency of Use    Rare per animal.

Business Rules      GBR-23, GBR-27; BR-D07, BR-D08.

Other Information   Screens SCR-29, SCR-31. OR-02, OR-03.

Assumptions         Tags are printed on a standard printer or label printer connected to the user's device.

5.3 Scan Cattle QR Code
UC ID and Name      UC-FE05-05 — Scan Cattle QR Code

Created By          G35 Team                         Date Created              21/10/2026

Primary Actor       Farm Owner, Farm Manager,        Secondary Actors          None
                    Farm Technician

Trigger             The user selects "Scan QR" (Quét QR) in the top bar or bottom navigation.

Description         As a farm member at the pen, I want to scan an animal's ear tag with my phone, so that
                    I open the right profile without searching long lists.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The user is signed in and has an ACTIVE membership in an ACTIVE farm.

Postconditions      POST-1: The profile of the identified cattle is opened within the current farm, or a safe
                    message is shown; no data changes.

Normal Flow            1. System opens Scan QR (SCR-32) and asks for camera permission when needed.
                       2. User points the camera at the tag.
                       3. System reads the code and resolves it to a cattle (GBR-28).
                       4. System checks that the cattle belongs to the current farm and that the user may
                           view it.
                       5. System shows the identified cattle card (code, ear tag, breed, sex, age, pen, herd
                           status, health state and reproductive status) with "Open profile" and "Scan
                           again".
                       6. User selects Open profile; System opens the Cattle Profile (SCR-27) showing only
                           the actions allowed to the role.

Alternative Flows   AF1 – Manual entry: user selects "Enter code manually" and types a display code, ear
                    tag or tag code; the System continues at step 3.
                    AF2 – Replaced tag: the cattle is resolved with MSG-FE05-05.
                    AF3 – Cattle of another farm the user can access: MSG-FE05-06 asks to switch farm; on
                    Yes the farm is switched (UC-FE02-02) and the profile opens.
                    AF4 – EXITED cattle: the profile opens read-only with MSG-COM-13.

Exceptions          E1: Camera unavailable or permission denied: MSG-FE05-07 and manual entry.
                    E2: Unknown code, payment VietQR or cattle of a farm the user cannot access:
                    MSG-FE05-09; no cattle data is shown.
                    E3: Tag code of a draft calf: MSG-FE05-10; the draft is available to the Owner and
                    Managers in Pending Calves.

Priority            High

Frequency of Use    Many times a day.

Business Rules      GBR-26, GBR-27, GBR-28; NFR-P02.

Other Information   Screens SCR-32, SCR-27. OR-02, OR-03.

Assumptions         The browser supports camera access over HTTPS.

6. FE-06 Herd Entry Management

6.1 Register Herd Entry Voucher
UC ID and Name    UC-FE06-01 — Register Herd Entry Voucher

Created By        G35 Team                        Date Created              22/10/2026

Primary Actor     Farm Owner, Farm Manager        Secondary Actors          None

Trigger           The user selects "Create entry voucher" (Tạo phiếu nhập) or "Continue" (Tiếp tục) on a
                  DRAFT voucher in Herd Entry Vouchers, "Pending calves" (Bê chờ xác nhận) or
                  reminder REM-09, or "Register now" while recording a calving (UC-FE12-04).

Description       As a farm owner or manager, I want to record every receipt of cattle as one voucher
                  with its cattle, value and payment, so that each animal gets a permanent identity and a
                  complete record from day one and the farm knows what it owes the seller.

Preconditions     PRE-1: The user is the Owner or a Manager of an ACTIVE farm.
                  PRE-2: At least one ACTIVE breed, production purpose and pen exist.

Postconditions    POST-1: The voucher is POSTED with its code, lines, totals and initial payment; for each
                  line one cattle exists with a new Internal Cattle ID and display code, its herd entry,
                  history declarations, first pen assignment and ACTIVE QR tag.
                  POST-2: For farm-born calves, each calf outcome is REGISTERED and linked to its cattle;
                  REM-09 is resolved.
                  POST-3: Matching vaccination schedules are listed; no dose is created automatically.
                  POST-4: Until posting, a DRAFT voucher has no effect on cattle, pens, payments or
                  reminders.




                                                                                                         119
Normal Flow            1. System shows Herd Entry Voucher – Purchase (SCR-34) with the next voucher
                           code, entry type Purchase and today as entry date.
                       2. User enters the voucher header: entry date, seller (chosen from the trading
                           partners or added inline), quarantine certificate number, vehicle plate and note.
                       3. User chooses the pricing basis (per kg or per head) and selects "+ Add cattle";
                           System opens Voucher Cattle Line (SCR-37).
                       4. User enters secondary ear tag (or leaves it empty to tag later), name, breed, sex,
                           birth date or month with accuracy, life stage (proposed from the age at entry,
                           GBR-25), production purpose, entry weight with basis, receiving pen and unit
                           price, and the pre-entry history (vaccination, disease, reproduction: KNOWN /
                           NONE / UNKNOWN, default UNKNOWN, GBR-30); selects "Save line". Steps 3–4
                           repeat for each cattle.
                       5. System shows the lines with line amounts, totals (cattle, weight, amount,
                           estimated weights marked UT), the occupancy change of each receiving pen and
                           the pre-save checks (ear tags unique, pen criteria and capacity, entry date not in
                           the future and not before birth dates).
                       6. User attaches documents (quarantine certificate, invoice, photos) and enters the
                           transport fee, amount paid, payment method and due date of the balance;
                           System shows total payable and outstanding (GBR-91).
                       7. User selects "Save & create n cattle" (Lưu & tạo hồ sơ n con), optionally with
                           "Print QR tags after saving".
                       8. System rechecks every line, the header and the version and, in one transaction,
                           posts the voucher, creates each cattle with its herd entry, history declarations
                           (known vaccinations as HISTORICAL vaccination records), first pen assignment
                           and QR tag, records the initial payment and saves the audit entry (GBR-90,
                           GBR-32).
                       9. System shows MSG-FE06-01, opens Herd Entry Voucher Detail (SCR-106) and,
                           when schedules match, MSG-FE06-05 with links to add the cattle to them
                           (UC-FE09-03).

Alternative Flows   AF1 – Farm-born calves (type "Farm-born calves" or trigger from pending calves /
                    REM-09): System shows SCR-36 with the PENDING_CONFIRMATION calves; dam, sire,
                    breed, sex, birth date and birth weight are read-only (GBR-31). User selects the calves,
                    checks the tag, receiving pen and production purpose of each (default: the first ACTIVE
                    purpose of type Undecided) and continues at step 7; the entry date of each calf is its
                    birth date, its life stage UNWEANED_CALF and the voucher has no amounts (GBR-93).
                    AF2 – Register now from a calving (UC-FE12-04): System creates and posts a
                    FARM_BORN voucher for the calf in the calving transaction.
                    AF3 – Import lines from Excel (step 3): User selects "Import from Excel" and uploads
                    the template; System opens Import Cattle from Excel (SCR-105) with the result of each
                    row (valid, warning, error, GBR-96); User selects "Add n cattle to the voucher"; only
                    valid rows are added (MSG-FE06-18, MSG-FE06-19).
                    AF4 – Save draft (any step): System saves the voucher as DRAFT (MSG-FE06-10); no
                    cattle is created; the Owner and Managers can continue it later.
                    AF5 – Edit or remove a line (step 5): User opens the line in SCR-37 and saves or
                    removes it.
                    AF6 – Pen warnings: a criteria warning needs a warning reason (MSG-FE04-06); a pen
                    that becomes full is shown with MSG-FE06-17.




                                                                                                            120
                    AF7 – Mark a calf as died before registration (AF1): User enters the reason; the calf
                    outcome is closed and REM-09 resolved.
                    AF8 – Discard draft / Cancel: User discards a DRAFT voucher or leaves without saving
                    (MSG-COM-14).

Exceptions          E1: No cattle line: MSG-FE06-16.
                    E2: Entry date in the future or before a birth date: MSG-FE06-03.
                    E3: Ear tag or tag code already used: MSG-FE05-02 / MSG-FE12-08.
                    E4: Pen INACTIVE or without enough places for its lines: MSG-FE04-05.
                    E5: Calf already registered or not LIVE: MSG-FE06-02 / MSG-FE06-04.
                    E6: Amount paid greater than the total payable: MSG-FE06-13.
                    E7: Missing seller, quarantine certificate (purchase) or other required field:
                    MSG-COM-06.
                    E8: Inactive catalogue value: MSG-FE18-08.
                    E9: Save failure: MSG-COM-02; nothing is created and the voucher stays DRAFT.

Priority            High

Frequency of Use    Weekly; several times during calving season.

Business Rules      GBR-20, GBR-21, GBR-25, GBR-29, GBR-30, GBR-31, GBR-32, GBR-58, GBR-90, GBR-91,
                    GBR-92, GBR-93, GBR-96, GBR-97; BR-D07, BR-D08, BR-D10, BR-D34, BR-D39.

Other Information   Screens SCR-33, SCR-34, SCR-36, SCR-37, SCR-105, SCR-106. Related: UC-FE06-04,
                    UC-FE12-04, UC-FE09-03, UC-FE05-04.

Assumptions         A voucher is entered by one user at a time; the voucher version prevents silent
                    overwrites.



6.2 Correct Herd Entry Record
UC ID and Name      UC-FE06-03 — Correct Herd Entry Record

Created By          G35 Team                         Date Created              22/10/2026

Primary Actor       Farm Owner, Farm Manager         Secondary Actors          None

Trigger             The user selects "Correct herd entry" (Sửa bản ghi nhập đàn) on the cattle profile or on
                    a cattle line of Herd Entry Voucher Detail (SCR-106).

Description         As a farm owner or manager, I want to correct wrong entry data of one cattle, or void
                    its entry when it was recorded by mistake, so that the herd record and the voucher are
                    accurate while the original values remain traceable.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The user is the Owner or a Manager of an ACTIVE farm.
                    PRE-2: The cattle is ACTIVE and its herd entry is VALID on a POSTED voucher.

Postconditions      POST-1: Correct: the corrected values are current, voucher totals are recalculated when
                    the weight or price changed, and a Record Revision keeps the old values, reason, actor
                    and time; the Internal Cattle ID is unchanged.
                    POST-2: Void: the herd entry is VOIDED, the cattle gets herd status VOIDED and is
                    removed from active lists and counts, its pen assignment is closed and its tag becomes




                                                                                                            121
                    RETIRED, its line amount is removed from the voucher total and its identifiers are never
                    reused.

Normal Flow             1. System shows Correct / Void Herd Entry (SCR-35) with the entry (cattle, voucher,
                            entry date, recorder) and the action choice "Correct record" / "Void record".
                        2. User keeps "Correct record" and enters new values for the fields to correct: entry
                            date (for a purchase voucher it applies to all its cattle), entry weight and basis,
                            unit price, breed, sex, birth date and accuracy, history declarations.
                        3. User enters the reason and selects Save.
                        4. System validates the new values against later events (entry date not after any
                            recorded event of the cattle concerned; sex not changed when the cattle has
                            reproductive events; birth date not after the entry date), the voucher payments
                            (BR-D39) and the version.
                        5. System saves the corrected values, the recalculated voucher totals and the Record
                            Revision in one transaction (GBR-33) and shows MSG-FE06-06.

Alternative Flows   AF1 – Void record (step 2): System checks that the cattle has no recorded event other
                    than its herd entry and first pen placement; user enters the reason and confirms
                    MSG-FE06-08; System voids the entry, closes the pen assignment and tag, recalculates
                    the voucher and shows MSG-FE06-12. When it is the last VALID line, the user is directed
                    to UC-FE06-05.
                    AF2 – Paid amount above the new total: the change is saved and MSG-FE06-23 is
                    shown; the difference is settled outside BeefCare.
                    AF3 – Cancel: nothing changes.

Exceptions          E1: Correction breaks the date order or changes the sex of a cattle with reproductive
                    events: MSG-FE06-07.
                    E2: Void not allowed because events exist: MSG-FE06-09.
                    E3: Missing reason: MSG-COM-06.
                    E4: Changed in another session: MSG-COM-05.
                    E5: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.

Priority            Medium

Frequency of Use    Rare.

Business Rules      GBR-33, GBR-91; BR-D07, BR-D15, BR-D39.

Other Information   Screens SCR-35, SCR-106. Related: UC-FE06-05, UC-FE05-03.

Assumptions         —



6.3 Void Herd Entry Voucher
UC ID and Name      UC-FE06-05 — Void Herd Entry Voucher

Created By          G35 Team                          Date Created              22/10/2026

Primary Actor       Farm Owner, Farm Manager          Secondary Actors          None

Trigger             The user selects "Void voucher" (Hủy phiếu) on Herd Entry Voucher Detail (SCR-106).




                                                                                                            122
Description         As a farm owner or manager, I want to void a whole entry voucher recorded by mistake,
                    so that cattle that never entered the herd disappear from active lists while the mistake
                    stays traceable.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The user is the Owner or a Manager of an ACTIVE farm.
                    PRE-2: The voucher is POSTED.

Postconditions      POST-1: The voucher is VOIDED with reason, actor and time; every VALID herd entry of
                    the voucher is VOIDED, each cattle gets herd status VOIDED and is removed from active
                    lists, its pen assignment is closed and its QR tag withdrawn; identifiers are never
                    reused.
                    POST-2: Recorded payments stay in the voucher history; farm-born calves return to
                    PENDING_CONFIRMATION and REM-09 is raised again.

Normal Flow            1. System shows Void Herd Entry Voucher (SCR-108) with the voucher summary and,
                           for each cattle, the events recorded after entry and the result "Can be voided" or
                           "Blocked".
                       2. System lists the effects of voiding (cattle voided, pen places released, tags
                           withdrawn, payments kept).
                       3. User enters the void reason and selects "Void voucher".
                       4. System rechecks that no cattle of the voucher has an event other than its herd
                           entry and first pen placement (GBR-33), the voucher state and version.
                       5. System voids the voucher and its entries in one transaction, saves the audit entry
                           and shows MSG-FE06-22.

Alternative Flows   AF1 – Some cattle have other events: the void button is disabled and MSG-FE06-21
                    names them; the user corrects or voids those entries one by one (UC-FE06-03).
                    AF2 – Close the dialog: nothing changes.

Exceptions          E1: Missing reason: MSG-COM-06.
                    E2: Voucher already VOIDED or changed: MSG-COM-05.
                    E3: Save failure: MSG-COM-02; nothing changes.

Priority            Low

Frequency of Use    Rare.

Business Rules      GBR-33, GBR-90, GBR-91; BR-D07, BR-D15.

Other Information   Screens SCR-106, SCR-108. Confirmation MSG-FE06-20.

Assumptions         Money already paid to the seller is recovered outside BeefCare.




7. FE-07 Farm Announcements

7.1 Update Farm Announcement
UC ID and Name      UC-FE07-02 — Update Farm Announcement

Created By          G35 Team                         Date Created              21/10/2026




                                                                                                          123
Primary Actor       Farm Owner, Farm Manager          Secondary Actors         None
                    (author)

Trigger             The author selects "Edit" on an own draft, or "Correct" (Đính chính) on an own
                    published announcement.

Description         As the author of an announcement, I want to edit my draft or publish a correction of my
                    published announcement, so that recipients always have accurate information and
                    earlier versions remain traceable.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The user is the author, still an ACTIVE Owner or Manager of the ACTIVE farm.
                    PRE-2: The announcement is the author's DRAFT, or the author's PUBLISHED
                    announcement.

Postconditions      POST-1: Draft edit: the draft content and audience are updated; nothing is sent.
                    POST-2: Correction: a new immutable version with the correction reason is published,
                    the current-version pointer moves to it and it is delivered to the recipients of the
                    original publication who are still ACTIVE members.

Normal Flow             1. Author opens Announcement Correction (SCR-41) on a published announcement;
                            the System shows the current version with title and body to edit and the version
                            history.
                        2. Author edits title and/or body and enters the correction reason.
                        3. Author selects "Publish version n".
                        4. System validates non-blank content and reason, authorship and version (GBR-61).
                        5. System saves the new version, the recipient snapshot (original recipients still
                            ACTIVE) and the pointer in one transaction and creates the notifications
                            (GBR-62).
                        6. System shows MSG-FE07-04.

Alternative Flows   AF1 – Edit a draft: the author opens the draft in the Announcement Editor (SCR-39),
                    changes title, body or recipients and selects "Save draft"; the System revalidates the
                    audience (GBR-60) and shows MSG-FE07-01; nothing is delivered.
                    AF2 – Cancel: nothing changes.

Exceptions          E1: Not the author: MSG-COM-01.
                    E2: Blank title, body or reason: MSG-COM-06.
                    E3: Recipient not allowed for the author's role: MSG-FE07-02.
                    E4: Changed in another session: MSG-COM-05.
                    E5: Save failure: MSG-COM-02; no version is published.

Priority            Medium

Frequency of Use    Weekly.

Business Rules      GBR-60, GBR-61, GBR-62; BR-D21.

Other Information   Screens SCR-38, SCR-39, SCR-41.

Assumptions         —



7.2 Publish Farm Announcement



                                                                                                             124
UC ID and Name      UC-FE07-03 — Publish Farm Announcement

Created By          G35 Team                         Date Created             21/10/2026

Primary Actor       Farm Owner, Farm Manager         Secondary Actors         None
                    (author)

Trigger             The author selects "Review & publish" (Xem lại & đăng) in the Announcement Editor.

Description         As a farm owner or manager, I want to review and publish my announcement once, so
                    that the intended staff receive it as a fixed version.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The user is an ACTIVE Owner or Manager of the ACTIVE farm.
                    PRE-2: The announcement is the user's saved DRAFT with non-blank title and body and
                    at least one recipient selector.

Postconditions      POST-1: The announcement is PUBLISHED with version 1, the resolved recipient
                    snapshot and one delivery per recipient.
                    POST-2: Recipients see the announcement and a notification; members who join later
                    do not receive it.

Normal Flow             1. System shows Review and Publish (SCR-40): title, body, author and role, audience,
                            resolved recipients (count and names) and farm.
                        2. Author selects "Publish announcement".
                        3. System resolves the current ACTIVE members for the selectors, removes
                            recipients not allowed for the author's role and duplicates (GBR-60), and checks
                            authorship, state and version.
                        4. System saves version 1, the recipient snapshot, the deliveries and the PUBLISHED
                            state in one transaction (GBR-62).
                        5. System creates the notifications (JOB-07) and shows MSG-FE07-03.

Alternative Flows   AF1 – "Back to edit": the draft is unchanged and the editor reopens.

Exceptions          E1: No eligible recipient remains: MSG-FE07-05; the draft stays DRAFT.
                    E2: Selected named recipient no longer eligible: MSG-FE07-02.
                    E3: Already published or changed in another session: MSG-COM-05.
                    E4: Save failure: MSG-COM-02; nothing is delivered.

Priority            Medium

Frequency of Use    Weekly.

Business Rules      GBR-60, GBR-61, GBR-62, GBR-64; BR-D21.

Other Information   Screens SCR-39, SCR-40, SCR-38, SCR-104.

8. FE-08 Growth and BCS Monitoring

8.1 Record Cattle Growth Assessment
UC ID and Name      UC-FE08-01 — Record Cattle Growth Assessment



                                                                                                         125
Created By          G35 Team                          Date Created              21/10/2026

Primary Actor       Farm Manager                      Secondary Actors          None

Trigger             The Manager selects "Record weight & BCS" (Ghi nhận cân & BCS) on the Growth & BCS
                    tab of a cattle profile, or from a weighing reminder (REM-01).

Description         As a farm manager, I want to record an animal's weight and/or body condition score
                    with how it was measured, so that growth can be followed honestly.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The user is a Manager of an ACTIVE farm.
                    PRE-2: The cattle is ACTIVE and registered (not a draft calf).

Postconditions      POST-1: A VALID growth assessment is saved with measured time, entry time and
                    recorder.
                    POST-2: Current weight, ADG and growth reminders are recalculated; REM-01 is
                    resolved and a pen-move or life-stage suggestion (REM-08) may be raised.

Normal Flow            1. System shows Record Weight and BCS (SCR-44) with the cattle (read-only),
                           measured time (default now) and measurer (default the user).
                       2. Manager enters the weight in kg and the method (electronic scale, measuring
                           tape, other estimate); the basis is set from the method (MEASURED for scale,
                           ESTIMATED otherwise) (GBR-38).
                       3. Manager selects a BCS value on the farm scale and/or enters a note, then selects
                           "Save assessment".
                       4. System validates: weight or BCS present, weight > 0, BCS on the scale, measured
                           time not in the future and not before herd entry, cattle state.
                       5. System saves the assessment and the timeline entry.
                       6. System recalculates current weight and ADG (GBR-39), resolves REM-01,
                           re-evaluates REM-08 (JOB-01) and shows MSG-FE08-01.

Alternative Flows   AF1 – Large difference from the last weight: when the new weight differs by more than
                    20 % from the latest valid weight within 30 days, MSG-FE08-04 asks the Manager to
                    confirm before saving.
                    AF2 – BCS only: no weight fields are required.
                    AF3 – Measured by a Technician: the Manager selects the Technician as measurer; the
                    Manager remains the recorder.
                    AF4 – Cancel: nothing is saved.

Exceptions          E1: Neither weight nor BCS: MSG-FE08-02.
                    E2: Weight not positive: MSG-FE08-03.
                    E3: Measured time in the future or before herd entry: MSG-COM-07 / MSG-COM-15.
                    E4: Cattle EXITED: MSG-COM-13.
                    E5: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.

Priority            High

Frequency of Use    Daily on the farm; monthly per animal.

Business Rules      GBR-08, GBR-26, GBR-38, GBR-39; BR-D14, BR-D16, BR-D20.

Other Information   Screens SCR-42, SCR-43, SCR-44. Related: UC-FE14-01.

Assumptions         The farm BCS scale is configured in UC-FE18-17.
