9. FE-09 Vaccination Schedule and Recording

9.1 Create Vaccination Schedule
UC ID and Name      UC-FE09-02 — Create Vaccination Schedule

Created By          G35 Team                          Date Created               21/10/2026

Primary Actor       Farm Manager                      Secondary Actors           None

Trigger             The Manager selects "Manage schedule" → "New schedule" (Quản lý lịch) on
                    Vaccination Schedule.

Description         As a farm manager, I want to plan a vaccine for a defined group of cattle with its doses
                    and booster, so that every planned dose is reminded and tracked per animal.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The user is a Manager of an ACTIVE farm.
                    PRE-2: The vaccine exists and is ACTIVE in the farm catalogue.

Postconditions      POST-1: An ACTIVE schedule exists with one PLANNED dose per target cattle and dose
                    number.
                    POST-2: Vaccination reminders (REM-06) are evaluated for the new doses.

Normal Flow            1. System shows Manage Vaccination Schedule (SCR-47) for a new schedule.
                       2. Manager enters the schedule name, selects the vaccine (the reference disease,
                           dose, route and withdrawal are shown), the start date, the number of doses, the
                           days between doses and the optional booster interval in months.
                       3. Manager selects the target: one or more pens, herd groups and life stages
                           (GBR-24); the System shows the number of matching ACTIVE cattle and the list,
                           from which the Manager may remove or add individual cattle.
                       4. System shows the planned doses table (dose number, planned date, number of
                           cattle).
                       5. Manager selects "Save schedule".
                       6. System validates the fields, the vaccine availability, the target (at least one cattle)
                           and name uniqueness among ACTIVE schedules.
                       7. System saves the schedule and the PLANNED doses in one transaction (GBR-41),
                           triggers JOB-01 and shows MSG-FE09-01.

Alternative Flows   AF1 – Cattle already received the same vaccine dose recently: the System marks them
                    in the list; the Manager decides to keep or remove them.
                    AF2 – Cancel: nothing is saved.
                    AF3 – Booster: when the last dose of the series (or a booster) is recorded, the System
                    plans the next booster at administered date + booster interval while the schedule is
                    ACTIVE.

Exceptions          E1: Missing required field or no cattle match the target: MSG-COM-06 / MSG-FE09-07.
                    E2: Vaccine inactive: MSG-FE18-08.
                    E3: Name already used by an ACTIVE schedule: MSG-FE09-10.
                    E4: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.

Priority            High

Frequency of Use    Several times a year.




                                                                                                              128
Business Rules      GBR-08, GBR-41, GBR-43; BR-D17.

Other Information   Screens SCR-46, SCR-47. Related: UC-FE09-03, UC-FE09-04, UC-FE14-01.

Assumptions         New cattle that later match the target are added only by the Manager (GBR-32).



9.2 Record Vaccination
UC ID and Name      UC-FE09-05 — Record Vaccination

Created By          G35 Team                        Date Created              21/10/2026

Primary Actor       Farm Manager                    Secondary Actors          None

Trigger             The Manager selects "Record vaccination" (Ghi nhận tiêm) on Vaccination Schedule (for
                    selected doses) or on the Vaccination tab of a cattle profile.

Description         As a farm manager, I want to record the vaccinations actually given, for one animal or a
                    batch, so that each animal's vaccination history and schedule status are correct.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The user is a Manager of an ACTIVE farm.
                    PRE-2: The selected cattle are ACTIVE and registered.

Postconditions      POST-1: One VALID vaccination record exists per vaccinated cattle; matching scheduled
                    doses are DONE and next doses (and boosters) are planned.
                    POST-2: Cattle marked "Defer" have DEFERRED doses with reason and new date.
                    POST-3: Reminders are updated (REM-06).
                    POST-4: For on-farm vaccination with withdrawal days above 0, each vaccinated cattle's
                    meat withdrawal end date is updated (GBR-46).

Normal Flow            1. System shows Record Vaccination (SCR-48) with the schedule and dose (when
                           started from a schedule) and the selected cattle (code, pen).
                       2. Manager confirms or selects the vaccine and enters the batch number,
                           administration time (default now), dose, route, withdrawal days (proposed from
                           the vaccine, editable), vaccinator and source "On-farm vaccination".
                       3. For each cattle the result is "Vaccinate" by default; the Manager may switch a
                           cattle to "Defer" and enter the reason and new expected date.
                       4. System shows the summary (vaccinated, deferred).
                       5. Manager selects "Save n vaccinations".
                       6. System validates the fields, each cattle's state, that the dose is not already DONE
                           and the versions.
                       7. System saves, in one transaction, one vaccination record per vaccinated cattle,
                           marks the doses DONE, recalculates the next dose dates, saves the deferrals
                           (GBR-42, GBR-43), updates each cattle's meat withdrawal end date (GBR-46) and
                           adds the timeline entries.
                       8. System triggers JOB-01 and shows MSG-FE09-04.




                                                                                                           129
Alternative Flows   AF1 – Single cattle from the profile: the same dialog with one cattle; when the cattle
                    has a matching PLANNED or DEFERRED dose, it is linked automatically.
                    AF2 – Historical vaccination: the Manager selects source "Historical entry"; batch
                    number and exact time may be unknown; no scheduled dose is closed and no
                    withdrawal is calculated.
                    AF3 – Reaction after vaccination: the Manager opens a health case linked to the record
                    (UC-FE10-02).
                    AF4 – Cancel: nothing is saved.

Exceptions          E1: Batch number missing for on-farm vaccination: MSG-FE09-05.
                    E2: Administration time in the future or before herd entry: MSG-COM-07 /
                    MSG-COM-15.
                    E3: Dose already done for a cattle: MSG-FE09-09; that cattle is skipped.
                    E4: Vaccine inactive: MSG-FE18-08.
                    E5: A cattle EXITED meanwhile: MSG-COM-13; that cattle is skipped.
                    E6: Save failure: MSG-COM-02; nothing is saved.

Priority            High

Frequency of Use    Weekly; seasonal campaigns.

Business Rules      GBR-08, GBR-26, GBR-42, GBR-43, GBR-46; BR-D14, BR-D17.

10. FE-10 Health Case and Treatment Management

10.1 Open Health Case
UC ID and Name      UC-FE10-02 — Open Health Case

Created By          G35 Team                          Date Created            21/10/2026

Primary Actor       Farm Manager                      Secondary Actors        None

Trigger             The Manager selects "Open health case" (Mở ca sức khỏe) on the Health tab of a cattle
                    profile, or "Record reaction" on a vaccination record.

Description         As a farm manager, I want to record abnormal signs of an animal and open or update a
                    health case, so that its health status, treatments and outcome are tracked in one place.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The user is a Manager of an ACTIVE farm.
                    PRE-2: The cattle is ACTIVE and registered.

Postconditions      POST-1: A new OPEN case with its opening observation exists, or the observation is
                    added to the selected OPEN or CHRONIC case.
                    POST-2: The health state is recalculated (GBR-45): a new case without treatment makes
                    it at least "Monitoring".




                                                                                                          132
Normal Flow            1. System shows Open Health Case (SCR-55) with the cattle (read-only), observed
                           time (default now) and observer (default the user).
                       2. Manager enters the body temperature (optional), the signs/description and the
                           severity (mild, moderate, severe), and selects "Open new case" (default) or "Add
                           to an existing case" with the case selected from the cattle's OPEN and CHRONIC
                           cases.
                       3. Manager optionally selects a Technician as observer and selects "Save".
                       4. System validates the fields and cattle state.
                       5. System creates the case with a case code (HC-nnnn) and its opening observation,
                           or adds the observation to the selected case, in one transaction.
                       6. System updates the health state and shows MSG-FE10-01.

Alternative Flows   AF1 – Reaction after vaccination: the vaccination record is linked to the case (GBR-47).
                    AF2 – Cancel: nothing is saved.

Exceptions          E1: Missing signs or severity: MSG-COM-06.
                    E2: Temperature out of range: MSG-FE10-06.
                    E3: Observed time in the future or before herd entry: MSG-COM-07 / MSG-COM-15.
                    E4: Cattle EXITED: MSG-COM-13.
                    E5: Selected case already CLOSED: MSG-COM-05.
                    E6: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.

Priority            High

Frequency of Use    Several times a week.

10.2 Update Health Case Status and Observation
UC ID and Name      UC-FE10-03 — Update Health Case Status and Observation

Created By          G35 Team                          Date Created            21/10/2026

Primary Actor       Farm Manager                      Secondary Actors        None

Trigger             The Manager selects "Record follow-up" (Ghi theo dõi) or "Update status" (Cập nhật
                    trạng thái) on an OPEN or CHRONIC case.

Description         As a farm manager, I want to record the progress of a case and close it with an
                    outcome, so that the animal's health history is complete and deaths are passed to the
                    Owner.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The user is a Manager of an ACTIVE farm.
                    PRE-2: The case is OPEN or CHRONIC and the cattle is ACTIVE.




                                                                                                         133
Postconditions      POST-1: Follow-up: a follow-up observation is added to the case.
                    POST-2: Close: the case is CLOSED with outcome, closing time and closer; the health
                    state is recalculated.
                    POST-3: Outcome DIED or MISSING: a PENDING death voucher is created for the Owner
                    (GBR-95); the cattle stays ACTIVE.
                    POST-4: Chronic monitoring: the case is CHRONIC with its "chronic since" date, or OPEN
                    again; the health state is recalculated (GBR-45).

Normal Flow             1. Manager selects "Record follow-up"; System shows Record Follow-up (SCR-56)
                            with the case.
                        2. Manager enters the time, optional temperature, the progress (improved,
                            unchanged, worsened) and the description and selects Save.
                        3. System validates and saves the observation; MSG-FE10-02.
                        4. Later the Manager selects "Update status"; System shows Update / Close Health
                            Case (SCR-58).
                        5. Manager enters the latest progress, selects "Close case", the outcome (recovered,
                            died, missing), the closing time and the confirming person, and selects Save.
                        6. System validates the outcome, time (not before the last observation or
                            treatment) and version.
                        7. System closes the case and recalculates the health state in one transaction;
                            MSG-FE10-04.

Alternative Flows   AF1 – Keep OPEN or Keep CHRONIC (step 5): the Manager saves the progress without
                    changing the status; only an observation is added.
                    AF2 – Move to chronic monitoring (step 5): for an OPEN case the Manager selects
                    "Move to chronic monitoring"; the System sets the case CHRONIC with the date,
                    recalculates the health state (Monitoring when no other OPEN case has a treatment)
                    and shows MSG-FE10-08.
                    AF3 – Return to OPEN (step 5): for a CHRONIC case the Manager selects "Return to
                    OPEN"; the System sets the case OPEN, recalculates the health state and shows
                    MSG-FE10-09.
                    AF4 – Outcome DIED or MISSING (step 7): in the same transaction the System creates a
                    PENDING herd exit (type DEATH, or OTHER with reason "Missing") linked to the case
                    and notifies the Owner (GBR-47); MSG-FE10-05.
                    AF5 – Cancel: nothing changes.

Exceptions          E1: Missing progress, description or outcome: MSG-COM-06.
                    E2: Time in the future or before earlier case entries: MSG-COM-07 / MSG-FE10-07.
                    E3: Case already CLOSED or changed: MSG-COM-05.
                    E4: Cattle EXITED: MSG-COM-13.
                    E5: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.

Priority            High

Frequency of Use    Daily while cases are open.

Business Rules      GBR-08, GBR-34, GBR-45, GBR-47; BR-D11, BR-D33.

Other Information   Screens SCR-54, SCR-56, SCR-58. Related: UC-FE13-01.

Assumptions         —




                                                                                                          134
10.3 Record Treatment
UC ID and Name      UC-FE10-04 — Record Treatment

Created By          G35 Team                          Date Created             21/10/2026

Primary Actor       Farm Manager                      Secondary Actors         None

Trigger             The Manager selects "Record treatment" (Ghi điều trị) on an OPEN or CHRONIC case.

Description         As a farm manager, I want to record each treatment actually given with its withdrawal
                    period, so that treatment history and meat-safety dates are known.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The user is a Manager of an ACTIVE farm.
                    PRE-2: The case is OPEN or CHRONIC and the cattle is ACTIVE.

Postconditions      POST-1: A treatment record is saved in the case with the withdrawal end date.
                    POST-2: The cattle's meat withdrawal end date is updated (GBR-46).
                    POST-3: The case is OPEN (a CHRONIC case returns to OPEN) and the health state is
                    "Under treatment" (GBR-45).

Normal Flow            1. System shows Record Treatment (SCR-57) with the case.
                       2. Manager selects the medicine from the farm catalogue (reference dose, route
                           and withdrawal days are proposed), enters the dose given, route, administration
                           time (default now), withdrawal days (editable), performer and optional
                           veterinary advice.
                       3. System shows the withdrawal end date = administration time + withdrawal days.
                       4. Manager selects "Save treatment".
                       5. System validates the fields, the case and cattle state.
                       6. System saves the treatment and timeline entry, updates the withdrawal end date,
                           recalculates the health state and shows MSG-FE10-03.

Alternative Flows   AF1 – Several administrations of a course: each administration is recorded separately
                    when it is given.
                    AF2 – Treatment on a CHRONIC case: MSG-FE10-10 is shown in the dialog; on save the
                    case returns to OPEN in the same transaction.
                    AF3 – Cancel: nothing is saved.

Exceptions          E1: Missing medicine, dose, route or time: MSG-COM-06.
                    E2: Medicine inactive: MSG-FE18-08.
                    E3: Time in the future or before the case was opened: MSG-COM-07 / MSG-FE10-07.
                    E4: Case CLOSED or cattle EXITED: MSG-COM-05 / MSG-COM-13.
                    E5: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.

Priority            High

Frequency of Use    Daily while cases are open.

Business Rules      GBR-08, GBR-45, GBR-46; BR-D14.

Other Information   Screens SCR-54, SCR-57.

Assumptions         Stock of medicines is not tracked.

11. FE-11 Estrus and Breeding Recording

11.1 Record Estrus Event
UC ID and Name      UC-FE11-02 — Record Estrus Event

Created By          G35 Team                          Date Created             21/10/2026

Primary Actor       Farm Manager                      Secondary Actors         None

Trigger             The Manager selects "Record estrus" (Ghi động dục) on the Reproduction tab of a
                    female's profile.

Description         As a farm manager, I want to record an observed estrus, so that the breeding window is
                    suggested and the reproduction history is complete.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The user is a Manager of an ACTIVE farm.
                    PRE-2: The cattle is an ACTIVE, registered female and its reproductive status offers this
                    action (GBR-54).

Postconditions      POST-1: A VALID estrus event exists with observed time, observer, description, recorder
                    and entry time.
                    POST-2: The reproductive status becomes IN_ESTRUS; the breeding window reminder
                    REM-02 is created when the parameters exist; a TENTATIVE expected calving date of an
                    earlier breeding is cancelled (GBR-53).

Normal Flow            1. System shows Record Estrus (SCR-61): female (read-only: code, ear tag, breed,
                           pen), observed time (default now), observer (default the user; a Technician may
                           be selected) and description.
                       2. Manager enters the observed time, observer and description and selects "Save
                           estrus".
                       3. System validates the time (not in the future, not before herd entry) and checks for
                           a possible duplicate (same female within 12 hours).
                       4. System saves the event, updates the reproductive status and reminders (JOB-01)
                           and shows MSG-FE11-01 with the suggested breeding window.

Alternative Flows   AF1 – Possible duplicate (step 3): MSG-FE11-03 asks whether to save anyway; No
                    returns to the form.
                    AF2 – Female PREGNANT: the action is not offered; if a pregnancy exists when saving,
                    MSG-FE11-05 asks the Manager to review the status first.
                    AF3 – Breeding parameters not configured: the event is saved; MSG-FE11-08 explains
                    that no window is suggested.
                    AF4 – Cancel: nothing is saved.

Exceptions          E1: Missing observed time: MSG-COM-06.
                    E2: Time in the future or before herd entry: MSG-COM-07 / MSG-COM-15.
                    E3: Cattle EXITED: MSG-COM-13.
                    E4: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.

Priority            High




                                                                                                          136
Frequency of Use    Several times a week.

Business Rules      GBR-08, GBR-48, GBR-49, GBR-53, GBR-54, GBR-63.

Other Information   Screens SCR-59, SCR-60, SCR-61. REM-02.

Assumptions         Estrus is observed by people; no sensor input.



11.2 Record Breeding Event
UC ID and Name      UC-FE11-03 — Record Breeding Event

Created By          G35 Team                          Date Created             21/10/2026

Primary Actor       Farm Manager                      Secondary Actors         None

Trigger             The Manager selects "Record breeding" (Ghi phối giống) on the Reproduction tab of a
                    female in status AWAITING_BREEDING, IN_ESTRUS or POST_CALVING (GBR-54).

Description         As a farm manager, I want to record a breeding with method and sire or semen, so that
                    a tentative expected calving date and the pregnancy-check reminder are prepared.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The user is a Manager of an ACTIVE farm.
                    PRE-2: The cattle is an ACTIVE, registered female to which reproductive status applies,
                    without an OPEN pregnancy episode (GBR-54).

Postconditions      POST-1: A VALID breeding event exists; the female is BRED.
                    POST-2: A TENTATIVE expected calving date (breeding time + gestation length) is shown
                    and REM-03 is created when parameters exist; an earlier tentative date is replaced.

Normal Flow            1. System shows Record Breeding (SCR-62) with the female (read-only), the latest
                           estrus of the last 72 hours proposed as link and the breeding eligibility indicator
                           with each condition (GBR-55).
                       2. Manager enters bred time, data source, method (natural service or AI) and sire
                           source (internal or external).
                       3. Internal sire: Manager selects a male ACTIVE cattle of the farm. External / AI:
                           Manager enters sire breed, semen straw/batch code and supplier.
                       4. Manager selects the performer (person or AI technician name) and an optional
                           note; the System shows the tentative expected calving date.
                       5. Manager selects "Save breeding".
                       6. System validates the fields, chronology (not before the linked estrus, not in the
                           future), the sire (different male of the same farm) and the female's state.
                       7. System saves the event, sets the female BRED, the tentative date and REM-03
                           (JOB-01) and shows MSG-FE11-02.

Alternative Flows   AF1 – No observed estrus: the Manager clears the estrus link; no estrus is invented
                    (GBR-50).
                    AF2 – Breeding eligibility Not met: MSG-FE11-09 is shown; the Manager may still save
                    (GBR-55).
                    AF3 – Second insemination in the same estrus: allowed; the latest breeding is the basis
                    of the tentative date.
                    AF4 – Cancel: nothing is saved.



                                                                                                            137
Exceptions          E1: Missing required field: MSG-COM-06.
                    E2: Internal sire not a different male of the farm: MSG-FE11-04.
                    E3: Female has an OPEN pregnancy: MSG-FE11-05.
                    E4: Time invalid: MSG-COM-07 / MSG-FE11-10.
                    E5: Gestation parameter missing: saved without expected date (MSG-FE11-08).
                    E6: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.

Priority            High

Frequency of Use    Several times a week.

Business Rules      GBR-08, GBR-48, GBR-49, GBR-50, GBR-51, GBR-53, GBR-54, GBR-55.

Other Information   Screens SCR-60, SCR-62. REM-03.

Assumptions         —



11.3 Correct or Void Estrus and Breeding Record
UC ID and Name      UC-FE11-04 — Correct or Void Estrus and Breeding Record

Created By          G35 Team                        Date Created             21/10/2026

Primary Actor       Farm Manager                    Secondary Actors         None

Trigger             The Manager selects "Edit · Void" (Sửa · Hủy) on an estrus or breeding row of the
                    reproduction timeline.

Description         As a farm manager, I want to correct or void a wrong estrus or breeding record with a
                    reason, so that reproduction data and dates stay correct without losing history.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The user is a Manager of an ACTIVE farm.
                    PRE-2: The record is VALID and the female is ACTIVE.

Postconditions      POST-1: Correct: the record has the new values with a "Corrected" marker and stays
                    VALID; a Record Revision keeps the old values.
                    POST-2: Void: the record is VOIDED and excluded from status and dates.
                    POST-3: Reproductive status, tentative date and reminders are recalculated.

Normal Flow             1. System shows Correct / Void Estrus or Breeding (SCR-63) with the record and the
                            action choice.
                        2. Manager keeps "Correct record" and changes the allowed fields (estrus: observed
                            time, observer, description; breeding: time, source, method, sire, semen,
                            performer, linked estrus).
                        3. Manager enters the reason and selects Save.
                        4. System checks the version and the dependencies: a breeding linked to a
                            pregnancy check or episode cannot change in a way that contradicts them
                            (GBR-59).
                        5. System saves the change and the Record Revision in one transaction, recalculates
                            status, dates and reminders and shows MSG-FE11-06.




                                                                                                         138
Alternative Flows   AF1 – Void record (step 2): the Manager selects "Void record", enters the reason and
                    confirms; dependencies are checked as in step 4.
                    AF2 – Cancel: nothing changes.

Exceptions          E1: Dependent records block the change: MSG-FE11-07 lists them.
                    E2: Missing reason: MSG-COM-06.
                    E3: Invalid values: as in UC-FE11-02 / UC-FE11-03.
                    E4: Changed in another session: MSG-COM-05.
                    E5: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.

Priority            Medium

Frequency of Use    Rare.

Business Rules      GBR-08, GBR-59; BR-D15.

Other Information   Screens SCR-60, SCR-63.

Assumptions         —




12. FE-12 Pregnancy and Calving Recording

12.1 Record Pregnancy Check Result
UC ID and Name      UC-FE12-02 — Record Pregnancy Check Result

Created By          G35 Team                         Date Created           21/10/2026

Primary Actor       Farm Manager                     Secondary Actors       None

Trigger             The Manager selects "Record pregnancy check" (Ghi khám thai) on the Reproduction
                    tab of a female in status BRED or PREGNANT, from "More actions" for any ACTIVE
                    female at stage Young stock or Adult (GBR-54), or from reminder REM-03 / REM-04.

Description         As a farm manager, I want to record the result of a pregnancy examination, so that
                    pregnancy is confirmed only by an actual check and the calving date becomes reliable.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The user is a Manager of an ACTIVE farm.
                    PRE-2: The cattle is an ACTIVE, registered female.

Postconditions      POST-1: A VALID pregnancy check is saved.
                    POST-2: PREGNANT: a pregnancy episode is OPEN (or confirmed) with a CONFIRMED
                    expected calving date and REM-05. NOT_PREGNANT: the female returns to OPEN and
                    the tentative date is cancelled. INCONCLUSIVE: status unchanged and REM-04 is
                    created.
                    POST-3: REM-03 is resolved.




                                                                                                       139
Normal Flow            1. System shows Record Pregnancy Check (SCR-64) with the female (read-only) and
                           the latest breeding proposed as link.
                       2. Manager enters checked time, data source, result (pregnant, not pregnant,
                           inconclusive), method (ultrasound, rectal, other), examiner and optional note;
                           for PREGNANT with a linked breeding the System shows the expected calving
                           date.
                       3. Manager selects "Save check result".
                       4. System validates the fields, chronology (not before the linked breeding) and the
                           female's state; it serializes changes for the female (GBR-52).
                       5. System saves the check and applies the result (GBR-51, GBR-53) in one
                           transaction, updates reminders (JOB-01) and shows MSG-FE12-01.

Alternative Flows   AF1 – No linked breeding: a PREGNANT result opens an episode without expected date
                    (MSG-FE12-11).
                    AF2 – PREGNANT while an episode is already OPEN: the check confirms the existing
                    episode.
                    AF3 – Cancel: nothing is saved.

Exceptions          E1: Missing required field: MSG-COM-06.
                    E2: Conflict with an existing check or newer episode: MSG-FE12-02.
                    E3: Time invalid: MSG-COM-07 / MSG-FE11-10.
                    E4: Cattle EXITED: MSG-COM-13.
                    E5: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.

Priority            High

Frequency of Use    Weekly.

Business Rules      GBR-08, GBR-49, GBR-50, GBR-51, GBR-52, GBR-53; BR-D18.

Other Information   Screens SCR-60, SCR-64. REM-03, REM-04, REM-05.

Assumptions         Results may come from an external veterinarian; no veterinarian account exists.



12.2 Update Pregnancy Status
UC ID and Name      UC-FE12-03 — Update Pregnancy Status

Created By          G35 Team                          Date Created           21/10/2026

Primary Actor       Farm Manager                      Secondary Actors       None

Trigger             The Manager selects "Record pregnancy update" (Ghi diễn biến thai) on the
                    Reproduction tab of a PREGNANT female.

Description         As a farm manager, I want to record observations, complications or a loss during
                    pregnancy, so that the pregnancy history is complete and a loss closes the pregnancy.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The user is a Manager of an ACTIVE farm.
                    PRE-2: The female has an OPEN pregnancy episode.




                                                                                                        140
Postconditions      POST-1: A VALID pregnancy update is saved against the episode.
                    POST-2: LOSS: the episode is LOST, REM-05 is cancelled and the female returns to OPEN.

Normal Flow             1. System shows Record Pregnancy Update (SCR-65) with the episode (pregnancy
                            number, expected date).
                        2. Manager selects the update type (observation, complication, loss), enters the
                            time, data source and details and, for a complication, optionally links an existing
                            health case of the female.
                        3. Manager selects "Save update".
                        4. System validates the fields, chronology (after the confirming check) and episode
                            state.
                        5. System saves the update in one transaction and shows MSG-FE12-03.

Alternative Flows   AF1 – Loss (step 5): the System closes the episode as LOST, cancels REM-05, updates
                    status and shows MSG-FE12-04 (GBR-56).
                    AF2 – Complication needing treatment: the Manager opens or updates the case in
                    FE-10 (UC-FE10-02).
                    AF3 – Cancel: nothing is saved.

Exceptions          E1: Missing required field: MSG-COM-06.
                    E2: Episode no longer OPEN: MSG-FE12-12.
                    E3: Time invalid: MSG-COM-07.
                    E4: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.

12.3 Record Calving Event and Outcome
UC ID and Name      UC-FE12-04 — Record Calving Event and Outcome

Created By          G35 Team                          Date Created             21/10/2026

Primary Actor       Farm Manager                      Secondary Actors         None

Trigger             The Manager selects "Record calving" (Ghi sự kiện đẻ) on the Reproduction tab of a
                    female in status BRED or PREGNANT, or "Record calving (untracked pregnancy)" under
                    More actions for any ACTIVE female at stage Young stock or Adult (GBR-54).

Description         As a farm manager, I want to record a calving with one outcome per calf and register
                    live calves now or as drafts, so that births are complete and every live calf becomes one
                    cattle record.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The user is a Manager of an ACTIVE farm.
                    PRE-2: The dam is an ACTIVE, registered female.




                                                                                                            141
Postconditions      POST-1: A VALID calving with at least one calf outcome is saved; the linked episode is
                    CALVED and the dam is POST_CALVING.
                    POST-2: Each LIVE calf is REGISTERED (Register now) or PENDING_CONFIRMATION with
                    REM-09 (draft).
                    POST-3: REM-05 is resolved and REM-07 is created when parameters exist.
                    POST-4: Registered calves have life stage UNWEANED_CALF; a dam whose life stage is
                    not ADULT gets a REM-08 suggestion to change it to ADULT (GBR-25).

Normal Flow            1. System opens Record Calving (SCR-66) with the dam (read-only) and the OPEN
                           episode proposed as linked pregnancy.
                       2. Manager enters birth time, data source, birth attendant, calving course (normal,
                           assisted, caesarean), dam condition (good, needs monitoring, critical) and the
                           number of calves; the System creates one outcome block per calf.
                       3. For each calf the Manager enters the outcome (live, stillborn), sex (male, female,
                           unknown), birth weight with unit and basis (measured, estimated).
                       4. For each live calf the Manager enters the tag/QR code, initial pen (default: the
                           dam's pen), breed (default from dam and sire) and chooses "Save as draft calf"
                           or "Register now"; for "Register now" the Manager also selects the production
                           purpose (default: the first ACTIVE purpose of type UNDECIDED).
                       5. Manager selects "Save calving".
                       6. System validates the fields, chronology, tag uniqueness in the farm, pen
                           availability for calves to register and the dam's state; it serializes changes for the
                           dam (GBR-52).
                       7. System saves, in one transaction, the calving, the outcomes, the episode closure
                           and the dam status; registers each "Register now" calf with UC-FE06-01 logic
                           (FARM_BORN, GBR-31); saves each draft calf as PENDING_CONFIRMATION and
                           creates REM-09 for the Owner and Managers (GBR-58).
                       8. System updates reminders (JOB-01) and shows MSG-FE12-05 with MSG-FE12-09
                           and/or MSG-FE12-10.

Alternative Flows   AF1 – Pregnancy not tracked: the Manager selects "No tracked pregnancy"; the calving
                    is saved without an episode (GBR-57).
                    AF2 – Stillborn calf: no tag, pen or registration choice is asked.
                    AF3 – Calf dies before registration (later): the Owner or a Manager marks the draft calf
                    DIED_BEFORE_REGISTRATION from the pending calves list (SCR-36).
                    AF4 – Add / remove calf blocks: "+ Add calf" / "Remove calf" before saving.
                    AF5 – Cancel: nothing is saved.

Exceptions          E1: No calf outcome: MSG-FE12-06.
                    E2: Birth weight not positive or without unit/basis: MSG-FE12-07.
                    E3: Tag already used in the farm: MSG-FE12-08.
                    E4: Pen INACTIVE or full for a calf to register: MSG-FE04-05.
                    E5: Duplicate calving, concurrent loss or newer episode: MSG-FE12-12.
                    E6: Time invalid: MSG-COM-07.
                    E7: Save failure: MSG-COM-02; nothing is saved.

Priority            High

Frequency of Use    Several times a month.




                                                                                                             142
Business Rules      GBR-08, GBR-31, GBR-49, GBR-52, GBR-57, GBR-58; BR-D08, BR-D10, BR-D19.

Other Information   Screens SCR-60, SCR-66, SCR-36. Related: UC-FE06-01. REM-05, REM-07, REM-09.

Assumptions         —

12.5 Correct or Void Pregnancy and Calving Record
UC ID and Name      UC-FE12-06 — Correct or Void Pregnancy and Calving Record

Created By          G35 Team                         Date Created              21/10/2026

Primary Actor       Farm Manager                     Secondary Actors          None

Trigger             The Manager selects "Edit · Void" on a pregnancy update, calving or calf outcome row
                    of the reproduction timeline.

Description         As a farm manager, I want to correct or void a wrong pregnancy update, calving or calf
                    outcome, so that birth records are accurate while registered calves stay consistent.

                        2. Manager selects the record type (pregnancy update, calving, calf outcome), the

                        3. Manager edits the allowed fields (update: type, time, details, health case; calving:
                            time, source, attendant, course, dam condition; calf outcome: sex, birth weight,

                            registered calves is blocked; voiding a LOSS reopens the episode only when no
                            later episode exists (GBR-59).

                            in one transaction; MSG-FE11-06.

                    E2: Missing reason: MSG-COM-06.
                    E3: Tag already used: MSG-FE12-08.
                    E4: Changed in another session: MSG-COM-05.
                    E5: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.

Priority            Medium




                                                                                                           144
Frequency of Use    Rare.

Business Rules      GBR-08, GBR-56, GBR-57, GBR-58, GBR-59; BR-D15, BR-D19.

Other Information   Screens SCR-60, SCR-68.

13. FE-13 Herd Exit Recording

13.1 Record and Confirm Herd Exit Voucher
UC ID and Name      UC-FE13-01 — Record and Confirm Herd Exit Voucher

Created By          G35 Team                        Date Created            22/10/2026

Primary Actor       Farm Owner                      Secondary Actors        None

Trigger             The Owner selects "Create exit voucher" (Tạo phiếu xuất) or "Continue" on a DRAFT
                    voucher in Herd Exit Vouchers, or "View & confirm" (Xem & xác nhận) on a PENDING
                    death voucher, the dashboard list A03 or a notification.

Description         As a farm owner, I want to record each departure of cattle as one voucher with weights,
                    prices and the money to collect, and confirm it after reviewing open items, so that the
                    herd, the records of each animal and the buyer's balance are correct.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The user is the Owner of an ACTIVE farm.
                    PRE-2: The cattle to add are ACTIVE and on no other DRAFT, PENDING or CONFIRMED
                    exit line, or a PENDING voucher exists.

Postconditions      POST-1: The voucher is CONFIRMED with its lines, totals and collected deposit; each
                    cattle on it is EXITED and read-only.
                    POST-2: For each cattle: the open pen assignment is closed at the exit date; future
                    doses and reminders are cancelled; OPEN and CHRONIC health cases are closed "Closed
                    by herd exit"; an open pregnancy is closed "Dam exited" (GBR-35).
                    POST-3: Until confirmation a DRAFT or PENDING voucher has no effect; its cattle stay
                    ACTIVE.




                                                                                                          145
Normal Flow            1. System shows Herd Exit Voucher (SCR-70) with the next voucher code, exit type
                           Sale and today as exit date.
                       2. Owner chooses the exit type (Sale, Cull or Other) and enters the exit date, buyer
                           (trading partner, added inline when new) or destination, transport quarantine
                           certificate, vehicle plate and note.
                       3. Owner selects "+ Select cattle"; System opens Select Cattle for Exit Voucher
                           (SCR-109) with ACTIVE cattle not on another open voucher, filters (pen, herd
                           group, minimum weight) and warnings per cattle; Owner selects cattle and
                           confirms, or scans their QR tags.
                       4. System adds one line per cattle with the latest weight as exit weight (basis
                           ESTIMATED, marked UT) and the check result.
                       5. Owner chooses the pricing basis, enters the unit price and, where weighed, the
                           measured exit weight of each line; System calculates line amounts and totals
                           (GBR-91, GBR-92).
                       6. Owner attaches documents and enters deductions, the deposit collected, method
                           and due date of the balance; System shows total receivable and outstanding.
                       7. System shows the pre-confirmation checks per item and cattle: active meat
                           withdrawal period (MSG-FE13-07), OPEN and CHRONIC health cases, future
                           doses and reminders that will be cancelled, open pregnancy and draft calves of a
                           dam, and the occupancy change of each pen.
                       8. Owner ticks "I have reviewed the items above and confirm the exit" and selects
                           "Confirm exit of n cattle" (Xác nhận xuất n con).
                       9. System validates the date (not in the future, not before the herd entry or the
                           latest recorded event of each cattle), the state of each cattle, the amounts and
                           the version.
                       10. System confirms the voucher and applies all effects to every line in one
                           transaction (GBR-35, GBR-94); the audit entry is saved.
                       11. System shows MSG-FE13-02 and opens Herd Exit Voucher Detail (SCR-110).

Alternative Flows   AF1 – Confirm a pending death voucher: System opens Death / Missing Exit Voucher
                    (SCR-71) with the cattle, source health case, reporter and date of death or loss; the
                    Owner completes the cause and carcass disposal, attaches evidence, reviews the checks
                    and continues at step 8; the voucher has no amounts (GBR-95).
                    AF2 – Dismiss a pending voucher: the Owner selects "Dismiss" with a reason; the
                    voucher becomes DISMISSED, the cattle stays ACTIVE and the health case keeps its
                    outcome (MSG-FE13-04).
                    AF3 – Save draft (any step): the voucher is saved as DRAFT (MSG-FE13-06); the cattle
                    stay ACTIVE and cannot be added to another voucher.
                    AF4 – Remove a line: the Owner removes a cattle from a DRAFT voucher; totals are
                    recalculated.
                    AF5 – Warnings present: MSG-FE13-01 lists them per cattle; warnings never block, the
                    Owner can still confirm after ticking the confirmation.
                    AF6 – Death recorded directly: the Owner chooses type "Death" for a loss not reported
                    through a health case and continues as AF1.
                    AF7 – Discard draft / Cancel: the Owner discards a DRAFT voucher or leaves without
                    saving (MSG-COM-14).




                                                                                                          146
Exceptions          E1: Exit date invalid: MSG-FE13-03.
                    E2: Cattle already on another open voucher: MSG-FE13-08.
                    E3: No cattle line: MSG-FE06-16.
                    E4: Deposit greater than the total receivable: MSG-FE06-13.
                    E5: Missing type, date, buyer or destination, cause or required field: MSG-COM-06.
                    E6: Confirmation not ticked: MSG-COM-16.
                    E7: A cattle is no longer ACTIVE or the voucher changed: MSG-COM-05.
                    E8: Save failure: MSG-COM-02; nothing changes.

Priority            High

Frequency of Use    Weekly.

Business Rules      GBR-07, GBR-34, GBR-35, GBR-46, GBR-91, GBR-92, GBR-94, GBR-95, GBR-97; BR-D09,
                    BR-D11, BR-D34, BR-D35, BR-D39.

Other Information   Screens SCR-69, SCR-70, SCR-71, SCR-109, SCR-110. Related: UC-FE10-03, UC-FE13-04.

Assumptions         Collections from the buyer are recorded in BeefCare as farm bookkeeping; invoices and
                    tax documents are outside BeefCare.



13.2 Void Herd Exit Voucher
UC ID and Name      UC-FE13-03 — Void Herd Exit Voucher

Created By          G35 Team                        Date Created              22/10/2026

Primary Actor       Farm Owner                      Secondary Actors          None

Trigger             The Owner selects "Void voucher" (Hủy phiếu) on a CONFIRMED voucher in Herd Exit
                    Voucher Detail (SCR-110).

Description         As a farm owner, I want to void a mistaken exit voucher with a reason, so that its
                    animals return to the herd while the mistake stays traceable.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The user is the Owner of an ACTIVE farm.
                    PRE-2: The voucher is CONFIRMED.

Postconditions      POST-1: The voucher is VOIDED with reason, actor and time; every cattle on it is ACTIVE
                    again with an open pen assignment in its last pen or in the pen chosen by the Owner.
                    POST-2: Items closed or cancelled by the exit are listed for review and are not restored
                    automatically; recorded collections stay in the voucher history.

Normal Flow            1. System shows Void Herd Exit Voucher (SCR-72) with the voucher (type, date,
                           number of cattle, buyer) and the effects: cattle back to ACTIVE, last pen and its
                           occupancy after the void, cancelled items not restored, collections kept.
                       2. Owner enters the void reason and selects "Void voucher".
                       3. System checks the voucher state and version, that no cattle of the voucher is on
                           another open exit line, and that each last pen is ACTIVE with enough free places.
                       4. System voids the voucher, sets each cattle ACTIVE, opens its pen assignment from
                           the void date and saves the audit entry in one transaction (GBR-36).
                       5. System shows MSG-FE13-05 with the list of items closed or cancelled by the exit.




                                                                                                          147
Alternative Flows   AF1 – Last pen unavailable or full (step 3): MSG-FE13-09; the Owner chooses an
                    ACTIVE pen with free places for each affected cattle (reception warnings need a reason)
                    and continues at step 4.
                    AF2 – Close the dialog: nothing changes.

Exceptions          E1: Missing reason: MSG-COM-06.
                    E2: Voucher already VOIDED or changed: MSG-COM-05.
                    E3: Save failure: MSG-COM-02; nothing changes.

Priority            Low

Frequency of Use    Rare.

Business Rules      GBR-07, GBR-20, GBR-26, GBR-36, GBR-91; BR-D09, BR-D11, BR-D35.

Other Information   Screens SCR-72, SCR-110. Related: UC-FE04-05.

Assumptions         Money already collected is refunded outside BeefCare.




14. FE-18 Farm Master Data and Reference Configuration

14.1 Configure Farm State and Reminder References
UC ID and Name      UC-FE18-17 — Configure Farm State and Reminder References

Created By          G35 Team                        Date Created             21/10/2026

Primary Actor       Farm Owner, Farm Manager        Secondary Actors         None

Trigger             The user opens "State & reminder parameters" (Tham số trạng thái & nhắc việc) in
                    Catalogues & Parameters and selects "Save new version".

Description         As a farm owner or manager, I want to set the farm's life-stage ages, breeding
                    conditions, gestation and reminder intervals with their source, so that suggestions and
                    reminders fit our farm.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The user is the Owner or a Manager of an ACTIVE farm.

Postconditions      POST-1: A new parameter version with source note, effective date and author is saved
                    and becomes effective at its effective time.
                    POST-2: Reminders evaluated after that time use the new values; recorded events are
                    unchanged (GBR-68).




                                                                                                        148
Normal Flow            1. System shows State and Reminder Parameters (SCR-85) with the current version,
                           its source and the version history.
                       2. User edits the values in the groups: life stages (weaning age, young-stock age,
                           adult age, in months); breeding conditions (minimum age, minimum weight);
                           gestation and checks (gestation length, pregnancy check after breeding, re-check
                           after inconclusive, calving preparation lead time, postpartum interval); breeding
                           window (start and end hours after estrus, estrus cycle length); growth and
                           vaccination (BCS scale, weighing interval, vaccination reminder lead time,
                           optional target finishing weight).
                       3. User enters the source note (for example "Owner interview 20/09/2026") and the
                           effective date (default today) and selects "Save new version".
                       4. System validates each value against its allowed range and the version.
                       5. System saves the new version and, when effective now, triggers JOB-01 to
                           recompute future reminders.
                       6. System shows MSG-FE18-05.

Alternative Flows   AF1 – Future effective date: the version is saved as scheduled and applied by JOB-01 at
                    that date.
                    AF2 – View an older version: the user opens the history; old versions are read-only.
                    AF3 – Cancel: nothing changes.

Exceptions          E1: Value out of range, end hour not after start hour, or life-stage ages not in increasing
                    order (weaning < young stock < adult): MSG-FE18-06.
                    E2: Missing source note: MSG-COM-06.
                    E3: Changed in another session: MSG-COM-05.
                    E4: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.

Priority            Medium

Frequency of Use    A few times a year.

Business Rules      GBR-25, GBR-55, GBR-63, GBR-68.

Other Information   Screen SCR-85 (default values and allowed ranges in III.18.5). Related: JOB-01.

Assumptions         Parameter values are agreed with the farm; BeefCare provides decision support only
                    (OR-06).




15. FE-19 Subscription and Payment Management

15.1 Request Subscription Payment
UC ID and Name      UC-FE19-02 — Request Subscription Payment

Created By          G35 Team                         Date Created              21/10/2026

Primary Actor       Farm Owner                       Secondary Actors          SePay (VietQR image)

Trigger             The Owner selects "Renew plan" (Gia hạn gói) on Farm Subscription, "Pay to activate"
                    on Select Farm, or continues from Register Farm (first purchase).




                                                                                                           149
Description         As a farm owner, I want to choose a 1-, 2- or 3-year term and get transfer instructions
                    with VietQR, so that I can pay for my farm by bank transfer.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The user is the Owner of the farm (any service status except SUSPENDED).
                    PRE-2: A price version is effective and the receiving account is configured.

Postconditions      POST-1: One PENDING payment request with immutable term, amount, price version,
                    receiving account, unique payment reference and 24-hour expiry exists for the farm (or
                    the existing valid request is reopened).
                    POST-2: The Owner sees the transfer instructions; no service period changes until the
                    payment is confirmed (UC-FE19-08 / UC-FE19-06).

Normal Flow            1. System shows Select Term and Review (SCR-87): the three terms with total prices
                           from the effective price version, the plan terms (same functions for all terms,
                           one payment per farm, no automatic renewal, request valid 24 hours) and the
                           farm's service status and current period.
                       2. Owner selects a term; the System shows the review: farm, term, amount,
                           estimated service period and price version.
                       3. Owner selects "Create payment request".
                       4. System checks ownership, service status, the renewal window (GBR-71), that no
                           unexpired PENDING request exists and that the reviewed price version is still
                           effective (GBR-70).
                       5. System creates the request with a unique payment reference (e.g. BCF7K2Q9),
                           expiry = now + 24 hours and the receiving account snapshot; it creates the
                           farm's Subscription record if none exists.
                       6. System shows Transfer Instructions (SCR-88): VietQR (API-01), status PENDING,
                           countdown, expiry time, bank, account number, account name, amount and
                           transfer content with copy buttons, and the progress steps (request created,
                           bank transaction received, confirmed and period granted), with MSG-FE19-05.

Alternative Flows   AF1 – A valid PENDING request exists (step 4): MSG-FE19-01 offers to reopen its
                    instructions; no new request.
                    AF2 – Price changed since review (step 4): MSG-FE19-02 shows old and new prices; the
                    Owner reviews again (step 2).
                    AF3 – "Later" / "Close instructions": the request stays PENDING until it expires; it can
                    be reopened from Payment History.
                    AF4 – Check again (step 6): the System refreshes the status; when PAID it shows the
                    purchased period (MSG-FE19-06).
                    AF5 – Request expired: MSG-FE19-07 offers a new request.

Exceptions          E1: Not the Owner: MSG-COM-01.
                    E2: Outside the renewal window: MSG-FE19-03 with the opening date.
                    E3: Farm SUSPENDED: MSG-FE19-09 with "Send support ticket".
                    E4: Prices or receiving account unavailable: MSG-FE19-04 with Retry.
                    E5: VietQR image cannot be rendered: the transfer details remain shown as text.
                    E6: Save failure: MSG-COM-02; no request is created.

Priority            High

Frequency of Use    Once every 1–3 years per farm.




                                                                                                          150
Business Rules      GBR-69, GBR-70, GBR-71, GBR-75; BR-D23.

Other Information   Screens SCR-86, SCR-87, SCR-88, SCR-89. API-01. JOB-03 expires requests.

Assumptions         The Owner transfers from a Vietnamese bank account that supports VietQR.



15.2 Reconcile Subscription Payment
UC ID and Name      UC-FE19-06 — Reconcile Subscription Payment

Created By          G35 Team                         Date Created              21/10/2026

Primary Actor       Platform Manager                 Secondary Actors          SePay (transaction lookup)

Trigger             The PM opens an evidence item marked for review in Payments and Reconciliation,
                    from a reconciliation notification or an internal request.

Description         As a Platform Manager, I want to review bank evidence that was not confirmed
                    automatically and accept, hold or reject it with a reason, so that every transfer is
                    handled exactly once.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The PM is signed in with ACTIVE PM access.
                    PRE-2: The evidence is authenticated and not allocated.

Postconditions      POST-1: ACCEPT: the selected request is PAID with its purchased period and receipt,
                    exactly as in UC-FE19-08.
                    POST-2: HOLD or REJECT: the decision with reason is saved; no service is granted.
                    POST-3: Every decision is kept in the evidence history and audit.

Normal Flow            1. System shows Payments and Reconciliation (SCR-91) with evidence needing
                           review (unmatched code, expired request, no request, amount mismatch) and
                           the selected evidence detail: provider, environment, transaction ID, amount and
                           direction, receiving account check, transfer content, bank-paid time and
                           candidate requests.
                       2. PM selects the candidate request (when the reference is mistyped but one
                           candidate clearly corresponds) and the decision ACCEPT, and enters the reason
                           and evidence references.
                       3. PM selects "Check and accept".
                       4. System runs the shared confirmation rules (GBR-72) on the evidence and the
                           selected request: amount, receiving account, bank-paid time within the request
                           window, no allocation, no posted manual income on the same transaction
                           (GBR-84); confirmations for the farm are serialized.
                       5. System allocates the evidence, marks the request PAID, creates the purchased
                           period (GBR-73), derives the farm service status, saves the review and audit in
                           one transaction and notifies the Owner.
                       6. System shows MSG-FE19-10.




                                                                                                            151
Alternative Flows   AF1 – HOLD: the PM saves HOLD with a reason; the evidence stays unresolved for later
                    review.
                    AF2 – REJECT: the PM saves REJECT with a reason; the evidence cannot fund any
                    request unless reviewed again.
                    AF3 – Lock for return: for money that must be returned outside BeefCare, the PM locks
                    the evidence with a reason; later records the outgoing bank reference and result, or
                    unlocks with a reason when no return happened (GBR-74).
                    AF4 – Look up a transaction: the PM searches SePay transactions by ID or period
                    (API-03); a found transaction is stored as evidence once.

Exceptions          E1: Acceptance blocked (amount, account, window, allocation or manual income
                    conflict): MSG-FE19-11 with the reason; nothing is allocated.
                    E2: No candidate selected for a mistyped reference: MSG-FE19-11.
                    E3: Missing reason: MSG-COM-06.
                    E4: Evidence or request changed meanwhile (for example confirmed by SePay):
                    MSG-COM-05.
                    E5: Save failure: MSG-COM-02; no partial result.

Priority            High

Frequency of Use    Daily or as needed.

Business Rules      GBR-15, GBR-72, GBR-73, GBR-74, GBR-84, GBR-86; BR-D24, BR-D28.

Other Information   Screens SCR-91, SCR-76. API-03. Related: UC-FE19-08, UC-FE20-13.

Assumptions         Bank account operators perform any outgoing return transfer outside BeefCare.

                    POST-2: New payment requests and the public page use the version effective at their

                        2. PM enters the new prices for 1, 2 and 3 years (the terms themselves are fixed and

                            (Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh) and the supported terms text.
                        3. PM selects "Publish version".
                        4. System validates the prices (positive whole VND), the effective time (now or

15.4 Confirm Subscription Payment from SePay
UC ID and Name      UC-FE19-08 — Confirm Subscription Payment from SePay

Created By          G35 Team                          Date Created             21/10/2026

Primary Actor       SePay                             Secondary Actors         Email Delivery Service

Trigger             SePay calls the BeefCare webhook (API-02) for a transaction on the receiving account.

Description         As the platform, I want to authenticate each bank transaction reported by SePay and
                    confirm the matching payment request exactly once, so that farms are activated
                    automatically and safely.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The webhook endpoint and the environment secret are configured.

Postconditions      POST-1: The authenticated transaction is stored once as evidence.
                    POST-2: When eligible: the request is PAID, the purchased period is created, the farm
                    service is derived (never lifting a suspension), the receipt is available and the Owner is
                    notified.
                    POST-3: When not eligible: the evidence waits for PM review; nothing is granted.




                                                                                                            153
Normal Flow            1. SePay sends the transaction with HMAC signature and timestamp headers.
                       2. System verifies the signature over timestamp + "." + raw body with the
                           environment secret and a 300-second tolerance.
                       3. System stores the evidence keyed by provider, environment, account namespace
                           and transaction ID (duplicates refer to the same evidence) together with a
                           processing job, and replies HTTP 200 {"success": true}.
                       4. JOB-06 processes the evidence: incoming direction, receiving account, extracted
                           payment code matching exactly one request, exact amount, bank-paid time
                           within [created, expires) and no allocation (GBR-72).
                       5. System allocates the evidence, marks the request PAID with confirmed time,
                           creates the purchased period (GBR-73), derives the service status and saves the
                           audit in one transaction.
                       6. System creates the Owner notification and email (JOB-07) and the receipt
                           becomes visible (UC-FE21-01).

Alternative Flows   AF1 – Duplicate delivery: the existing evidence is reused; a committed confirmation is
                    returned without a new period or notice.
                    AF2 – Not eligible (no or wrong code, amount mismatch, expired window, outgoing
                    transfer): the evidence is marked for review and a reconciliation notification is created
                    for PMs (UC-FE14-04).
                    AF3 – Request EXPIRED but bank-paid time inside the window: the request becomes
                    PAID.
                    AF4 – Farm SUSPENDED: the request becomes PAID and the period is created; the farm
                    stays SUSPENDED (GBR-15).

Exceptions          E1: Invalid signature or timestamp: HTTP 401; only restricted delivery metadata is kept;
                    no evidence.
                    E2: Storage failure before step 3 completes: no success reply, so SePay retries.
                    E3: Processing failure after storage: JOB-06 retries; the evidence is never lost.

Priority            High

Frequency of Use    Every subscription transfer.

Business Rules      GBR-13, GBR-15, GBR-72, GBR-73, GBR-74, GBR-84; BR-D23, BR-D24.

Other Information   API-02, JOB-06, JOB-07. Related: UC-FE19-06, UC-FE21-01. NFR-P05.

Assumptions         SePay retries failed deliveries; the same transaction ID is kept across retries.




16. FE-20 Customer Support Ticket Management

UC ID and Name      UC-FE20-01 — Create Support Ticket

                    farms, so that Customer Support can help me even when the farm service is inactive.

16.4 Manage Ticket Assignment and Processing
UC ID and Name      UC-FE20-07 — Manage Ticket Assignment and Processing

Created By          G35 Team                         Date Created              21/10/2026

Primary Actor       Customer Support                 Secondary Actors          None

Trigger             The CS selects "Claim" (Nhận) on an unassigned ticket, or "Hand over", "Ask customer",
                    "Return to queue", or edits category/priority on an assigned ticket.

Description         As Customer Support, I want to take responsibility for tickets, classify them and move
                    them through processing states, so that every ticket has one accountable person and a
                    clear status.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The CS is signed in with ACTIVE CS access.
                    PRE-2: The ticket is not CLOSED. Claim: the ticket has no available assigned CS. Other
                    actions: the CS is the assigned CS.

Postconditions      POST-1: The assignment, status, category or priority is updated with reason where
                    required, version and history.
                    POST-2: The Owner and/or the new CS are notified as applicable.

Normal Flow             1. CS selects "Claim" on a ticket in the queue (SCR-96).
                        2. System checks CS access, that no available CS is assigned and the version; it
                            assigns the ticket to the CS atomically and sets OPEN → IN_PROGRESS (a
                            RESOLVED ticket keeps its status).
                        3. System saves the history event and shows MSG-FE20-07; the Ticket Processing
                            screen (SCR-97) opens.




                                                                                                           157
                        4. CS sets category and priority as needed; System saves them with history
                            (MSG-COM-09).

Alternative Flows   AF1 – Hand over: CS selects another available CS and enters a reason; the System
                    reassigns atomically, keeps status and OPEN internal requests, notifies the new CS
                    (MSG-FE20-08).
                    AF2 – Ask customer: CS sets WAITING_CUSTOMER after a PUBLIC question; the
                    Owner's next reply returns the ticket to IN_PROGRESS (GBR-78).
                    AF3 – Return to queue: CS releases the ticket with a reason; it becomes unassigned
                    with unchanged status.
                    AF4 – Assigned CS becomes unavailable: the ticket returns to the unassigned queue
                    automatically (GBR-77).

Exceptions          E1: Ticket already claimed by another CS: MSG-COM-05 and the queue refreshes.
                    E2: Not the assigned CS: MSG-FE20-09.
                    E3: Missing handover or release reason: MSG-COM-06.
                    E4: Ticket CLOSED: MSG-FE20-06.
                    E5: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.

Priority            High

Frequency of Use    Many times a day.

Business Rules      GBR-77, GBR-78, GBR-86.

Other Information   Screens SCR-96, SCR-97.

Assumptions         —

                    PRE-2: The ticket is RESOLVED, the Owner has acknowledged the latest resolution

Postconditions      POST-1: The ticket is CLOSED and read-only; the closure history records the resolution
                    version, the acknowledgment and the reviewing CS and time.
                    POST-2: The Owner is notified.

                            version with time), the Owner's confirmation text and an optional closing note.
                        2. CS ticks "I have reviewed the Owner's confirmation and agree to close the ticket"
                            and selects "Close ticket".
                        3. System rechecks the conditions and version (GBR-80).
                        4. System sets CLOSED and saves the closure history in one transaction.

Exceptions          E1: No acknowledgment of the latest version: MSG-FE20-13; Close is disabled.

                    E3: Confirmation not ticked: MSG-COM-16.

16.7 Manage Ticket Internal Requests
UC ID and Name      UC-FE20-12 — Manage Ticket Internal Requests

Created By          G35 Team                         Date Created             21/10/2026

Primary Actor       Customer Support (assigned)      Secondary Actors         Platform Manager, System
                                                                              Admin (recipients)

Trigger             The assigned CS selects "Create request" (Tạo yêu cầu cho PM / SA), "Change
                    recipient" or "Cancel request" in the internal request panel of Ticket Processing.

Description         As the assigned CS, I want to ask a named Platform Manager or System Admin for help
                    on a ticket, so that domain work is done by the right person while I keep responsibility
                    for the customer.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The CS is the assigned, available CS.
                    PRE-2: Create: the ticket is IN_PROGRESS or WAITING_CUSTOMER and has no OPEN
                    request for the target role.

Postconditions      POST-1: Create: an OPEN request (IR-nnn) with target role, named recipient, question
                    and permitted context links exists; the recipient is notified.
                    POST-2: Reassign: the recipient changes and the former recipient immediately loses
                    access. Cancel: the request is CANCELLED with reason.




                                                                                                         160
Normal Flow             1. CS selects "Create request" and chooses the target role (PM or SA).
                        2. System lists the currently available staff of that role; CS selects the recipient,
                            writes the question and selects the context links allowed for the role (PM: farm,
                            subscription, payment or finance references; SA: account reference).
                        3. CS selects Send.
                        4. System checks assignment, ticket status, the one-OPEN-request-per-role rule and
                            the recipient's current role (GBR-79).
                        5. System saves the request and history in one transaction and notifies the recipient
                            (JOB-07).
                        6. System shows MSG-FE20-15; the ticket shows the OPEN request, which blocks
                            resolve and close.

Alternative Flows   AF1 – Change recipient: CS selects another available person of the same role and a
                    reason; System reassigns and notifies (MSG-FE20-17).
                    AF2 – Cancel request: CS enters a reason; the request becomes CANCELLED and the
                    recipient is notified (MSG-FE20-17).

Exceptions          E1: An OPEN request for the role exists: MSG-FE20-16.
                    E2: Ticket not IN_PROGRESS or WAITING_CUSTOMER: MSG-COM-05.
                    E3: Recipient no longer available: MSG-COM-08.
                    E4: Missing question or reason: MSG-COM-06.
                    E5: Not the assigned CS: MSG-FE20-09.
                    E6: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.

Priority            Medium

Frequency of Use    Weekly.

Business Rules      GBR-11, GBR-79, GBR-81.

Other Information   Screen SCR-97. Related: UC-FE20-13.

16.8 Handle Assigned Internal Support Request
UC ID and Name      UC-FE20-13 — Handle Assigned Internal Support Request

Created By          G35 Team                         Date Created             21/10/2026

Primary Actor       Platform Manager, System         Secondary Actors         None
                    Admin (named recipient)

Trigger             The PM or SA opens a request from Assigned Internal Requests or from a support
                    notification.

Description         As the named PM or SA, I want to see only the context of the request, do the work in
                    my own functions and return a sanitized result, so that CS can answer the customer.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The user is the current named recipient with the request's target role ACTIVE.
                    PRE-2: The request is OPEN.




                                                                                                          161
Postconditions      POST-1: The request is DONE with outcome (performed, unable, outside domain),
                    sanitized result and, for performed work, a reference to the committed domain change.
                    POST-2: The assigned CS is notified; the ticket status does not change.

Normal Flow             1. System shows Assigned Internal Requests (SCR-99): request code, ticket code and
                            farm, from CS, time, status and the question with allowed context links.
                        2. Recipient opens the linked function within their own permissions (for example
                            UC-FE19-06, UC-FE02-05, UC-FE03-08) and performs the work there.
                        3. Recipient selects the outcome, writes the sanitized result (visible to the CS only)
                            and, for "Performed", selects the committed domain result to reference.
                        4. Recipient selects "Send result & mark DONE".
                        5. System checks the recipient, request state and version and that a performed
                            outcome references a committed change.
                        6. System saves DONE with the result and history in one transaction, notifies the CS
                            and shows MSG-FE20-18.

Alternative Flows   AF1 – Unable or outside domain: the recipient enters the reason as result; no domain
                    reference is needed.
                    AF2 – Saving DONE fails after the domain work was committed: the recipient retries
                    with the same reference; the domain work is never repeated.

Exceptions          E1: Request reassigned or cancelled meanwhile: MSG-FE20-19; access is removed.
                    E2: Missing outcome or result: MSG-COM-06.
                    E3: Performed without a valid reference: MSG-COM-08.
                    E4: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.

Priority            Medium

Frequency of Use    Weekly.

Business Rules      GBR-11, GBR-79, GBR-81.

Other Information   Screens SCR-99, SCR-75.

UC ID and Name      UC-FE21-02 — Create Manual Finance Record

Description         As a Platform Manager, I want to record platform income or expenses other than
                    subscription receipts, so that the platform cash report is complete.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The PM is signed in with ACTIVE PM access.




                                                                                                            162
Postconditions      POST-1: A DRAFT record with code FR-nnnn exists; it is not yet included in reports.

Normal Flow            1. System shows Manual Finance Record Form (SCR-102) for a new record.
                       2. PM selects the type (income or expense) and a compatible category, enters the
                           amount (VND), occurrence date, scope (general platform or a farm, with the
                           farm selected), document number and description, and selects the source

                    E5: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.
