18. FE-23 Feed Management

18.1 Record Feed Receipt
UC ID and Name   UC-FE23-02 — Record Feed Receipt

Created By       G35 Team                        Date Created              22/10/2026

Primary Actor    Farm Owner, Farm Manager        Secondary Actors          None

Trigger          The user selects "Create feed receipt" (Tạo phiếu nhập kho) on Feed Stock or Feed
                 Receipts, "Receive" (Nhập kho) on a feed row, or "Continue" on a DRAFT receipt.

Description      As a farm owner or manager, I want to record each delivery of feed with its lots,
                 quantities and prices, so that stock, average cost and what the farm owes the supplier
                 are always correct.

Preconditions    PRE-1: The user is the Owner or a Manager of an ACTIVE farm.
                 PRE-2: At least one ACTIVE feed item and one ACTIVE supplier exist (or the supplier is
                 added inline).

Postconditions   POST-1: The receipt is POSTED with its lines, totals and initial payment; one RECEIPT
                 stock movement per line adds the quantity in kg; the weighted average cost of each
                 feed is recalculated (GBR-99).
                 POST-2: REM-10 is resolved for each feed whose stock is now above its minimum level.
                 POST-3: A DRAFT receipt changes no stock or balance.

Normal Flow         1. System shows Feed Receipt Form (SCR-113) with the next receipt code and today
                        as receipt date.
                    2. User enters the supplier, receipt date, invoice or delivery-note number, receiver,
                        storage location and note.
                    3. User selects "+ Add item" and, for each line, the feed item, lot number, expiry
                        date, quantity and purchase unit and unit price; System converts the quantity to
                        kg using the item's conversion and calculates the line amount.
                    4. System shows the totals, the stock before → after for each feed and the pre-save
                        checks (feed ACTIVE, quantity > 0, unit price ≥ 0, expiry date after the receipt
                        date, receipt date not in the future).
                    5. User attaches the invoice or delivery note and enters the transport fee, amount
                        paid, method and due date of the balance; System shows total payable and
                        outstanding (GBR-91).
                    6. User selects "Save & add to stock" (Lưu & cộng kho).
                    7. System rechecks the receipt and version and, in one transaction, posts it, records
                        the stock movements, recalculates average costs, records the initial payment
                        and saves the audit entry.
                    8. System shows MSG-FE23-01 and resolves or keeps REM-10 for the feeds
                        concerned.




                                                                                                         166
Alternative Flows   AF1 – Save draft: the receipt is saved as DRAFT (MSG-FE23-02); stock and balances do
                    not change.
                    AF2 – Record a later payment (posted receipt): the user selects "Record payment";
                    System opens Record Voucher Payment (SCR-107); the payment must not exceed the
                    outstanding amount (MSG-FE06-13, MSG-FE06-14).
                    AF3 – Void a wrong payment: the user voids it with a reason (MSG-FE06-15); it stays in
                    the history.
                    AF4 – New supplier: the user adds the supplier inline (name, phone); it is saved as a
                    trading partner with role SUPPLIER (GBR-97).
                    AF5 – Discard draft / Cancel: nothing changes (MSG-COM-14).

Exceptions          E1: Expiry date not after the receipt date: MSG-FE23-03.
                    E2: Receipt date in the future: MSG-COM-07.
                    E3: Missing supplier, feed, quantity or required field: MSG-COM-06.
                    E4: Amount paid greater than the total payable: MSG-FE06-13.
                    E5: Feed item inactive: MSG-COM-08.
                    E6: Changed in another session: MSG-COM-05.
                    E7: Save failure: MSG-COM-02; nothing is posted.

Priority            High

Frequency of Use    Several times a week.

Business Rules      GBR-91, GBR-97, GBR-98, GBR-99, GBR-103, GBR-104; BR-D34, BR-D36, BR-D39.

Other Information   Screens SCR-111, SCR-112, SCR-113, SCR-107. Related: UC-FE23-03, UC-FE23-10.

Assumptions         Feed is stocked in one storage location per farm; lots are recorded for expiry warnings
                    only.



18.2 Create or Update Ration
UC ID and Name      UC-FE23-06 — Create or Update Ration

Created By          G35 Team                        Date Created               22/10/2026

Primary Actor       Farm Owner, Farm Manager        Secondary Actors           None

Trigger             The user selects "Create ration" (Tạo khẩu phần) or "Edit" (Sửa) on a ration in Rations
                    (SCR-115).

Description         As a farm owner or manager, I want to define how much of each feed every animal of a
                    pen should get per session, and change it from a chosen date, so that daily feeding
                    follows a plan and past feeding records stay unchanged.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The user is the Owner or a Manager of an ACTIVE farm.
                    PRE-2: ACTIVE feed items and pens exist.

Postconditions      POST-1: A ration version is saved with its components, pens, sessions and effective
                    date; when it replaces a version, the previous version ends the day before (GBR-101).
                    POST-2: Planned quantities of feeding sessions from the effective date use the new
                    version; recorded feeding is not changed.




                                                                                                            167
Normal Flow            1. System shows Ration Editor (SCR-116): for a new ration the next code and version
                           v1; for an edit the current values and the next version number.
                       2. User enters the name, herd group, pens, effective-from date (tomorrow or later)
                           and basis or source.
                       3. User chooses the number of sessions per day (1, 2 or 3) and their times, and adds
                           feeds with the kg per head for each session.
                       4. System shows kg per head per day, the average cost of each feed, the cost per
                           head per day (compared with the previous version), the planned daily quantity
                           and cost for the current headcount of the pens and the days of cover of each
                           feed at current stock.
                       5. System shows the checks: feeds ACTIVE with quantity > 0; each pen has only one
                           effective ration per day; feeds whose stock does not cover one day are warned.
                       6. User selects "Save ration".
                       7. System validates the version and saves it; the previous version of the ration (or
                           another ration of the same pens from that date) ends the day before; System
                           shows MSG-FE23-09.

Alternative Flows   AF1 – Cancel a scheduled version: the user cancels a version that has not taken effect;
                    the previous version stays effective.
                    AF2 – Stop a ration: the user enters an end date (tomorrow or later); the pens have no
                    ration after it.
                    AF3 – Stock warning (step 5): the warning does not block saving.
                    AF4 – Cancel: nothing changes (MSG-COM-14).

Exceptions          E1: Effective date earlier than tomorrow: MSG-FE23-10.
                    E2: A pen has another ration on the date that cannot be ended automatically (a
                    scheduled version of another ration): MSG-FE23-11.
                    E3: No component or a quantity ≤ 0: MSG-COM-06 / MSG-COM-10.
                    E4: Changed in another session: MSG-COM-05.
                    E5: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.

Priority            Medium

Frequency of Use    Monthly per ration.

Business Rules      GBR-101, GBR-103, GBR-105; BR-D38.

Other Information   Screens SCR-115, SCR-116. Related: UC-FE23-05, UC-FE23-08.

Assumptions         Rations are expressed per head for all animals of the pen; individual feeding is not
                    planned.



18.3 Record Feeding Session
UC ID and Name      UC-FE23-08 — Record Feeding Session

Created By          G35 Team                            Date Created          22/10/2026

Primary Actor       Farm Manager                        Secondary Actors      None




                                                                                                           168
Trigger             The Manager selects "Record feeding session" (Ghi cữ cho ăn) or an empty session cell
                    on Feeding Log (SCR-117), or opens reminder REM-11.

Description         As a farm manager, I want to record the feed actually given to a pen in each session, so
                    that stock is deducted automatically and consumption and cost can be compared with
                    the ration.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The user is a Farm Manager of an ACTIVE farm.
                    PRE-2: The pen is ACTIVE.

Postconditions      POST-1: One VALID feeding record exists for the pen, date and session with its lines,
                    headcount and ration version.
                    POST-2: One FEEDING stock movement per feed deducts the actual kg at its average
                    cost; REM-11 for the session is resolved; REM-10 is created for feeds at or below their
                    minimum level.

Normal Flow            1. System shows Record Feeding Session (SCR-118) for the selected date (default
                           today) with pen and session.
                       2. Manager selects the pen and session (if not preselected); System shows the
                           feeding time, the effective ration version, the current headcount and, for each
                           feed of the ration, the kg per head, the planned quantity (kg per head ×
                           headcount) and the current stock.
                       3. Manager enters the actual kg of each feed, or selects "Fill from plan" (Điền theo
                           kế hoạch) and adjusts; optionally adds feeds outside the ration, the leftover kg,
                           the member who fed (Manager or Technician) and a note.
                       4. Manager selects "Save & deduct stock" (Lưu & trừ kho).
                       5. System validates: no VALID record exists for the pen, date and session; the date is
                           not in the future; each actual quantity ≥ 0 and not more than the stock
                           (GBR-102).
                       6. System saves the record and the stock movements in one transaction, resolves
                           REM-11 and evaluates REM-10.
                       7. System shows MSG-FE23-12 and updates the Feeding Log.

Alternative Flows   AF1 – No effective ration: MSG-FE23-20; the Manager enters actual feeds without a
                    plan.
                    AF2 – Feed out of stock: the actual quantity of that feed is 0 and the reason is written
                    in the note; the log shows "out of stock".
                    AF3 – Cancel: nothing changes (MSG-COM-14).

Exceptions          E1: A record already exists for the pen, date and session: MSG-FE23-13.
                    E2: Actual quantity above stock: MSG-FE23-14.
                    E3: Date in the future: MSG-COM-07.
                    E4: Missing pen, session or quantities: MSG-COM-06.
                    E5: Save failure: MSG-COM-02; no stock is deducted.

Priority            High

Frequency of Use    Two to three times a day per pen.

Business Rules      GBR-102, GBR-103, GBR-104, GBR-105; BR-D36, BR-D37.




                                                                                                            169
Other Information   Screens SCR-117, SCR-118, SCR-122. Medicine mixed into feed is recorded as a
                    treatment in FE-10, not here.

Assumptions         Feeding is recorded per pen, not per animal.



18.4 Correct or Void Feeding Record
UC ID and Name      UC-FE23-09 — Correct or Void Feeding Record

Created By          G35 Team                        Date Created             22/10/2026

Primary Actor       Farm Manager                    Secondary Actors         None

Trigger             The Manager selects "Correct" (Sửa) or "Void" (Hủy) on a recorded session in Feeding
                    Log (SCR-117).

Description         As a farm manager, I want to correct or void a wrong feeding record with a reason, so
                    that stock and consumption are right while the original entry stays traceable.

Preconditions       PRE-1: The user is a Farm Manager of an ACTIVE farm.
                    PRE-2: The feeding record is VALID.

Postconditions      POST-1: Correct: the new quantities are current, a Record Revision keeps the old values,
                    reason, actor and time, and one FEEDING_CORRECTION movement per changed feed
                    adjusts stock by the difference.
                    POST-2: Void: the record is VOIDED and one FEEDING_VOID movement per feed returns
                    the deducted quantity to stock; REM-11 is re-evaluated for the session.

Normal Flow            1. System shows Correct / Void Feeding Record (SCR-119) with the pen, session,
                           date, recorder and time, and the action choice "Correct quantities" / "Void
                           record".
                       2. Manager keeps "Correct quantities" and enters the new kg of the feeds to
                           correct; System shows the stock difference of each feed.
                       3. Manager enters the reason and selects "Save changes".
                       4. System validates that each new quantity ≥ 0, that no feed's stock becomes
                           negative (BR-D36) and the version.
                       5. System saves the correction, the Record Revision and the stock movements in one
                           transaction and shows MSG-FE23-15.

Alternative Flows   AF1 – Void record (step 1): the Manager enters the reason and confirms MSG-FE23-16;
                    System voids the record, returns the stock and shows MSG-COM-09.
                    AF2 – Close: nothing changes.

Exceptions          E1: Stock would become negative: MSG-FE23-14.
                    E2: Missing reason: MSG-COM-06.
                    E3: Changed in another session: MSG-COM-05.
                    E4: Save failure: MSG-COM-02.

Priority            Medium

Frequency of Use    Weekly.

Business Rules      GBR-102, GBR-103; BR-D15, BR-D36.



                                                                                                         170
 Other Information     Screens SCR-117, SCR-119.

 Assumptions           —




III. Functional Requirements
This part describes the screens of BeefCare grouped by feature (and sub-feature where a feature
serves different actors), followed by the background jobs (III.24) and external APIs (III.25). For each
screen it gives the mockup reference, the use cases it serves and the field descriptions. Mockups are
taken from the BeefCare Figma file (pages 01–10); the figure boxes mark where the final mockups
are inserted.
Table III-1 Common field and screen conventions

 Item                        Convention

 Required field              Marked with * on the label. A required field left empty shows MSG-COM-06
                             under it.

 Text                        Leading and trailing spaces are removed. Unless stated otherwise: short text max
                             100 characters, long text (descriptions, notes, reasons) max 1,000 characters. A
                             reason must contain at least 5 non-space characters.

 Date / time                 Displayed as dd/mm/yyyy and HH:mm (24 h) in the farm time zone (platform
                             screens: Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh). Date pickers do not allow future dates for events
                             that already happened (MSG-COM-07).

 Numbers and money           Vietnamese format: "." thousands separator, "," decimal separator (e.g.
                             1.200.000 đ; 3,5). Money is whole VND. Weight in kg with at most 1 decimal.

 Lists                       20 rows per page with page navigation; sortable by the column headers marked
                             in the field table; filters are kept while the user stays in the session. An empty
                             result shows MSG-COM-04 with "Clear filters". A loading failure shows
                             MSG-COM-03 with Retry.

 Status badges               Status values are shown with the codes used in this document (e.g. ACTIVE,
                             PENDING, RESOLVED) and a Vietnamese label on the interface.

 Actions by role             Buttons the user is not allowed to use are hidden; the backend still checks every
                             request (GBR-09).

 Dialog behaviour            Cancel or ✕ closes without saving; when data was entered, MSG-COM-14 asks
                             for confirmation. Save buttons are disabled while a request is in progress, and a
                             repeated click returns the first result (GBR-88). After a successful save a dialog or
                             form closes, shows its success message and returns to the screen it was opened
                             from (the source screen), which is refreshed. Full-page forms state their
                             destination in their own section.

 Home screen by role         After sign-in, after entering a farm and from the logo: Farm Owner and Farm
                             Manager → Farm Dashboard (SCR-77); Farm Technician → Technician Home
                             (SCR-104); Customer Support → Ticket Queue (SCR-96); Platform Manager →
                             Farm Service Overview (SCR-13); System Admin → User Accounts (SCR-20).



                                                                                                              171
 Links from email            The verification link opens SCR-07 (or SCR-02 when Owner registration is not
                             pending); the password-reset link opens SCR-06; the invitation link opens SCR-19
                             after sign-in or registration with the invited email.

 Read-only records           An EXITED cattle shows MSG-COM-13 and a VOIDED cattle MSG-COM-18, with
                             no recording buttons; a cattle on a PENDING death voucher shows MSG-COM-17
                             and only correction and void actions (GBR-26). A farm that is not ACTIVE shows
                             MSG-COM-12 on farm operation screens (GBR-14).

 Sign out                    Available from the user menu in the top bar of every signed-in screen
                             (UC-FE01-03); ends the session, clears the selected farm and returns to SCR-02.

 Farm scope and versions     Every list, count and detail shows only data of the current farm (BR-D01); every
                             update carries the record version (BR-D31, GBR-89).

 Phone screens               The same screens adapt to phone width: tables become cards, the sidebar
                             becomes the bottom navigation and dialogs become full-screen sheets (NFR-I02).

                                                                                                          194
6. FE-06 Herd Entry Management
Every receipt of cattle is recorded as a herd entry voucher (GBR-90): a purchase voucher of one or
more bought-in cattle with prices and payments, or a voucher of farm-born calves. A DRAFT voucher
has no effect; posting creates the cattle. Payments to the seller are recorded with the shared dialog
SCR-107.

6.1 SCR-33 Herd Entry Vouchers
                      [Figure to be inserted] Mockup – Figma 05 › "Phiếu nhập – Danh sách"

continue a voucher (UC-FE06-01) and open a voucher's detail.

                              today.

 Total weight                  kg.

13. FE-13 Herd Exit Recording
Every departure of cattle is recorded as a herd exit voucher created and confirmed by the Owner
only (GBR-94). The cattle stay ACTIVE until the voucher is confirmed. A health case closed as Died or
Missing creates a PENDING death voucher (GBR-95). Collections from the buyer are recorded with
the shared dialog SCR-107.

13.1 SCR-69 Herd Exit Vouchers
                     [Figure to be inserted] Mockup – Figma 05 › "Phiếu xuất – Danh sách"




This screen allows the Owner or a Manager to view herd exit vouchers (UC-FE13-02). The Owner
creates and confirms vouchers (UC-FE13-01) and opens pending death vouchers.
Field Description

All catalogue screens share one page with the tabs Breeds (SCR-81), Vaccines (SCR-82), Reference
medicines (SCR-83), Production purposes (SCR-84) and State & reminder parameters (SCR-85); the
page opens on Breeds from the menu Catalogues & Parameters. Each catalogue has a list, a search
box, a status filter (All, ACTIVE, INACTIVE) and an editor panel. Codes are unique per catalogue
(BR-D12) and referenced items can only be deactivated (GBR-67).

23. FE-23 Feed Management
Feed Management is a page of the Care menu with the tabs Stock, Feed Receipts, Stock Count,
Rations, Feeding Log, Catalogue and Report. Stock is kept in kg per feed item and changes only
through posted receipts, stock adjustments and feeding records (GBR-99); it is never negative
(BR-D36). The Owner and Managers manage stock, rations and the catalogue; only Managers record
feeding; Technicians view rations and the feeding log (GBR-103).




                                                                                                                262
23.1 Feed Stock

23.1.1 SCR-111 Feed Stock
                        [Figure to be inserted] Mockup – Figma 10 › "Thức ăn – Tồn kho"




This screen allows the Owner or a Manager to view the stock of each feed (UC-FE23-01) and start a
receipt (UC-FE23-02) or stock count (UC-FE23-04).

24. Background Job Inventory

24.1 Evaluate Farm Reminders (JOB-01)
Trigger: Scheduled every 15 minutes, and event-driven after each committed change of a source
record (weight, estrus, breeding, check, calving, dose, schedule, pen criteria, profile, parameters,
feed stock movement, ration, feeding record).
Purpose: Keep the farm reminders REM-01 to REM-13 consistent with the source records and the
effective parameters (Table III-3, GBR-63).
Processing steps:
1. Collect the farms and sources to evaluate: the changed sources for an event run, all ACTIVE farms
for a scheduled run.
2. Apply parameter versions whose effective time has been reached and load the effective version of
each farm.
3. For each rule, find the candidate sources and calculate the due date or window; for REM-08
compare each ACTIVE cattle with the criteria of its current pen, and its age (from the birth date) with
the life-stage ages, and check first calvings of females not yet Adult; for REM-10 compare the stock of
each ACTIVE feed item with its minimum level; for REM-11 compare the sessions of each effective
ration whose time + 2 hours has passed today with the feeding records.
4. Create or update each reminder by (rule, source, occurrence key) (BR-D22); unchanged reminders
are not rewritten.
5. Resolve, cancel or supersede reminders whose condition no longer holds.
6. Log the number of created, updated, resolved and cancelled reminders per farm.
Failure behavior:

 Scenario                           System behavior

 Database unreachable               Retries 3 times at 1-minute intervals, then logs an error. The next run
                                    recomputes everything; reminders are idempotent.

 Parameter missing for a rule       The rule is skipped for that farm; screens show MSG-FE14-01. Other rules
                                    continue.

 Error in one farm                  The farm is skipped and logged; other farms are processed.



24.2 Expire Farm Invitations (JOB-02)
Trigger: Scheduled every 15 minutes.
Purpose: Mark invitations that passed their validity as EXPIRED (GBR-16).


                                                                                                              273
Processing steps:
1. Find PENDING invitations whose expiry time is before now.
2. Set them EXPIRED and save the history.
3. Log the number of expired invitations.
Failure behavior:

 Scenario                     System behavior

 Job fails or is delayed      Acceptance and resend check the expiry time in real time, so an expired link
                              never grants access. The next run expires the remaining invitations.

 No invitation to expire      The job ends without action.



24.3 Expire Unpaid Payment Requests (JOB-03)
Trigger: Scheduled every 5 minutes.
Purpose: Mark PENDING payment requests older than 24 hours as EXPIRED and inform the Owner
(GBR-70).
Processing steps:
1. Find PENDING requests whose expiry time is before now.
2. Set them EXPIRED (never a PAID request) and create the Owner notification.
3. Log the number of expired requests.
Failure behavior:

 Scenario                      System behavior

 Job fails or is delayed       Request creation and confirmation check the expiry in real time. Evidence
                               paid inside the original window still confirms the request (UC-FE19-08 AF3).

 Concurrent confirmation       The confirmation and the expiry are serialized per farm; a PAID request is
                               never set EXPIRED.



24.4 Evaluate Subscription Coverage (JOB-04)
Trigger: Scheduled every 15 minutes.
Purpose: Update the farm service status when a purchased period starts or ends (GBR-13).
Processing steps:
1. Find farms with a purchased period that started or ended since the previous run.
2. Recalculate the service status: ACTIVE when a period covers now, EXPIRED when coverage has
ended, PENDING_PAYMENT when no period was ever accepted; SUSPENDED is never changed by the
job.
3. Save the status change and audit entry; create an "expired" notification for the Owner.
Failure behavior:



                                                                                                            274
 Scenario                       System behavior

 Job fails or is delayed        Every request derives the service status from the periods in real time, so
                                access is always correct; only notifications are delayed.

 Database unreachable           Retries at the next run.



24.5 Generate Subscription Notices (JOB-05)
Trigger: Scheduled daily at 07:00 (Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh).
Purpose: Inform Owners about renewal and expiry of each farm's coverage.
Processing steps:
1. For each farm with a confirmed end date, determine whether today is the renewal opening day
(end − 90 days), 30, 7 or 1 day before the end, or the day coverage expired.
2. Create one in-app notification and one email per notice type, farm, period and Owner
(deduplicated).
3. Log the number of notices.
Failure behavior:

 Scenario                       System behavior

 Job fails                      The next run creates missed notices for the current day only; no duplicates
                                are created.

 Owner changed meanwhile        Notices go to the current Owner of the farm.



24.6 Process Received Bank Evidence (JOB-06)
Trigger: Queue; runs continuously for evidence stored by API-02 or API-03.
Purpose: Run the shared payment confirmation for each authenticated evidence exactly once
(GBR-72, GBR-73).
Processing steps:
1. Take the next evidence with pending processing.
2. Lock processing per farm and evaluate eligibility (direction, receiving account, payment reference,
amount, bank-paid time window, allocation, manual income conflict).
3. Eligible: commit allocation, PAID, purchased period, service status, receipt and audit in one
transaction; create the Owner notification.
4. Not eligible: mark the evidence NEEDS_REVIEW with the reason and create a PM reconciliation
notification.
5. Mark the processing done.
Failure behavior:

 Scenario                       System behavior




                                                                                                             275
 Transient error                Retries after 1, 5 and 15 minutes, then hourly for 24 hours; then a PM
                                notification is created. Evidence is never lost.

 Repeated processing            Returns the committed result; no second period, receipt or notification
                                (GBR-88).



24.7 Dispatch Notifications and Emails (JOB-07)
Trigger: Scheduled every minute.
Purpose: Deliver notifications and emails for committed business events (GBR-64).
Processing steps:
1. Read undelivered events from the outbox written with each business change.
2. Resolve the current recipients and recheck their access.
3. Create the in-app notifications and send the emails (API-04); deduplicate by recipient and event.
4. Create a reminder notice to the recipient of an internal request that has been OPEN for 4 hours.
5. Mark the events delivered.
Failure behavior:

 Scenario                       System behavior

 Email provider error           Retries with increasing delay for up to 24 hours; the in-app notification is still
                                created; the business change is never rolled back.

 Recipient lost access          The notification is not created; the event is closed.



24.8 Purge Expired Tokens and Snapshots (JOB-08)
Trigger: Scheduled daily at 02:00.
Purpose: Remove technical data that is no longer needed.
Processing steps:
1. Delete verification, reset and invitation link token hashes that expired more than 7 days ago
(invitation records and statuses are kept).
2. Mark cash report snapshots older than 30 days EXPIRED and delete their rows.
3. Process in batches of up to 5,000 records and log the counts.
Failure behavior:

 Scenario                       System behavior

 Timeout during a batch         The batch is rolled back; the job continues at the next run. No business
                                record is affected.




25. External API Inventory


                                                                                                              276
25.1 GET: VietQR image (API-01)
Item               Value

URL                GET
                   https://qr.sepay.vn/img?acc={account_number}&bank={bank_code}&amount={amou
                   nt}&des={payment_reference}

Purpose            Render the VietQR image of a payment request on Transfer Instructions (SCR-88).

Caller             The Owner's browser loads the image URL built by the BeefCare backend from the
                   immutable request snapshot (BeefCare → SePay QR service).

Auth               None (public image service); the URL contains no secret. Displaying the QR confirms
                   nothing.



25.2 POST: SePay transaction webhook (API-02)
Item                Value

URL                 POST /api/v1/payments/sepay/webhook (one endpoint per environment: Sandbox,
                    Live)

Purpose             Receive each bank transaction on the receiving account, store it as evidence and start
                    payment confirmation (UC-FE19-08).

Caller              SePay.

Auth                HMAC-SHA256: headers X-SePay-Signature (sha256=hex) and X-SePay-Timestamp;
                    signature over "{timestamp}.{raw body}" with the environment secret; 300-second
                    tolerance.

Data                id (deduplication key), gateway, transactionDate (bank time, Vietnam time),
                    accountNumber, code, content, transferType (in/out), transferAmount,
                    referenceCode, description. Response HTTP 200 with body {"success": true} within
                    30 seconds after the evidence is stored.



25.3 GET: SePay transaction lookup (API-03)
Item                Value

URL                 GET https://my.sepay.vn/userapi/transactions/list (filters: account number,
                    transaction date range, limit) and GET
                    https://my.sepay.vn/userapi/transactions/details/{id}

Purpose             Let the PM find a transaction that was not delivered by webhook and store it as
                    evidence for reconciliation (UC-FE19-06).

Caller              BeefCare backend, on request from the PM workbench (SCR-91).

Auth                Bearer API token per environment, stored server-side only.




                                                                                                      277
25.4 POST: Transactional email (API-04)
Item                  Value

URL                   Provider send-mail API or SMTP submission (provider to be selected at deployment).

Purpose               Send invitation, verification, password-reset, password-changed, subscription and
                      support emails (JOB-07).

Caller                BeefCare backend.

Auth                  Provider API key or SMTP credentials stored server-side; TLS.



25.5 OAuth 2.0 / OpenID Connect: Google sign-in (API-05)
Item                  Value

URL                   GET https://accounts.google.com/o/oauth2/v2/auth (scope openid email profile,
                      state, nonce, PKCE); POST https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token (code exchange);
                      callback GET /api/v1/auth/google/callback

Purpose               Authenticate a person with Google for registration, sign-in and invitation acceptance
                      (UC-FE01-01, UC-FE01-02, UC-FE03-04).

Caller                The user's browser (redirects) and the BeefCare backend (code exchange).

Auth                  OAuth client ID and secret stored server-side; the ID token signature and claims (iss,
                      aud, exp, nonce, email_verified) are validated.




IV. Non-Functional Requirements
The requirements below are acceptance targets for the capstone deployment. They apply to the
responsive BeefCare web application, its backend and its integrations.


1. External Interfaces

1.1 User Interfaces
ID            Requirement

NFR-I01       BeefCare is one responsive web application for desktop (≥ 1280 px), tablet (≥ 768 px) and
              phone (≥ 360 px) browsers; all authorized workflows are available without a native
              application.

NFR-I02       Forms, dialogs and navigation adapt to the device: on phones tables become cards, the
              sidebar becomes a bottom navigation and dialogs open full screen; required actions and
              validation messages are never hidden. Wide tables may scroll inside their own container.

NFR-I03       Cattle QR scanning uses the device camera through the browser (HTTPS); when the camera is
              unavailable or the tag unreadable, the user can enter the display code, ear tag or tag code
              (SCR-32).



                                                                                                          278
NFR-I04         Cattle QR tags and payment VietQR codes are named and displayed differently; a payment QR
                never opens a cattle and a cattle QR never starts a payment (OR-03).

NFR-I05         The farm interface is in Vietnamese with consistent terminology for roles, statuses and events
                across devices (OR-01).



1.2 Hardware Interfaces
ID              Requirement

NFR-I06         Only standard devices are needed: a phone with a camera and a modern browser for field
                use, a desktop or laptop for management, and any printer able to print the QR ear tag layout
                (OR-02). No IoT sensor, RFID reader or scale integration is required.



1.3 Software Interfaces
ID              Requirement

NFR-I07         Google Identity (API-05): OAuth 2.0 / OpenID Connect sign-in; BeefCare validates the ID token
                and the verified email and never receives Google passwords.

NFR-I08         SePay (API-01 to API-03): bank-transaction webhooks for the subscription receiving account
                only, transaction lookup and VietQR images. Sandbox and Live use separate endpoints,
                accounts, tokens and secrets with the same business rules (OR-09).

NFR-I09         Email Delivery Service (API-04): transactional emails sent after the business change is
                committed, with retry; delivery never changes business state.

NFR-I10         Supported browsers: the two latest major versions of Chrome, Edge, Firefox and Safari;
                Chrome on Android 10+ and Safari on iOS 15+.

NFR-I11         Technology platform of the capstone implementation: Java 21 with Spring Boot 3 (backend),
                Next.js with TypeScript (frontend), PostgreSQL (database), Docker containers.



1.4 Communications Interfaces
ID              Requirement

NFR-I12         All browser and integration traffic uses HTTPS (TLS 1.2 or higher) with a valid certificate; the
                SePay Live webhook endpoint is public HTTPS.

NFR-I13         Dates exchanged with SePay are interpreted in Vietnam time (Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh); the
                backend stores timestamps with time zone.




2. Quality Attributes

2.1 Usability



                                                                                                              279
ID            Requirement

NFR-U01       A trained Technician opens the correct cattle profile by scanning its tag in at most 2 taps from
              the home screen, without searching lists.

NFR-U02       A Farm Manager records a routine event (weight, estrus, vaccination of one animal) from the
              open profile in under 60 seconds; field forms show only the fields needed and mark required
              fields.

NFR-U03       Touch targets on phone screens are at least 44 × 44 px; all actions are also usable with a
              keyboard on desktop.

NFR-U04       Measured and estimated weights, UNKNOWN and negative history, TENTATIVE and
              CONFIRMED dates, and scheduled and performed doses are always labelled distinctly (OR-10).

NFR-U05       Error messages say what to correct, do not expose internal or other-tenant information, and a
              failed or pending save is never shown as successful.

NFR-U06       Buttons for actions the role cannot perform are hidden; the current farm and role are always
              visible in the top bar; switching farm clears the previous farm's data and filters.

NFR-U07       Subscription status, payment status and suspension are shown separately; PAID never implies
              that a SUSPENDED farm is usable.

NFR-U08       A new Farm Owner completes registration, farm registration and the payment request in at
              most 4 screens (SCR-03/07, SCR-11, SCR-87, SCR-88).

2.3 Security
Requirement                   Description

NFR-S01 Authentication        Every page and action requires sign-in except the public landing page, sign-in,
required                      registration, email verification, password reset and the SePay webhook (which
                              uses its own authentication).

NFR-S02 Authorization on      The backend checks account status, tenant, membership, role, platform role,
every request                 assignment and farm service status on every protected request; hidden
                              buttons are never the only protection (GBR-09).

NFR-S03 Tenant isolation      No query, list, count, export, cache, notification or link returns data of a farm
                              the user cannot access; cross-tenant access is treated as a critical defect.

NFR-S04 Password              Passwords are stored only as salted Argon2id or bcrypt hashes and are never
protection                    logged or returned; 8–128 characters; lockout of password sign-in for 15
                              minutes after 5 failures (GBR-02).

NFR-S05 Links and tokens      Verification, reset and invitation tokens are random (≥ 128 bits), single-use,
                              time-limited and stored only as hashes (GBR-03).

NFR-S06 Session security      Sessions use secure, HTTP-only cookies; sessions end on sign-out, password
                              change (other sessions), account suspension and inactivity (8 hours, or 30 days
                              with Remember me).

NFR-S07 Secrets               Provider tokens, webhook secrets and OAuth secrets are kept server-side in
                              environment configuration, never in the client bundle or source control;
                              Sandbox and Live secrets are separate.

NFR-S08 Webhook               SePay webhooks are accepted only with a valid HMAC-SHA256 signature
authenticity                  within 300 seconds; invalid calls create no evidence (API-02).

NFR-S09 QR is not             A scanned code is resolved only inside a farm the user may access (GBR-28).
authorization

NFR-S10 Least privilege for   CS, PM and SA access only the data of their use cases; no platform role sees
platform staff                cattle, care or reproduction records; internal requests expose only allowlisted
                              context (GBR-11, GBR-81).

NFR-S11 Audit                 Important changes are written to the audit log in the same transaction with
                              actor, role, time and allowlisted before/after values; audit entries cannot be
                              modified (GBR-86).

NFR-S12 Sensitive logging     Logs and error messages never contain passwords, tokens, secrets, full bank
                              evidence payloads or ticket bodies.

NFR-S13 Access withdrawal     Membership deactivation, role change, account suspension and platform
                              access revocation take effect at the next request of existing sessions and old
                              notification links.



                                                                                                               281
2.4 Reliability and Data Integrity
 Requirement                   Description

 NFR-R01 Atomic changes        Each command saves the record, its history, its audit and its outbox event in
                               one transaction; a failure leaves no partial change (GBR-88).

 NFR-R02 Idempotency           Repeated submissions, webhook retries and job re-runs return the first result
                               without duplicates (GBR-88, BR-D23, BR-D24).

 NFR-R03 Concurrency           Updates use optimistic versions (GBR-89); changes to the same female's
 control                       pregnancy and to the same farm's payments are serialized (GBR-52, GBR-72).

 NFR-R04 Availability          Target availability 99 % per month during the capstone pilot, excluding
                               announced maintenance.

 NFR-R05 Backup                The database is backed up daily with 14 days of retention; a restore is tested
                               before the pilot.

 NFR-R06 Time handling         All timestamps are stored with time zone; farm dates use the farm time zone
                               and platform finance uses Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh (GBR-66).




V. Requirement Appendix

1. Business Rules
The business rules below are referenced by the business flows (I.2), use case specifications (II) and
functional requirements (III). Data integrity rules that follow directly from the conceptual model are
listed separately in I.3.3 (BR-Dxx).

            BeefCare account, identified by a                          accounts and keeps      registration,
            verified email (case-insensitive). A                       one history per         sign-in and
            Google identity and a BeefCare                             person across farms.    profile update
            password with the same verified

GBR-38   A growth assessment contains a       UC-FE08-01          Comparable and      System, on save
         weight, a BCS or both. A weight is a                     honest growth data.
         positive number stored in kg with a

         other estimates are always
         ESTIMATED). BCS uses the farm BCS
         scale (default 1.0–5.0 in steps of
         0.5). The measurement time cannot
         be in the future or before the herd
         entry.

         latest valid ESTIMATED weight when                       estimates.

         (average daily gain) for a window =
         (last valid weight − first valid weight
         in the window) ÷ days between
         them, shown only when the two
         weights are at least 14 days apart.

GBR-54   Reproductive status applies to an         FE-11, FE-12   Users see only the   System, on
         ACTIVE female whose life stage is                        actions that make    display and
         YOUNG_STOCK or ADULT and whose                           sense now.           save
         purpose type is BREEDING or

         AWAITING_BREEDING (Chờ phối: no
         current estrus, breeding, pregnancy or
         post-calving period, including after a
         NOT_PREGNANT check or a
         pregnancy loss), IN_ESTRUS (estrus
         within the last 24 hours, no later
         breeding), BRED (breeding without a
         later estrus or check result; also after
         an INCONCLUSIVE check), PREGNANT
         (OPEN episode; labelled "Sắp đẻ"
         within the calving preparation lead
         time before the expected date),
         POST_CALVING (calved within the
         post-calving interval, no later estrus or
         breeding). The recording actions
         offered follow the status:
         AWAITING_BREEDING → Record
         estrus, Record breeding; IN_ESTRUS
         → Record estrus, Record breeding;
         BRED → Record estrus, Record
         pregnancy check, Record calving;
         PREGNANT → Record pregnancy
         check, Record pregnancy update,
         Record calving; POST_CALVING →
         Record estrus, Record breeding. For
         any ACTIVE female at stage
         YOUNG_STOCK or ADULT, "More
         actions" also offers Record pregnancy
         check and Record calving (untracked
         pregnancy), so that a real event can
         always be recorded.

GBR-91   Line amount = unit price × weight        UC-FE06-01,   The farm knows        System, on save

         accounting documents, and are not

GBR-98    A feed item has a code (TA-nn)      UC-FE23-10        Stable units keep     System, on save
          unique in the farm, a group, the                      stock and cost
          stock unit kg, an optional purchase                   correct.
          unit with its kg conversion and a
          minimum stock level. Code and
          stock unit cannot change after the
          first stock movement; an item with
          movements is made inactive, never
          deleted; inactive items cannot be
          used in new receipts, rations or
          feeding records. Editing an item
          never changes past documents.

GBR-99    Feed stock changes only through         UC-FE23-01,   Stock is always       System, on post
          stock movements created by              UC-FE23-02,   explainable by        and void
          posting a receipt, voiding a receipt,   UC-FE23-03    documents.
          posting an adjustment and saving,
          correcting or voiding a feeding
          record; stock = sum of movements
          and never becomes negative
          (BR-D36). A DRAFT receipt has no
          effect. Posting a receipt adds each
          line in kg and recalculates the
          weighted average cost = (stock
          value + received value) ÷ (stock +
          received kg). A POSTED receipt is
          voided, with a reason, only while
          the stock of every feed covers its
          received quantity; otherwise a
          stock adjustment is used.

GBR-100   A stock adjustment compares          UC-FE23-04       Physical counts are   System, on post
          counted stock with system stock at                    reconciled without
          the count date; every non-zero                        rewriting history.
          difference needs a reason and
          creates one movement valued at
          the weighted average cost. Posted
          adjustments are immutable and
          never change receipts or feeding
          records; a mistake is corrected with
          a new adjustment.

GBR-101   A ration defines, for its pens, the kg UC-FE23-06    The plan can evolve   System, on save
          per head of each feed for each                       while the record of
          daily session (1–3 sessions).                        what was planned
          Changing a ration saves a new                        stays true.
          version effective from tomorrow or
          later; the previous version ends the
          day before. At most one ration
          version is effective per pen and
          date (BR-D38). A scheduled version
          can be cancelled and an effective
          ration stopped from a future date.
          Recorded feeding is never changed
          by a new version.

GBR-102   A feeding record is kept per pen,     UC-FE23-08,    Daily feeding is      System, on save
          date and session (BR-D37), not in     UC-FE23-09     recorded once and
          the future. Planned kg per feed =                    reflected in stock.
          kg per head of the effective ration
          × headcount of the pen at
          recording time. Actual kg per feed
          ≥ 0 and not above its stock. Saving
          deducts the actual kg from stock at
          the weighted average cost. Only a
          Manager records, corrects or voids
          feeding; a correction or void needs
          a reason, keeps the original values
          (BR-D15) and adjusts stock by the
          difference. The person who fed
          may be a Technician.

GBR-103   Feed permissions: the Owner and       FE-23          Feeding follows the   System, on

          for an ACTIVE feed item whose                        missed feedings are

          Managers and Technicians. Both

GBR-105   Feed cost of a feeding record = Σ     UC-FE23-01,   Reproducible feed   System, on
          actual kg × weighted average cost     UC-FE23-05,   consumption and     calculation
          at recording time. Days of cover =    UC-FE23-11    cost figures.
          stock ÷ average daily use of the last
          7 days. Stock value = Σ stock ×
          average cost. Average head of a
          period = mean daily headcount
          from pen assignments. Variance
          versus ration = (actual − planned) ÷
          planned over recorded sessions.
          Losses from stock adjustments are
          reported separately and not
          allocated to herd groups. Rations
          show estimated cost from current
          average costs.



1.12 Herd Performance
GBR-ID    Rule Statement                       Applies To     Rationale           Enforced By




                                                                                                314
GBR-106   Reproduction indicators use VALID      UC-FE15-01,   Breeding herds are System, on
          events only and are shown with the     UC-FE15-02,   judged by calving    calculation
          number of females or events (n); a     UC-FE11-01,   interval, conception
          value based on fewer than 3 is         UC-FE12-01    and losses; the
          shown as "Insufficient data".                        figures come from
          Definitions: (1) calving interval =                  the events already
          days between two consecutive                         recorded.
          calvings of the same female; (2)
          days open = days from a calving to
          the breeding that led to the next
          confirmed pregnancy (the breeding
          linked to the PREGNANT check); (3)
          services per conception = number
          of breedings in a cycle (since the
          last calving, or since the first
          breeding of a heifer) up to and
          including the conceiving breeding;
          (4) first-service conception rate =
          first services of a cycle with a
          PREGNANT result ÷ first services
          with a PREGNANT or
          NOT_PREGNANT result; (5) overall
          conception rate = breedings with a
          PREGNANT result ÷ breedings with a
          PREGNANT or NOT_PREGNANT
          result (INCONCLUSIVE checks are
          excluded); (6) pregnancy loss rate =
          episodes closed LOST ÷ episodes
          closed CALVED or LOST; (7) age at
          first calving = age in months at the
          first calving; (8) overdue for
          conception = applicable females not
          PREGNANT more than (post-calving
          interval + 3 × estrus cycle length)
          days after their last calving (farm
          parameters, default 108 days). Herd
          values are averages or ratios over
          the events that end in the report
          period.




                                                                                                  315
 GBR-107    Fattening performance covers cattle UC-FE15-02,          Feeding decisions     System, on
            with purpose type FATTENING             UC-FE08-02,      need gain, feed use   calculation
            during the period. The weight of a      FE-14            and feed cost per
            cattle at a date is interpolated                         kg of gain,
            linearly between the nearest VALID                       computed from
            weights before and after it when                         data already
            both are within 60 days; otherwise                       recorded.
            the nearest VALID weight within 7
            days is used; otherwise the weight
            is unknown. Gain = weight at the
            end of the period (or exit) − weight
            at the start (or entry); ADG = gain ÷
            days. The feed of a pen on a day
            (VALID feeding records) is shared
            equally among the cattle in the pen
            that day (pen assignments), and its
            cost likewise (GBR-105). Feed per kg
            gain = attributed feed (kg as fed) ÷
            gain, and feed cost per kg gain =
            attributed feed cost ÷ gain, over the
            cattle with a known start and end
            weight; the others are reported as
            "Insufficient weights (n head)".
            Projected finishing date = latest
            valid weight date + (target finishing
            weight − latest weight) ÷ ADG of the
            last 60 days; it is not shown without
            a target, without two valid weights
            at least 14 days apart, or when that
            ADG is not positive. A cattle at or
            above the target shows "Target
            reached" and raises REM-13. No
            purchase price, sale price or profit is
            calculated.




2. System Messages
Message content is given in English; the interface shows the Vietnamese translation with the same
meaning (OR-01). Values in braces are filled at run time; text in square brackets is a button or link.
Messages report committed results only and never replace backend checks.

                                                                                                       332
3. Other Requirements
ID         Other Requirement

OR-01      Language: the farm interface uses Vietnamese terminology suitable for the target farms; this
           specification uses English names with the Vietnamese interface titles in brackets. Translated
           labels and messages keep the same roles, conditions and meaning.

OR-02      QR ear tag layout: the printed tag shows the display code (BC-nnnnnn) above the QR code;
           the QR resolves the permanent Internal Cattle ID and stays valid when the secondary ear tag
           changes.

OR-03      QR distinction: cattle QR tags and payment VietQR codes are named and displayed distinctly;
           a payment QR shows the receiving account, amount and payment reference; a cattle QR
           opens an authorized cattle profile.

OR-04      Data retention: cattle, herd entry and exit, care, reproduction, membership, payment and
           evidence records needed for traceability remain available to authorized users after cattle exit,
           membership deactivation or service expiry. No automatic deletion period is defined in this
           release.

OR-05      Responsive web: all roles use the same responsive web application on desktop, tablet and
           phone; no separate native mobile application is built.

OR-06      Decision support only: BeefCare shows conditions, reminders, warnings and summaries; it
           never decides breeding, sale, culling, treatment or recovery and never records an event by
           itself. A person with the right role records or confirms every event.

OR-07      External professional input: veterinarians, AI technicians and buyers do not have accounts;
           their advice, results and names are recorded as information by the Farm Manager or Owner.
           BeefCare provides no diagnosis or prescription.

OR-08      Scope: one Owner may own several independent farms; organisation-level consolidation of
           farms is out of scope. Feed management (FE-23: feed stock, feed receipts, rations and feeding
           log) and the values, payments and collections of herd entry and exit vouchers are in scope as
           farm bookkeeping; general inventory of other goods (medicines, equipment), purchase
           orders, invoices and tax documents, farm accounting, payroll, IoT devices and native mobile
           applications are out of scope. Platform support, payment reconciliation, basic platform cash
           records and account administration are in scope.

OR-09      SePay environments: Sandbox uses simulated transactions and Live uses real bank transfers.
           Endpoints, receiving accounts, account namespaces, API tokens and webhook secrets are
           separate per environment; the same business rules apply in both.

OR-10      Record presentation: screens and reports distinguish occurrence time from entry time,
           UNKNOWN history from a negative finding, estimates from measurements, scheduled
           activities from performed ones, and payment status from service status and suspension.

OR-11      Identity and email: people sign in with Google or with a verified email and a BeefCare
           password (API-05, GBR-01); Google manages the security and recovery of Google accounts,
           BeefCare manages BeefCare passwords. A phone number is contact information only. Emails
           (invitations, links, notices) are sent through the Email Delivery Service (API-04), provider to be
           selected at deployment.




                                                                                                         333
OR-12   Subscription billing: one functional plan per farm with 1-, 2- and 3-calendar-year prices; an
        immutable 24-hour payment request per purchase; renewal requests open in the last 90 days
        of confirmed coverage; service dates are estimates until the payment is accepted (Policy A,
        GBR-69 to GBR-75).

OR-13   Support and finance traceability: ticket conversations, status and resolution history, and
        finance posting and voiding history are kept for authorized review. Legal accounting
        compliance is not claimed.

OR-14   Public content: the landing page shows the official project name BeefCare, the introduction,
        implemented features, the current 1/2/3-year prices, the FAQ and the public contact email
        tuanpqhe181869@fpt.edu.vn and phone 0386671863, without a postal address. No
        unpublished price, private record or unsupported feature is shown.




                                                                                                     334
